Community counts only posts and replies that are not anonymous; its invite badges count people who joined with your codes and used the app on three days (`invites_joined` over `active_at`, the moment the referral pays, 1, 5, 20, 50) and its Connected badges the team's connections that came true and paid your side by the pay rule (`connections_made`, distinct pay keys: never one you made, never a manager's side of a job; 1, 5; 2026-11-10), counted for admins too though admins earn no xp from them. A badge pays nothing. `BadgeArt` draws the medal (shape from the family, finish from the tier in the `--badge-*` tokens, a padlock when locked, a one-time GSAP shine for a badge just earned, `gsap.set` under reduced motion); the gallery sits under Profile as a lazy chunk, earned first then the exact next step with a bar where a number can be counted, and a sheet per badge; a family past ten medals reads in two parts through `TileGroups` (Community's referral, invite and Connected badges under "People you brought in", 2026-11-10), and a name of four words or more keeps its last three together ("Twenty / through the door"). Honors keep their keys and wear a seal with a tier. A new badge is one line in `BADGES` with a stat that already has a column in `getStats`; a new stat needs both.
- The groups are in one fixed order throughout the app (`MODULE_GROUP_ORDER` and `inGroupOrder` in shared, returned by `useGroupOrder` in lib/modules.ts: Contribution, Reflection, Connection, Creation, Essentials, Operations; on Apps, Profile Features, the sidebar, the phone menu, Home's Go to and the public Features page). Inside a group the apps come in the person's own order from Profile, Features (`orderInGroup`, see Apps), then in the default order, which is `MODULES`' own (the founder, 2026-11-07, in place of moving apps by what fits and what gets opened, so an app stays where it was last seen; his order for Connection, Essentials and Operations, Reflection by importance and Creation by value; `useModules` only puts what is on first, and Games sits last in Connection).
- Agency (`agency`, group Contribution, accent servant_leader, off on a new account, paths `/agency`, `/agency/:clientId`, `/agency/invoices/:id`, `/agency/contracts/:id`; public `/a/:token`, `/i/:token`, `/k/:token`; `routes/agency.ts`, `routes/agencyDocs.ts`, `routes/kpis.ts`, `agencyStats.ts`, migration 0054): for a content agency or a freelance creator, not agencies in general. The screen (2026-11-03) is a `TabRow` of Overview, Pipeline, Clients, Paperwork and, for the owner only, Team (Invoices, Agreements and Documents as segments, `agseg`; an old `?tab=invoices` or `agreements` link is rewritten there), plus a 44px gear for Agency settings (`?tab=settings`, where the invoice, agreement and house templates now live as rows), with one primary button in the header for the tab; Overview is four tiles with the rest under "Show all numbers" and the first five of Coming up; a client page (`AgencyClient`) is one fact line, a More menu (edit, billing, open link, log time, invoice this month), the stage card (`AgencyLifecycle` compact: the bar, the next move, "Stage details" folded), three tiles and the segments Work, Money (the retainer only from the pilot on), Paper and Link (`cseg`); the client link reads as one "For you to do" list and the client pages use `SiteLayout minimal`. Owner only: every query filters by the session user; the day comes from the device (`?day=`, `dayFromDevice` in db.ts), never the server's UTC date. A client (`agency_clients`: status lead, active, paused, ended; health; channels; retainer cents; deliverables and hours a month; renewal day; billing details) has deliverables (`agency_items`, `DELIVERABLE_STAGES` idea, script, making, review, approved, published; kind, channel, due and publish days, https link, fee), a notes thread per deliverable (`agency_notes`: `me`, `client` from the link, `brand` from Army; a person's note is private unless shared), time (`agency_time`) and a dashboard that opens on a tap. Every pipeline (clients, deliverables, invoices, agreements) switches between a list and a kanban board (`ViewToggle`, `useView`, `KanbanBoard`; the choice lives in the URL, and with none a phone starts on the list; a board column is a page of its own with "Show more", a fixed 260px lane (280 from `md`) in a `.scroll-row` with a fade at the edge that has more, `GET /agency/board`, `/agency/invoices/board`, `/agency/contracts/board`; cards are short and move by drag or by the Move button, 44px, which opens a sheet of the columns the card may go to) and every status is a `StatusTag` (`TagTone`, the `*_TONE` maps in shared; the tag vocabulary is under Screens). Brand-side work stays in Army: a client links to an Army campaign the person is an accepted creator on, `POST /agency/clients/:id/army-import` brings its deliverables in, `POST /agency/items/:id/army/send` goes through Army's `uploadVersion` (one action, two callers) and `…/army/sync` brings the brand's answer and feedback back; all of it is refused while Army is off for them. The client link (`portal_token` 16 characters, `portal_json`: `PORTAL_PARTS` waiting, coming up, published, progress, notes, then channels, hours, invoices, agreements, and `PORTAL_ACTIONS` approve or send back, write notes; `parsePortalShow`) is switched on, off or renewed from the client page, and what is off is not sent: `GET /public/agency/:token` builds only the parts that are on. A renewed or closed link is kept only as a SHA-256 hash (`retired_portal_links`, migration 0069, a year, gone with the client and the account) and answers 410 `{error: 'closed', agencyName, agencyLogo, agencyEmail}` (`closedLinkAnswer`), which `LinkTrouble` shows as "This link was closed" with who to ask, never a bare 404. The client's page opens "Hello, <client>" under the business's mark, then the stage bar and one "For you to do" list. A brand is entered once: `contractVarsFor` fills every agreement and document from the profile and the client's record, `emailVarsFor` fills every note, a client's copy leaves out any line whose words are missing and the owner gets one `contractGapNote`, and each value no record holds is asked once (`AddOnce` in `DocActions.tsx`) and saved on the client or the profile. The Email a copy sheet (`EmailNote` in `DocActions.tsx`) opens with the note `suggestEmailTemplate` picks for the kind of paper and the client's stage. `sendAgencyDueNotes` in cron.ts sends one inbox note a day (`agency-due-<user>-<day>`, kind `agency`, the Agency inbox tab) at 09:00 in the owner's zone for pieces due that day, only while Agency is on. Across the app (the emergence wires, each only while Agency is on, owner only): Daily lists pieces and invoices due (`agency.due`, `invoicesDue`), Finance counts sent invoices as owed to you, search finds clients and invoices, the brief has `agency-change`, `agency-due` and `agency-month`, the private calendar link carries due and go-live days with the title and client and never a fee or a note, and the assistant has the Agency tools (list and read clients and invoices, add and move a piece, add a private note, mark an invoice sent or paid). Never in it, whatever is on: private notes, per-piece fees, the retainer amount, time line by line, other clients, what the brand wrote in Army (`PORTAL_NEVER`). Approving, sending back and commenting are limited per address (`portal`). Billing reuses `invoices` and `createInvoice`/`setInvoiceStatus` in finance.ts (Agency does not need Finance on): the invoice designer (`InvoiceDoc`: title, both parties, bank, look, discount, GST with a CGST and SGST split, TDS added or shown, footnote and footer) follows the founder's own invoice: a title from `INVOICE_TITLES` (Advance Invoice among them, or typed), the sender as "Prop. <name> for <business>" (`invoiceFromProfile`, which fills any empty part from the agreement paper's brand, address, first email and phone, so the business is typed once; the email sign-off reads the same), the quantity column named from `INVOICE_QTY_LABELS` (Qty/Hr rate) and written "05" with `qtyPad` (`invoiceQty`), a bank block with UPI, TDS adjusted shown even at ₹0.00 while the client pays the TDS, the footnote from `invoiceFootnote`, the two centred footer lines from `invoiceFooterFor` ("Prop. <business> - <address>", then phones, email and the profile's `website`; an empty part is left out), and the look `founder`, "Founder classic" in `INVOICE_DESIGNS` (grey bars, a teal bank header, green totals, a blue words bar; keys only, the paper colours in app.css); "From a package" in a line's menu adds `packageInvoiceLine` ("05 Sample Videos Package", the count as the quantity, the fee shared to the cent); nothing on the printed invoice is an editing hint (no placeholder, empty tax row or date picker). On the paper a total's label and value never wrap (the sums column grows only when a label needs room), the total steps down to 20px for lakh and 18px for crore (`totalSize`), the columns are 38/22/20/20% with unit cost, quantity and amount right-aligned, the founder look sets Billed To tight (`Party` `tight`), and a phone shows the Indian way ("+91 98765 43210") through `phoneDisplay` in shared, for display only, never stored. The designer and calculator are one rule, `invoiceTotals`, in whole cents and basis points, and `amountInWords` (lakh and crore for rupees); `total_cents` is always the calculator's answer. The person's own details (sender, tax numbers, bank, look, prefix, pay days, signer, courts) are the business profile in `agency_settings.profile_json`, saved once, owner only, in the export; they are never written in code, and a sample invoice's real numbers must never be seeded anywhere. An invoice or an agreement can be changed after it is sent (the client then sees it marked Revised); a paid invoice must be marked sent again first so `finance_transactions` stays true; a signed agreement is never edited, only revised into a new draft. A void can be undone (migration 0075): voiding keeps the status it came from (`voided_from`; an agreement also keeps its link in `voided_token`), and Restore in the page's More menu and the row menu (`POST /agency/invoices/:id/restore`, `/agency/contracts/:id/restore`, limit `agency`, owner only, guarded `WHERE status = 'void'` so two taps restore once) brings it back to that status, a void with no record as a draft, with the same number and the same link; a paid invoice books its income again once (`INSERT OR IGNORE`, on its paid day, `restoreInvoice` in finance.ts); a line in the client's log, no inbox note. Other documents have no Void, so nothing to restore. A copy of an invoice takes the next number in its series (`nextNumberAfter`), the device's day, the same gap to the due date, and moves a `YYYY-MM` in its lines on by the months between. What the client sees on an invoice is a set of switches on the invoice (`INVOICE_SHOW_PARTS`: bank, tax numbers, due date, total in words, notes, comments, download) and `invoiceForClient` removes what is off on the server before anything is sent. Agreements are built from sections (`CONTRACT_TEMPLATES` trial and pilot, monthly retainer, one-off project; text, packages, brief and signatures; `fillContractText`), filled from the profile and the client, edited in place; **the Trial and pilot template is the founder's Trial Engagement Agreement word for word** (title and subtitle in capitals, the opening paragraph as section `intro`, 1 PARTIES to 10 ACCEPTANCE AND SIGNATURES with the PDF's numbering, subheads, bullet lists, bold arrow chains, curly quotes and casing; the ids parties, purpose, brief, ownership, after, packages, payment, how, law and sign stay because `TRIAL_RULES` and `trialPart` read them): only a person's or company's details are variables, filled from the business profile (`brandName`, `operatingUnder`, `businessName`, `businessAddress`, two `emails`, two `phones`, `signerTitle` making "Founder, <brand>", or kept as typed when it already holds a comma, `jurisdiction`, `formatName`, `formatNameInline`, `pastClients` (brand names, "Brand A, Brand B", for "brands such as $ and other"), `packages`) and the client (with onboarding's designation and brands), and where a profile field is empty the sentence falls back to the agreement's own shape through the either forms `{{key|fallback}}` and `{{key?words with $|fallback}}` ("Our team owns the creative execution."), never an invented name; the founder's own names, numbers and prices are in no file, only in his profile. Section 6's table is the profile's packages (`ProfilePackages`, typed once on Agency settings' paper: the unit, up to `MAX_PROFILE_PACKAGES` rows of name, count and fee in paise, and the block sold past the largest; `parsePackages` the one reader; `withProfilePackages` when the agreement is made; the same rows feed the invoice's "From a package"; a retainer offer still prices from the client's retainer amount) with the effective rate per video and the row for blocks past the largest, and 6.1's worked example is computed from them (`packageExampleLine` through `docVars`); with no packages the client copy has no table and the owner's note says "Your packages are not set". The brief asks 3.1, 3.2 and 3.4 as tick boxes with Other (real checkboxes at the client's link, ☐ and ☑ on paper), 3.3 as "Client response:", 3.5 as the brand guide's lines and 3 to 5 links (`ContractQuestion` `heading`, `intro`, `group`, `answerLabel`, `fields`, `links`; `cleanContractAnswers` is the one reader of answers on every route), and 3.6 as the section's `tail`. It is drawn in the typed layout (`DocStyle.layout` `typed`, `DOC_LAYOUTS`: sans, left bold capitals, headings as typed, a grey hairline between sections, no reference row, letterhead or running title; a typed agreement keeps an empty client blank as a line to write on, `writeOnKeys`). On paper it follows the founder's PDF line for line (2026-11-03): `TYPED_MARGIN` in PrintSheet.tsx, the typed print block in app.css and `TYPED_PAGE_BREAKS` (`pageBreaksIn` in ContractView.tsx, `[data-page-break]`) put every page's first and last line where the PDF has them, 13 pages. Read-only paper keeps a hyphenated word whole (`ReadText`, `HYPHEN_WORD`: a nowrap span), the title keeps its last two words with a nowrap span (`tiedTitle`), never a U+00A0, U+2011 or any changed character in the text; the typed title steps down on a phone screen and beside a tablet's sidebar (`clamp`) so it stays on one line, and `useNoLoneWord` (`lib/noLoneWord.ts`) pulls in the right edge of a typed clause, list line or heading under the laptop width, 0.75em at a time up to 4.5em, until its last line is not one word (the owner's editable paper cannot take a span); words, paper and laptops are untouched. Printing every section gives 13 pages like the PDF; by default sections 3, 5 and 8 stay on the screen and at the link and the paper says so; the owner switches any section but the signatures on or off the paper with "Print this section" in its handle's menu, through the autosave on a draft or a sent one and, on a signed or void agreement or an approved document, through `POST /agency/contracts/:id/print` and `POST /agency/docs/:id/print` (`{sectionId, print}`, limit `agency`, owner only, a team member with Paperwork Edit through the gate and logged, guarded on `updated_at`; `withSectionPrint` in shared changes that one flag and nothing else, so a record's words, answers and acceptance never move; on a record the handle's menu holds only that item, unseen until pointed at or focused, a long press on the heading on a phone, which swallows its own lift so the menu stays open, and a section left off says how to put it back; 2026-11-07). `contractAsText` follows the paper. A scratch diff of the filled template against the PDF's text reports 0 differing words; the client answers the brief and accepts by typing their name at `/k/:token` (`agency_contracts`, answers kept only for questions that exist and options that were offered); while it is sent the answers save as they type (800 ms after the last change, on leaving a field and when the tab is put away, through `PUT /public/contracts/:token/answers`, behind `portalDocGate` and refused to a team session like the rest of `/public/contracts`, limit `contractAnswers` per address so it never eats `portal`, guarded `WHERE status = 'sent'` and 409 once signed, `cleanContractAnswers` the one reader, `updated_at` untouched so the owner's autosave is never refused, the words never logged), a quiet Saved line under the brief's heading says so and rides along the top of the screen while the brief is in view (`.brief-save`, sticky, white like the paper), a body that is not an object is a 400 (`objectBody` in db.ts, so never a 500), a failed save is tried again after 5 s and twice as long each time up to a minute, a refusal a retry cannot change stops and says signing still sends them, and a link that locked again (401 `locked`) brings the lock screen back with what was typed held in the tab's memory and saved once it is open, they load back on a revisit and the owner's page shows them, and signing still sends them; acceptance is typed and the screen says it is not a certified e-signature, and the templates say they are a starting point and not legal advice. Comments for changes live in `agency_doc_comments` for both. A PDF is the browser's print and save (`.no-print`, `.print-doc` in app.css, the title set to the number), because a Worker does not make one. A document (`.legal-doc`) is always white paper with dark ink, in dark mode too (its theme variables are pinned to the light values), and only the document prints: a print rule hides everything that is not the sheet, inside it or holding it, so headings, editors and buttons never reach the page. When the client bears the TDS (`tdsMode` `grossup`, "The client pays the TDS"), `invoiceTotals` adds the smallest extra amount that leaves exactly the price after the client's TDS comes off (to the cent, tested), the invoice shows the subtotal, "TDS adjusted*", the total, "TDS to deduct" and "To pay after TDS", and `tdsGrossupNote` prints under the total: "*Additional 10% adjusted on total invoice amount applicable for TDS deducted to be borne by the company as per the agreement terms." A paid or signed record stays through an erase of progress (`POST /me/reset` leaves Agency alone: it is business records, not progress); deleting the account removes the agency data, and an invoice stays for the books with its totals and loses who it was for, the lines, the bank and its link. **Team** (wave 14, migration 0076 `agency_team` with `agency_log.actor_id` and `agency_notes.author_id`; `routes/agencyTeam.ts`, `agencyTeamAccess.ts`, `components/AgencyTeam.tsx`, `TeamInvites.tsx`, `lib/agencyWorkspace.ts`; `TEAM_ROLES`, `TEAM_ROLE_META`, `teamDefaults`, `parseTeamAccess`, `teamAllows`, `MAX_TEAM` 25 and `MAX_TEAM_INVITES` 10 in shared `agencyTeam.ts`): the owner asks a content creator, a videographer, a video editor or a client (tied to one of their clients) by email, @username or friend code (`POST /agency/team`, limit `teamInvite`; a block reads like an unknown person, "We could not find that person."), which also sends a friend request through `requestFriend` when the two are not friends and the person takes them, and gives each member Off, View or Edit per tab (Overview, Pipeline, Clients, Paperwork; `parseTeamAccess` is the one reader: Overview is never Edit, a client never sees Clients and never edits), changed with `PATCH /agency/team/:id`, resent at most once a day, or removed. The member answers or leaves under `/me/teams` (core, like `/me/coach`, so it works with Agency off) and picks whose Agency to work on (the Agency select, `?team=`, kept for the tab in sessionStorage); every /agency request then carries `X-Agency-Team`, and the one gate reads the row on every request, opens only the routes on `TEAM_ROUTE_RULES` at their tab's level (never settings, adding or deleting clients, the client link or its password, Army, time, template writes or the team itself), runs the route as the owner so every owner filter still holds, scrubs the answer (no business profile or bank, private notes, fees, the retainer amount, the client link or Army links) and logs a member's write in the client's log with who did it; a member deletes only their own notes. A client member reads one read-only view (`GET /me/teams/:id/client-view`). Both sides are told in the inbox; the rows are in both people's export and go with either account and with the tied client. **Paper and documents** (migration 0064, `components/PrintSheet.tsx`, `DocActions.tsx`, `DocStyleSheet.tsx`, `AgencyDocs.tsx`, `routes/agencyOther.ts`, `routes/adminAgency.ts`, `agencyPaper.ts`; the build is the 2026-11-02 line in docs/DECISIONS.md): every invoice, agreement and other document is drawn by `PrintSheet` and prints (the browser's print and save, `?print=1` opens the dialog on load, the title set to "Invoice KR-0007" or "Agreement - Acme - Pilot") as a legal page: A4 or Letter, serif, numbered small-caps headings, a parties table, signature blocks kept whole with the typed acceptance as "Accepted electronically by <name> on <date and time>" and the not-a-certified-e-signature line, a stamp (DRAFT, REVISED, PAID, OVERDUE, SIGNED, APPROVED, VOID), a running title and "Page X of Y" in `@page` margin boxes (Chrome and Edge 131 and up; a fixed footer elsewhere); the print block at the end of app.css is the one place raw black, white and rule colours are allowed. A document's look is `DocStyle` (`parseDocStyle`, keys only, inside its own JSON, the person's default in `profile.docStyle`; CSS maps the keys to values, stored data never becomes a raw value; a logo is the person's own upload, checked by `ownLogo`). `contractAsText` and `invoiceAsText` (the client's version) make the text version, shown in a Fold, copied with Copy text and emailed with Email a copy (`POST /agency/invoices|contracts|docs/:id/email`, limit `docEmail` ten an hour, the link built from `PUBLIC_URL` never the Host, never marketing, says plainly when email is not set up, logs the masked address in `agency_doc_log` and never the words; a draft has no link so is refused). On the owner's pages a list row is one link and a More menu (`PaperRowMenu`), and a document page is the paper itself, edited in place (see Paper is edited in place below); it is always whole once it is a record. Download shows to a client on an invoice only while its download switch is on and always on an agreement or document once sent. Other documents (`agency_docs`, `AGENCY_DOC_KINDS`: strategy session summary, trial video terms built from the trial agreement, retainer proposal priced from a monthly fee, monthly report of counts and titles only) share the agreement's shape and editor at `/agency/docs/:id`, go to the client at `/d/<token>` behind the client's password (`portalDocGate`), are approved once by a typed name and are then a record, revised into a new draft; `ClientDocs` in `AgencyDocs.tsx` is the Documents block for a client's page. House templates (`agency_house_templates`, Admin, Agency templates, `/admin/agency/templates`) are made, changed and retired only by a super admin from their own finished paper, never carry who it was for or the admin's business details unless "include my business details" is ticked, show to every Agency user as a House template and are copied into their own documents (copy only, so a client's document never changes under them); nothing is seeded. Business metrics (`kpis`, `kpi_values`, `kpi_prefs`; `/reviews/kpis`; `BusinessMetrics` in Reviews) are for people who run a business: a switch on the Reviews page, off until the person turns it on, and the server answers 403 to everything but the switch itself while it is off; a metric is typed in for each period or read from Agency (`KPI_AUTO`: pieces published, approved first time, published on time, billable hours, utilisation, invoiced, paid, new clients), judged against its target by `kpiStatus`, up to `MAX_KPIS`. Limits `agency` and `portal`; every route is in `ROUTE_LIMITS`. **Lifecycle** (migration 0063; `routes/agencyLife.ts` for the owner, `lifecycle.ts`, `agencyCycles.ts`, `portalAccess.ts`; `AGENCY_STAGES` in shared; `components/StageBar.tsx`, `AgencyLifecycle.tsx`, `PortalLock.tsx`): a client goes brand reaches out, then agreement sent, agreement signed, strategy session, trial video, pilot month, retainer proposed, retainer active, monthly delivery (`agency_clients.stage`, nine keys in `AGENCY_STAGE_KEYS`, each with what the client does, what the owner does and a checklist). The owner moves one stage forward at a time or one back to undo (`moveProblem`, `POST /agency/clients/:id/stage`, guarded `WHERE stage = from`, every move a line in `agency_log`); the app moves it by itself only forward and only on what happened (`autoStage`: an agreement sent or signed through `syncStageFromAgreements`, which the client page, the client link and the cron all run; an offer sent; a retainer started). A step back sticks: the sync counts only an agreement sent or signed after the client reached their current stage (`sent_at` or `signed_at` later than `stage_since`), so stepping back to Enquiry with an agreement still out leaves them at Enquiry until one is sent or signed again or the owner moves them (the founder, 2026-11-07; before, the next page load moved them straight forward). The progress bar is at the top of the owner's client page and of the client link (an ordered list with `aria-current="step"` and a word for every state). `TRIAL_RULES` are quoted from the Trial and pilot agreement (a shared test checks each still appears in it word for word) and are shown to the client in the trial stage and to the owner on the client page; the docs hold no other trial rules, so none are invented. **The password on a client link** is the owner's (`portal_pass_hash` and salt on `agency_clients`, set, changed, cleared or generated from the client page with `PUT /agency/clients/:id/password`; `portalPasswordProblem`, ten characters or more; a generated one is four words and two digits, shown once, drawn again until no word of it is part of the client's name, `newPortalPassword(clientName)`): hashed by auth's own `hashPassword` (PBKDF2, 100,000 rounds, a `randomHex(16)` salt) and compared with auth's `safeEqual`, with a decoy hash when the link is unknown so the time matches. `POST /public/agency/:token/unlock` (and `/public/contracts/:token/unlock`, `/public/invoices/:token/unlock`, `/public/agency/doc/:token/unlock`) is limited per address (`portalLogin`, 5 in ten minutes) and per client (`portalLoginLink`), answers a wrong password and an unknown link in the same words, and returns a portal session: 32 random bytes kept as a SHA-256 hash in `portal_sessions`, twelve hours, per client, held by the browser in sessionStorage only (`lib/portalSession.ts`, never a cookie or localStorage) and sent as `X-Portal-Session`. A client with a password gets `401 {error: 'locked', clientName, agencyName, agencyLogo}` and nothing more until then (`agencyFace` in portalAccess.ts is all a client page says about the business: the business name in Agency settings or the person's name, their own logo upload, the settings email); `requirePortalAccess(c, clientId)` is the one check any client-facing route calls, and `portalDocGate` in front of the agreement, invoice and document links applies it to their clients without the routes knowing. Changing or clearing the password, a fresh link or closing it ends every session; the cron prunes the expired ones; hashes and sessions are never in the export; the log keeps the fact and never the word; the app log names a link as `:token` (a team link too, `cleanPath`). A client with no password behaves as before and the page warns "Anyone with the link can open this. Set a password."; the add-client form asks for one by default. A new client needs a name, an email and the password the owner sets or generates (`POST /agency/clients` answers 400 "set a password for their page" without one); only the owner ever sets, changes, generates or clears it, and no client route can. **Asking for a new password** (migration 0071 `agency_password_requests`): the lock screen of every client link and the client's page offer "Lost it, or think it was shared?", which opens "Ask for a new password" with an optional short reason (`PasswordAsk` and `PortalLock` in `components/PortalLock.tsx`) (`POST /public/agency/password-request`, `kind` agency, contract, invoice or doc, limit `portalPasswordAsk` per address, `PASSWORD_REQUEST_REASON_MAX`); it answers the same for an unknown link, a link with no password and one already asked about, keeps one open ask per client (a partial unique index, `INSERT OR IGNORE`), keeps the reason in that row only (never `agency_log` or the app log), and tells the owner once in the inbox (`pwreq-<client>`) and by one private email; the owner's client page shows "<client> asked for a new password", setting a new one closes the ask in the same batch and ends every session, `POST /agency/clients/:id/password-request/close` dismisses it, while it is open "Set a new password" is the page's one solid button and the stage move is outlined (`AgencyLifecycle.tsx`), and the app never sends a password (the owner shares it by another channel). Every owner control for it is one module, `components/ClientPassword.tsx` (2026-11-04): `ClientPassword` (the state in words, a `StatusTag`, one main action, a `MoreMenu` with Generate, Dismiss the ask and Remove behind a confirm sheet), `PasswordField` and `ShownOnce`; it sits on the client page (the Link segment, and at the top while an ask is open or there is no password), on every document page through `DocPassword` and in the add-client form. Since the popups pass (2026-11-06) a document page draws it under the paper in a closed `Fold` titled "Password for <client>" (`DocPasswordFold` in `AgencyLifecycle.tsx`; More's Password item opens it and moves focus there with `revealIn`), and setting or changing the password opens a form inline in the card, under its main button (aria-expanded, a `ToggleChevron`; Cancel, Escape or a save hand focus back to that button), never a sheet. Its menu and its confirm and shown-once sheets still portal to `document.body`, since the card sits in a blurred card (`Sheet` and `MoreMenu` do not portal, so anything fixed inside a transformed sheet panel or a blurred card is clipped; a component that opens a sheet from inside one portals it). **Team link** (2026-11-04, migration 0081 `agency_team_link`, `routes/teamLink.ts`, `teamLinkAccess.ts`, `components/TeamLink.tsx`, `TeamPortal` in `site/ClientPortal.tsx` at `/t/:token`; `TEAM_LINK_PARTS`, `TEAM_LINK_ACTIONS`, `TEAM_LINK_NEVER`, `TEAM_LINK_BLOCKED`, `parseTeamLinkShow` and `TeamLinkView` in shared `agency.ts`): a second link per client for the client's own team (a junior), the client page without the paper. It has its own token (`team_link_token`, 16 characters), switches (`team_link_json`: waiting, coming, published, progress, notes, channels; notes on, approving off until the owner allows it), password (`team_link_pass_hash`, the same `portalPasswordProblem`, `newPortalPassword`, PBKDF2 and decoy timing) and sessions (`team_link_sessions`, sent as `X-Team-Link-Session`, kept apart in the browser by `keepTeamLinkSession`); owner routes `POST /agency/clients/:id/team-link` (on, off, fresh, show), `PUT …/team-link/password`, `POST …/team-link/password-request/close`; public `GET /public/team-link/:token`, `POST …/unlock` (limits `teamLinkLogin` per address, `teamLinkLoginLink` per client), `POST …/items/:id` (limit `portal`). Whatever is switched on it never shows or reaches `TEAM_LINK_NEVER` (invoices and money, agreements, other documents, the retainer and its fee, onboarding, billing, the stage's paperwork steps, hours, private notes and paid-change notes): `TeamLinkView` has no field for them and is built from the deliverable tables only, its routes look clients up by the team token only (an invoice, agreement, document or client link token finds no page), and every `/public/agency`, `/public/contracts` and `/public/invoices` route answers 404 to a request carrying a team session (`refuseTeamSession`); a team session is never a portal session, so it opens no locked client link or paper. It never opens without a password (2026-11-05): switching it on takes one in the same request (`password` on the on call, the client link's rule; 400 "set a password for the team link" without one, unless one is already set), "Switch the team link on" (with no password set yet) opens a form inline in the team link card with `PasswordField` first (2026-11-06; a password set from before switches it straight on, and after a save focus moves to Copy team link), and its password cannot be removed while it is on (400, and no Remove in its menu then). Renewing, closing or re-passwording either link never touches the other; a retired team token answers 410 through `retired_portal_links` and `closedLinkAnswer`. A note or approval from it is a client note marked `via_team_link` (`clientTeam` on the owner's note, `team` on a thread line, "Client's team" and "Your team" on screen), the owner's inbox says "<client>'s team", and a paid change is never asked from it. Its "Lost it?" ask is kind `team` on `POST /public/agency/password-request` (`agency_password_requests.link` 'team', one open ask per client and link, note `pwreq-team-<client>`). The owner's card sits under the client link on the Link segment (outline Copy team link, a `MoreMenu`, a switch sheet with the never-list in a `Fold`, and `ClientPassword link="team"`); the page says "Team view: paperwork and payments are not shown here." Settings are in the export (never a hash, salt or session) and go with the client and the account; the cron prunes expired team sessions. **Client onboarding** (migration 0071, `routes/agencyLife.ts`, `components/ClientOnboarding.tsx`, `parseOnboarding` and `onboardingProblem` in shared): a client added from then on (`onboarding_needed`) unlocks the link and sees only a short stepped form until it is sent: company legal name, brands for the trial, address with PIN and state, GSTIN and PAN (optional, checked), point of contact, designation, email, phone, billing email, then the trial brief from the owner's own Trial template (3.1 to 3.5); saved as they go (`PUT /public/agency/:token/onboarding`, limit `onboardingSave`, 300 in ten minutes per address) and sent once (`POST …/onboarding/submit`, guarded `WHERE onboarded_at IS NULL`, limit `onboardingSubmit` per address; each has its own budget, so a long form's saves never use up `portal` and refuse the Send with a 429, as happened on 2026-11-06), behind `requirePortalAccess`. What the client's record already holds (billing legal name, address read back into line, city, state and PIN, GSTIN, PAN taken from the GSTIN, phone, contact and billing email) fills the empty fields when the form loads (`onboardingFromRecord` in shared), never over what the client typed, so a step already answered opens finished and the client is not asked again. On submit the details land on the client record (billing and contact), the Trial agreement is made once, filled from the profile and the form with the brief answered, and sent at once when the business side is complete (else left a draft for the owner), and the owner is told in the inbox and by one private email that never carries the answers; then the client's page opens as before. A client made before stays as it was. The form is in the export and goes with the client and the account; closed asks go after `RETENTION_DAYS.passwordRequests`. **Retainer** (`agency_retainers`): after the pilot the owner offers up to four lengths of 1 to 24 months, each with an optional discount in basis points, the month's fee coming from the client's retainer amount (the one place that amount reaches the client, and only inside an open offer); the client chooses at the link (`POST /public/agency/:token/retainer`, only an offered length, limited by `portal`); the owner confirms; each step is one guarded status change (`offered`, `chosen`, `active`, then `ended` or `withdrawn`), a month's fee is whole cents with the discount rounded to the nearest cent (`retainerMonthlyCents`), and an ended retainer is renewed with a new row. **Months** (`runAgencyCycles` in the cron, and once at confirm): each month of a running retainer makes its invoice (a draft through `createInvoice` in the series, sent through `setInvoiceStatus` when the retainer's auto-send is on) and its pieces (titled "Month 3, piece 2", idea stage, due days spread through the month), on the retainer's own day counted by the repeat rule (`retainerCycles`, a retainer begun on the 31st starts on the 28th or 29th in February, the 31st again in March) in the owner's zone; `UNIQUE (retainer_id, month)` on `agency_cycles`, written in the same batch as the pieces, is what makes a month happen once however often the cron runs, a month missed while nothing ran is made on the next run, and a run that stopped between the pieces and the invoice is finished half an hour later. **Changes**: a piece has changes included (`revisions_allowed`, else the client's `revisions_per_piece`, two like both agreements say; the retainer sets it for the pieces it makes, so a new month starts with a fresh count); the client's "Ask for a change" spends one and sends the piece from review or approved back to making in one guarded statement (two taps spend one), writes a note of kind `change` and tells the owner; with none left the client can only leave a `paid_change` note, which moves nothing; a plain `comment` is always possible where the owner allowed notes; the owner adds a change to a piece. Brand teams that run Army campaigns use Agency by the ordinary switch: an admin turns it on for a person (Admin, People, "What does this person see?") or for a whole organization (a `flag_overrides` row with `subject_type` `organization`), and nothing in Agency assumes a creator. **Templates** (migration 0055 `agency_templates`, `routes/agencyDocs.ts`, `INVOICE_STARTERS` and `CONTRACT_TEMPLATES` in shared): a new invoice or agreement starts from a built-in starting point or from the person's own template (`templateId` or `starter` on `POST /agency/invoices`, `templateId` on `POST /agency/contracts`); any invoice or agreement can be saved as a template (`POST /agency/templates` with `fromId`, `PATCH` renames it or replaces its contents from another one), up to `MAX_TEMPLATES` of each kind. A saved invoice template keeps the look, sender, bank, GST and TDS and the lines but never who it was billed to (`invoiceTemplateDoc`); an agreement template keeps the sections but not the names filled in (`contractTemplateDoc`). Templates are owner only, in the export, gone with the account. **Paper is edited in place** (2026-11-03, `components/PaperEdit.tsx`, `lib/useAutosave.ts`): an agreement, another document, an invoice and a house template are edited on the page that prints, never in sheets or a stack of summary cards. Every heading, paragraph, bullet, table cell and blank is a plain-text editable region (`EditableText`, `contenteditable="plaintext-only"` with a plain `contenteditable` fallback whose paste and drop insert text only; words go in and out through `textContent` and `innerText`, never HTML); a blank is a `VarField` on the document's variable, a fee a `MoneyCell` read with `typedCents`. A section's words are `parseBodyBlocks` blocks (paragraph, `- ` bullet, `### ` subhead, whole bold line), joined back byte for byte by `joinBodyBlocks` (a shared test round-trips every built-in body): Enter adds a block of the same kind, Backspace in an empty one removes it, the arrows move between regions. A section's handle (`SectionHandle`, hidden until the section is pointed at or has focus, a long press on the heading on a phone) moves it, switches "Print this section", adds a section below and deletes it (asked twice). The page saves as you go (`useAutosave`: one PATCH 800 ms after the last change with the whole paper and the version it read, `ifUpdatedAt` on agreements, documents and house templates and `ifVersion` on invoices, answered 409 `changed` when another tab saved first, then the page reloads and says "Changed somewhere else. Reloaded."); the quiet line beside the status says Saving or Saved, with Undo for the last save. Each document page shows back, the title, one `StatusTag`, then one main action for its state (Send to client on a draft, Revise on a signed or approved one, none when sent or void), Download and one `MoreMenu` (copy link, email a copy, copy text, comments, client, client permissions, password, duplicate, save as template, save as house template, style, take back to draft, void, delete); nothing else sits above the paper (4 visible buttons on a draft at 390 and 1440). Since the popups pass (2026-11-06) an agreement or other document that can still change shows one bar under its header instead of two sheets (`DocClientBar` in `AgencyContract.tsx`): "Who it is for" as a dropdown and Client permissions as the Write comments switch with the line on what the client can always do; More's Client and Client permissions items move focus to it (`revealIn`). Switching the client link or the team link off asks first in a sheet (a red "Yes, switch it off", an outline "Keep it on"), like a fresh link. Agency settings is the same: the business profile is typed on a sample invoice and agreement paper (`PaperText`; what only the sample shows, such as its client, is never saved), saved as you go with Saved and Undo, and sent once more when the tab or page is left inside the wait. House templates open as paper from Admin (`?tpl=<id>`, `GET` and `PATCH /admin/agency/templates/:id` with `body`, admin only, audited, still never carrying names or, unless included, business details). **Fill in the details** (2026-11-05, the founder's ask "in trial engagement where to fill in all that data?"; `components/FillDetails.tsx`, `contractVarsUsed` and `contractVarGroups` in shared contract.ts): one `Sheet` on the agreement page and the other-documents page lists every blank the paper's words use (plain and either forms, the signature block's name and designation; never the currency in words or the package table, which links to Agency settings), in reading order under Your business (saved to the business profile with `PUT /agency/settings`), The client (their record, `PATCH /agency/clients/:id`, the changes per piece through `PUT /agency/clients/:id/lifecycle`; with no client picked these stay on the paper) and This agreement only (the paper's own `vars`), each with the line saying where it saves, "N of M details are not filled yet", "Not filled yet" and Optional marks, `PHONE_HINT` and `phoneProblem` on phones. Saving writes the records first, then sets this paper's words through the page's own autosave (`ifUpdatedAt`, its 409 reload): a word a record change altered is changed, a blank the records now fill is filled, and a word typed by hand on the paper stays unless the form changed it. A signed or approved paper opens it read only with Revise; a team member edits only the paper's own lines. It is the first item of More, the page's main action on a draft while a blank is empty (Send to client then waits in More), and the owner's gap note ends with "Fill them in"; Agency settings opens the same sheet with the business group only ("Fill in your business details", saved through the settings page's own autosave). A fold-out is `components/Fold.tsx` (a rounded flat button with a chevron, tinted while open; `adds` for a fold that opens a form to add something, whose mark is a plus that turns into a cross; `tone="plain"` for the laptop sidebar's groups, controlled so their open state follows the route), never a bare `<details>` (Help's and the public home's questions, the pillar page's layer lists and the sidebar's groups are Folds). Short titles in narrow cards keep their last two words together with `tie()` in `lib/text.ts`.
- From the Superfocus study (`docs/superfocus-study.md`, which also says what was left out and why): rules only, no model, so no credits. **Plan my week** on Daily (`planWeek`, `GET /today/week-plan?date=&hours=`, `components/WeekPlan.tsx`): the open tasks that have no day get a suggested day in the next seven, highest priority first (then ones made from a pillar), each on the lightest day it fits in, never past the hours a day the person set (default `PLAN_DAY_MINUTES`, 1 to 12); a task with a do date or a due date is already placed, a late one counts on today; **plan strength** (`planStrength`: days with something planned 40, days not overloaded 40, tasks with an estimate 20; Strong 75 and up, Fair 50, Light) is shown now and with the suggestions kept, with up to four plain notes. The route writes nothing: Keep applies each ticked suggestion through the ordinary `PATCH /nodes/:id` `{doDate}`. **Time blocks** (`dayBlocks`, `components/DayBlocks.tsx`) lay the day's tasks that have a time out by their estimate (30 minutes when none), flag overlaps and show free stretches of 15 minutes or more. A project's **pace** (`projectPace`, `components/PaceTag.tsx`, on the pillar page): the share of its tasks done against the share of days gone between the day it was made and its due day, within ten points On track, within thirty Slipping, further Behind, nothing shown without a due day or tasks. **Focus quality** (migration 0056 `focus_rounds.quality`, `PATCH /wellbeing/focus/:id`, `FOCUS_QUALITY` Scattered to Deep): one tap after a finished focus session, private, pays nothing, never changes what a round logs; the dashboard's tile is the average of the rounds that were rated (an unrated round is left out, never a zero). The **Lo-fi** sound (`AMBIENT_SOUNDS` `lofi`) is made in the browser from chords, a soft beat and crackle; it follows the master switch and the volume like every sound. The **private calendar link** (`calendar_feeds`, `/me/calendar`, public `GET /api/public/calendar/<token>.ics`, `buildCalendar`): off until switched on, a 24-character secret, a fresh one closes the old, carries only a title, a pillar and a priority (and the time, in the person's zone, when a task has one), never a note, the journal, a mood or anything from a tracker, and is one way: it shows tasks in a calendar and never changes them. Help says a web app cannot block other apps.
- Reviews on a calendar (migration 0057 `review_schedules`, `routes/reviews.ts`, `cron.ts` `sendReviewReminders`, `components/ReviewCalendar.tsx`): a person schedules a review for a day and a time (`kind` a day, a week, a month, a quarter, a half year or a year; `anchor` the first day; `time` HH:MM in their zone), once or repeating the way a task repeats (`Repeat` and `RepeatPicker`: every N days, weekdays, weekly on the weekdays they choose, monthly on a day they choose with a short month falling on its last day, yearly, an optional end day; `occurrencesBetween` and `nextOnOrAfter` in shared are the one rule, and a weekly rule that names weekdays never fires on a first date that is not one of them), up to `MAX_REVIEW_SCHEDULES`. `GET /reviews/schedule?month=` gives the month grid its reminders and the reviews already written (marked on the day their period ended, each with the name it was saved under, else its period's, so a new half reads as its part and an older one keeps "Half-year review"). Removing a reminder or a whole repeating series is a second tap (`RemoveTwice`, "Tap again to remove the series" on solid red, with "Reviews you wrote stay."), since a schedule holds none of the person's words and can be set again. The cron sends one inbox note on the day at the time (id `review-<id>-<day>`, so once; a day missed while nothing ran is skipped, never sent late), linking to the period it covers (on its questions, `#review-heading`, where the calendar and a past review open it too) and named by `reviewNameFor`: on the first day of a week, month or quarter it opens the one that just ended, a half or a year goes by `reviewPeriodNow` (`reviewPeriodFor`; so a reminder on 1 or 5 January opens the year just ended and the half's start-of-year setting, and one in early July the mid-year check-in), and a daily review by its time (set before noon it opens the day before, else that day); `next_on` is moved on after each so the cron only looks at what is due. Scheduled reviews also show in the private calendar link (category Reviews). A task can be **brought to the review** (`nodes.for_review`, `forReview` on create and `PATCH /nodes/:id`, the Toggle in `TaskPlanFields`, the tag 📝 For review): the review for a period lists it under "Tasks you brought" (`flaggedFor` and `ReviewSummary.flagged`: ticked in the period, or still open and planned in it, or open with no day while the period is the current one), each with an "Add to my answer" that appends to an answer and never overwrites; a saved review keeps the list it was saved with, and the reminder says how many are waiting. A repeating task carries the flag to the next one. Owner only, in the export, gone with the account.
- Emerging (wave 16, 2026-11-04; the rules `insightsFor` and `insightsForEvery` in `insights.ts`, the subpath `@kreators/shared/insights`, read by the API and the tests; the types, the small helpers and each place's share, `insightsForPlace` and `insightsForApp`, in `insightPlaces.ts`, in the barrel; `GET /insights` in `routes/insights.ts`, `components/EmergenceSlot.tsx`, `Emergence.tsx`, `screens/Insights.tsx`): patterns read across the person's own apps (a habit from a task that keeps coming back, days that go together, the weekday check-ins slip, since the last review), rules only, no model, computed on read and stored nowhere, owner only and core. One query (2026-11-05): `GET /insights` (limit `insights`, 240 an hour per person, with the dashboard below) answers the person's whole list with each pillar's own balance line (`insightsForEvery`), the client keeps it once per device day (`['insights', day]`) and each place picks its share with `insightsForPlace`; an ordinary write only marks it stale (`useRefreshAll`), and then it waits fifteen minutes from its last read before reading again (2026-11-07: it visits two months of the person's rows), the Make it a habit and not to do sheets refetch it, the agency-load read has `idx_agency_items_user_due` (migration 0083), and `insights.golden.test.ts` pins the whole output. A signal runs only while every app it reads is on, and the reads for an app that is off are never sent; the journal is read by when an entry was written, never a word, wellbeing by kind, day and number, never a note or an extra, money only in the person's currency; nothing reaches the assistant, staff or the leaderboard, and a coach gets it only through one consented door: the student's own Patterns switch on that link, the Emerging rules run on read over only the parts that link shares, a fixed list of keys, stored nowhere (see Life coaches); and a line says things happen together, never that one causes another (`CAUSE_WORDS`, `INSIGHT_BASIS_TAIL`). Local times go through `localStamp` beside `zonedToUtc`. One block after a screen's numbers and before How it works (Home's top three with See all, Daily, Pillars, each pillar page, Mood, Sleep, Stress, Mindfulness, Wellbeing, Life, Journal, Finance, Reviews, Discover and Fitness), downloaded only when there is a ready line: a card is the number, a `StatusTag` in the corner, one sentence, a small chart, the basis line, one outline action and a More menu (Not now, kept per person on the device). Make it a habit opens a sheet with the suggested repeat, time and first day and keeps through the ordinary `PATCH /nodes/:id` or `POST /nodes`. The ways in (2026-11-06): Home's and Life's Emerging sections always show (`EmergenceSlot` `always`, Home and Life; with nothing ready for Home it is one quiet line, how many patterns were found elsewhere or are still forming, and See all, without downloading the Emergence chunk), Reviews has "See what's emerging" under The numbers, opening on the period it is on, and Help keeps its line; never in the sidebar or the phone menu (the founder, 2026-11-06). **Good and bad habits** (the 2026-11-04 ask, key `habit-tie`, `habitTies`, `classifyHabitTie` the one classifier, `HabitTie` on the insight): a task ticked on 3 or more days in 8 weeks (any day for one counted as a habit; one the person said is not a habit only on a strong pattern, `HABIT_TIE_STRICT_DAYS` 10 each side and half the scale, and never watched), or an item of the not to do list (a slip is the day with it, a kept day the day without, an unmarked day is not counted), is set against the person's own days with it and without, through `splitCompare` with `HABIT_TIE_MIN_DAYS` 5 and `PAIR_MIN_DAYS` 7: mood, sleep that night (the next morning's log), stress (lower is better), other tasks ticked and focus minutes, each only while its app is on. How the day felt decides first; tasks and focus decide only when no feeling is clear and are said beside it only when they agree. A clear lift is a **Good habit** (`StatusTag` good), a clear dip a **Bad habit** (bad), a slip can only be bad, and too few days, no gap or a mix is Still watching with what it needs. The line names the task and says what went together ("On days you ticked “Evening walk”, your mood was 0.8 higher, on average."), the basis ends with `INSIGHT_BASIS_TAIL`. A good habit offers Make it a habit (or Open the task once it repeats), a bad one Add to not to do (`NotToDoFold`, in the card, the ordinary `POST /today/not-to-do`, while the list has room and the name is not on it) or Open the task; a not to do item opens the list (`/today#ntd-heading`); the More menu always opens where the thing lives. The plain habit card is not shown for a task that has a good or bad habit card. Home takes up to two of them, Daily two, a pillar page its own. The not to do list keeps its own name; only an item of it is shown under Bad habit. `/insights` is a page, not an app: the period filter, Your life, two counts, What stands out with a `Segmented` filter (`show`), then a Good and bad habits block (two counts and a `Segmented` Good, Bad, All, `habits` in the URL), then Still watching with what each needs in a `Fold`, and How it works from `HOW_TO` `insights` (`HowItWorks` takes `page`). **By period** (2026-11-07, the founder: "emergence to display more clearly and with dashboards data that is useful"): a `PeriodFilter` of all six periods (`kind` and `date` in the URL, the words Reviews uses; a period still to come or a day not on the calendar opens that kind's own period, and the server refuses it with a 400) sets the page's window; with none picked no pill is pressed, the row says The last 8 weeks and the page is what it always was, byte for byte (the golden test), and "Back to the last 8 weeks" returns there. `GET /insights?kind=&start=` reads the period's days (`InsightFacts.window`, the night after its last day for sleep) and every card names it in its basis line (`spanOf`, a test walks every card); the minimum days stay as they are, a line a period cannot get to says so (`needOr`: "Not enough days in this week for this one.", "One day is not enough for this one."), What stands out says once why a short period shows more under Still watching, and Since your last review stays with the rolling view. **Your life, this week** (`GET /insights/dashboard?kind=&start=&day=`, core, owner only, limit `insights`; `lifeDashboardFrom` in the subpath `@kreators/shared/lifeDashboard`, of which the web app imports only types, so its words stay on the server; `components/LifeDashboardBlock.tsx`): built on `numbersFor` and `layerFor`, never the supplement count, it is the five areas of life in `LIFE_AREA_ORDER` (physical: sleep, movement, eating, water; financial: money in, money out, budgets over, savings goals, other currencies named under them and never added; mental: focus with its quality, stress, mindful minutes; emotional: mood, journal and gratitude as counts; spiritual: breath and stillness, the mindful minutes no focus round wrote, found by `focus_rounds.log_id` and never by a log's extra, small acts, the routine for someone who keeps one), then the seven pillars in their order on their soft colour and flower (xp, tasks ticked, the layer the period walks, a day's still open). Each tile is one link: its name, the number big, the change as a `StatusTag`, a small chart at its foot (`PillBars` up to eight points, else `AreaLine`, a share a `Ring`; up to a month by day, up to 200 days by week, longer by month; a day draws the week up to it). A running period is set beside the same number of days of the one before, a closed one beside the whole one before, the last 8 weeks beside the 8 before. The change is good the good way (lower for stress, toward 7.5 hours for sleep), warm the other way where the way is clear, neutral where it is not ours to judge (money out, journal entries, sleep moving away from the middle). A day says done or not, never "1 of 1". A part shows only while its app is on, and an area with nothing on goes. The grid sizes by its own column (`@lg`, `@3xl`), so a tablet beside the sidebar keeps a tile wide enough. **Clearer patterns**: What stands out groups by area of life, then Across your days (`PLACE` in Insights.tsx), the strongest first, two a group (one on a phone) then Show N more; plain habits join Good and bad habits (four, two on a phone, then more). On /insights only (`corner` on `InsightCard` and `InsightGrid`) the state tag sits in the corner beside the "…", the chart draws the sentence's lead pairing, the basis leaves off the tail the cover says once, and two cards share a row only from a 48rem column; every other screen's card is as it was. Daily lists good and bad habits as a section of their own, **Habits** (2026-11-05, `HabitsBlock` in `Emergence.tsx` through `HabitsSlot`, right under Not to do, with Fitness's Supplements fold after it when that is on): short rows (the task's words, Good habit or Bad habit in the corner, the one sentence, the basis, the card's own action and More), up to `DAILY_HABITS_MAX` (5) apart from Daily's two other patterns (`insightsForApp` for `today`), the charts left to /insights; Daily's Emerging block leaves them out (`exclude`). **Your kinds of day** (2026-11-06, the founder's ask; key `daytype-done`): a day's kind is the day template set for that date, else the weekly pattern's; the default (Everyday), a deleted template and a day before the account count as none (`kindsOfDays` in shared `dayKinds.ts`, a leaf in the `shared-base` chunk beside `templateForDate`, the one reading for all four places). The card shows the last four weeks as a strip in each template's colour (`templateColor`: the template's own colour, `day_templates.color`, migration 0098, picked in its form with the block picker, each starting template its own wheel colour, three pairs sharing (docs/time-blocks.md, "A kind's colour"); with none, its longest block over the day, leaving out the slots that cross midnight) and each group's tasks a day, focus minutes (only while Mindfulness is on) and mood (only while Mood is on), the sentence saying a kind and a reading "went together", never a cause; it has its own block (`KindsSlot`, `KindsBlock`) on Daily, the Time blocks page and Reviews, whose Emerging blocks leave it out. Reviews' The numbers has a Kinds of day tile (`PeriodNumbers.kindDays` and `kinds`, `SummaryPart` `kinds`, days up to the person's own today); the month calendar on Daily marks each day with its kind's dot (the name in its label) and its day sheet sets the kind for any date through `PUT /time-blocks/days`, "Follow your week" clearing it (`GET /today/month` `kinds`); the Time blocks page has a This month line (`GET /time-blocks/month?day=`). **Fitness and gratitude** (2026-11-07, the founder's "make the emergence/relationships through out the app"; `insights.fitness.test.ts`, the golden person `fit`): movement (`move` rows, minutes only) is read while Fitness or Kreators Run is on (both write it), the eating check-in (`eat`, how well 1 to 5 by `eatHowWell`, a fasting day's 0 left out, never its extra or note) while Fitness is on (supplements are never read by Emerging), and gratitude only as which days have it (`InsightFacts.gratitudeDays`, `gratitude_days.day`, never a line) while Journal is on. **Moving** is a good-habit candidate in `habitTies` (`HabitTie.kind` `move`, id `habit-tie:move`, Yourself): a day with movement against a day the app was used without, from the first day moved in eight weeks, on mood, sleep that night, stress, tasks and focus like any habit, but only ever a Good habit (`classifyHabitTie` `goodOnly`: a worse day after moving is Still watching), "On days you moved, …", private, on Home, Daily's Habits, Fitness, Life and the Yourself pillar page, its action Open Fitness (or Kreators Run's tool). **When you move** (`move-weekday`): average active minutes on each weekday over eight weeks (every day counted) once `MOVE_WEEKDAY_MIN_DAYS` (8) days had movement; a weekday holding a quarter of the minutes stands out ("You move most on Saturdays: …, against … on other days"), a spread says so and ranks 0. **How you eat and how you feel** (`eat-days`): days the person said they ate well (4 or 5 of 5) against the other days they checked in, on mood, stress and sleep that night, each only while its app is on, the clearest one said, once `EAT_MIN_DAYS` (10) check-ins are in. **Gratitude and mood** (`gratitude-mood`): mood on days with gratitude written against days without, on Journal and Mood. Since a review, active minutes are a mover while Fitness or Kreators Run is on. Fitness's screen has its own block (`EmergenceSlot app="fitness"`); the four cards group under Body on /insights. **Water and budgets** (2026-11-07, the founder: "Water and budgets in Emerging patterns"; `insights.period.test.ts`): **days you met your water goal** (`water-days`, core) against days you logged water under it, an unlogged day counting on neither side, through `classifyHabitTie` `goodOnly` with `HABIT_TIE_MIN_DAYS` and `PAIR_MIN_DAYS` on mood, sleep that night, stress, tasks and focus (stress left out when `stress-calm` already says it of water), never a bad habit, anything else Still watching; **the day you drink least** (`water-weekday`, `WATER_WEEKDAY_MIN_DAYS` 14 logged days, a weekday needing two of them, `WATER_WEEKDAY_MIN_GAP_ML` 200 and 15% under the other days, a spread says so and ranks 0). **A budget that keeps running over** (`budget-over`, Finance on; over in at least two finished months and half of them, among `BUDGET_OVER_MIN_MONTHS` 3 or more with spending logged; the last `BUDGET_OVER_MONTHS` 6, or a picked quarter or longer) and **the month's pace** (`budget-pace`; over is the money itself, a rupee past the budget at any day of the month, never a pace held or Held; the fullest budget's share against the share of the month gone, from `BUDGET_PACE_MIN_DAYS` 7, ten points ahead is worth saying; no period, or a month or shorter, read as of its end, so a closed month says how it ended): spending in the person's own currency only (`InsightFacts.budgets`, `budgetSpend`), set against each budget as it is now, since the app keeps no history of one, and the basis says so. "Ran over", never "failed"; the tag is warm, never red. Water sits under Physical, budgets under Financial, and they reach Home, Finance, Life and Daily by rank like any card. Spending against kinds of day or mood was left out: daily spending is too lumpy to be honest beside `money-stress`.
- Plan my week is a sheet you work in, not only read (`components/WeekPlan.tsx`): each suggestion row has its day picker at the right; every line under "Worth a look" (`WeekPlan.attention`, the same lines as `notes` with the tasks each is about) has a tick for "dealt with" and a Fix that offers what the line is about right there (move a task off a heavy or late day, give a task a length from 15 minutes to an hour and a half, fill an empty day, place one that did not fit); the fixes are staged and one Keep applies the suggestions, the fixes and anything added, one `PATCH /nodes/:id` for each task, merged so a task is saved once. "Add your own" finds any open task by name (`GET /wellbeing/focus/tasks?q=`, twelve results; without `q` the nearest sixty), reads it (`GET /nodes/:id`, the person's own only) and opens the same `TaskPlanFields` form a task has on Daily: day, due day, repeat, priority, time, length, Remember, bring to review. Nothing is written before Keep (`node scripts/smoke/drive-plan.mjs` drives it). A Fix row keeps its day picker a fixed width (`w-[148px] shrink-0`) so the task title beside it keeps the rest of the line, never squeezed to nothing (`drive-plan.mjs` measures it). A Suggested row (2026-11-08, the founder: "put checkbox on top, put text below it left aligned") stacks while its list is under 32rem (a phone, `@container` on the list, `@lg` the switch): the box on top, the words under it left-aligned with it (the pillar's dot starts the title's line), then the day and block pickers side by side at 148px each; from 32rem (the sheet on a tablet or laptop) it is the one line it was, the dot hanging beside the words. The box stays 24px and its label's `::before` is the 44px target.
- Layout rules found by probing every screen: on Home's two-column desktop layout (`screen-even`) the two halves of a row are the same height, the taller one's, and their cards start on the same line, so every half-width section has one short line under its title that fits on one line; a section's action ("Open", "Wellbeing") is level with the title at the top of the block, never down by the line under it (`Section`; `node scripts/smoke/drive-headings.mjs shots.json` measures every heading and must report 0 not level); beside a link the line under the title takes the whole width under it, beside a button (anything `rounded-pill`, whose 44 to 48px reach past the one-line box) it keeps to the title's column, and with no line the heading keeps 8px more so the button never touches what follows; a hand-made header whose button is followed by rows keeps the button's row height (2026-11-08). Cards in a stretched list (Knowledge's reads, whose list on Home is `auto-rows-fr` at every width, so a three-line title never leaves one card taller on a phone, 2026-11-07) fill their cells, so the cards in one row are one height, on Home and on every other screen (a list cell that holds one `toy` card stretches it, `ul.grid > li:has(> .toy:only-child)` in app.css, and kanban lanes stretch to the tallest; a bento's spanning feature cell is the one meant exception; `node --experimental-strip-types scripts/smoke/shoot.mjs` with `SHOOT_PROBE=scripts/smoke/height-probe.js` over `shots.json` at phone, tablet and laptop width must report 0 rows of unequal cards); `node scripts/smoke/drive-home.mjs` checks equal pairs, a shared start line and filled cards at 1100, 1280 and 1600px. A half whose content is shorter than its pair centres it in the stretched card (the Companion card, `flex flex-col justify-center`) rather than leaving an empty band at the foot. Spacing and placement on the core screens (2026-11-07, the founder's "check redundant spacing ... and placement using design principles"): every titled block on Home sits a section's 32px from the one before (Breathe and focus's slot is `mt-lg lg:mt-md`, since a phone folds the panel's own 16px into it and the laptop grid adds them; the daily brief `mt-lg`, Getting started 16px under it as its pair; More on Home `mt-lg`); a card's own ways on sit at its foot's right (Up next's All events and Circles); a dot beside a line of words sits on the line's middle (`h-[1lh]` box, the brief); a pillar page's hero shares Screen's column and `--nav-w`, so its title, the guide's line and the tasks share one left edge with the sidebar open or folded, and its layer folds are one box each (no padded card round the fold's own row); a tall action beside a section title reaches past the title's row from the tablet up (`md:-my-xs`) rather than pushing the line under it down; stacked table cards keep 12px between them and the same columns (`table-fixed`, Plans' limits); and Your year's month names stand 8px over the first week.
- Calm, in the spirit of a quiet Mac app site (the 2026-10-31 pass, from trymonocle.app): the second half of a public headline is the quiet half (`.type-hook em` in `--color-ink-mute`, large type only), arrivals use `--ease-open` (`cubic-bezier(.16, 1, .3, 1)`, on every `toy`), the outline button is a hairline (`border-ink/30`) with semibold text, the public pages get wider gutters, and the card table and the Discover quiz dim the rest of the screen while the pointer or keyboard is inside them (`.focus-scope`, `.focus-target`, `.focus-dimmable`; mouse and keyboard only, never on a touch screen or under reduced motion).
- Motion kit (2026-11-02, from Kokonut UI and Skiper UI, leaning on Apple's Human Interface Guidelines; `components/fx/`, the block at the end of app.css; no package, no copied code, no Apple assets, fonts or symbols). Pieces: `Reveal` (staggered fade-and-lift once on scroll, not above the fold), `SpotlightCard` / `.fx-spot` (one delegated pointermove handler writes --mx and --my; pointer-fine only), `BorderBeam` (a masked hairline with a rotating conic square, now on the offer card only), `TextReveal` (hero words rise in, real text from the first paint, once), `MorphShapes` (four soft blurred shapes with uneven rounded corners that slowly turn, stretch and drift on GSAP yoyo timelines, transform only; with `page` one fixed layer at z-index -1 just over the page's wash, behind the whole of the public home and Welcome, so a hero is never a band of its own; with a mouse each shape leans toward the pointer by its own depth (quickTo on x and y, adding to the timelines' xPercent and yPercent) and a soft glow trails it, nothing on touch or under reduced motion; it replaced `BackgroundPaths`, whose lines the founder found did not work, 2026-11-03; the light-mode colours `--fx-morph-1` to `-4` stay at or just past the page glows so muted ink over them keeps 4.5:1, and since `contrast-probe.js` cannot see blurred layers a hero with it is checked by sampling pixels from shots; hidden under reduced transparency), `Magnet` (GSAP quickTo, the two main buttons on the site), `burstFrom` (twelve dots, removed when done, on Welcome's start button), `CountUp` (Home's scores and the site's live numbers), `CoverArt` (an app cover's art drawn in code: the app's own colour, a second pillar colour from its key, its icon large and soft and one flower; `ICON_NUDGE` moves an icon left where a title would meet it, as on Circles) inside `LiquidRipple` (one inline SVG turbulence and displacement filter: nothing at rest, a faint drift while on screen in a visible tab, a little more round a mouse; no filter under reduced motion; only the picture moves, never the words), `RulerPicker` (a swipeable ruler of tick marks for choosing a number from a range with a step: pointer drag that snaps, click a tick, arrow, Shift, Page and Home and End keys, `role="slider"`, `touch-action: pan-y`, a haptic tick and a very soft `tickSound()`, GSAP glide, the maths in shared `rulerTicks`, `rulerValueAt`, `rulerClamp`, `rulerIndex`; used on the Mindful panel and breathing length, a task's minutes, Sleep's hours, Plan my week's hours a day and the water goal and glass; it only changes how a number is chosen, never what is saved), `Segmented` and `useSlider` (a sliding thumb: the theme and view switches, the site header's page pill, the tab bar's circle and the sidebar's pill; a segment that can be picked answers a mouse, app-wide, with the pillar's tint at 12% and the full ink under the thumb's layer, `.fx-seg` in app.css, the founder's own exception to "never a new filled hover", 2026-11-12: "the tab selector doesnt show hover when clickable"; the chosen one, a touch and the look at rest unchanged, and the ink stays the label's own colour, so its contrast only rises), `CondensedTitle` (the large title shrinks to a small centred one on a blurred bar once it scrolls away; `PageHeader` and Apps), `haptics.tick` (a tiny buzz on a switch, a segment change or a sheet dismissed, only where `navigator.vibrate` exists and the person's own Vibrate setting is on), and Button `variant="shimmer"` (a sweep of light on hover or focus). Learned from wensity (ui.wensity.com, 2026-11-03), our own code from each piece's description and look, no package, no copied source: `FractalGlass` (fluted "fractal" glass, the founder's pick of 2026-11-05 from the Framer fractal glass component, replacing the old `GradientStripes`: deep navy `--fx-glass-base` in both themes, one glow of electric blue, violet and a white-lavender core rising from the top right, seen through narrow upright ribs, `--fg-rib` 18px and 14px on a phone, each a dark edge and a bright streak in overlay plus a thin crest masked to the glow; GSAP only slides the glow under the fixed ribs and leans it toward a mouse, paused off screen, still under reduced motion, solid navy under reduced transparency or more contrast; a scrim keeps the words' side dark, from the left on a laptop and from the top below 1024px; the card carries `fx-fglass-host` and `text-on-fill`, and its one main action is `SITE_PRIMARY_ON_GLASS`, white with the fixed slate ink; on the public home's last call to action and the Features close; `hue` gives each page's closing box its own colour from the pillar tokens over a deep base (the founder, 2026-11-05: navy on the home, teal on `/features`, wine on `/7rp`, whose close is now the same glass card; all three closes have the seven guides peeking up from the card's foot, `site/PeekGuides.tsx`, the founder 2026-11-06), white measured from pixels at 4.72:1 or better at 360 to 1600 in both themes, the quiet half of a heading about 3.2:1 at its worst on teal, above AA for display text), `LiquidMetal` (a chrome rim round a button: a conic metal square and a counter-turning sheen in the `--fx-metal-*` tokens, turning faster on hover, focus and press; the face of the button is untouched; on the hero's and the last call to action's Start free inside `Magnet`, and Welcome's start button; solid ring under reduced transparency or more contrast), `ActivityGrid` ("Your year" on Profile, lazy: a GitHub-style year of days, `ACTIVITY_DAYS` 371, `activityLevel`, `activityWeeks` and `activityRange` in shared, `GET /me/activity?day=` core and owner only, counts of xp or tasks ticked and never what for, in the person's zone; one tooltip glides by quickTo, arrow keys move by day and week, the cells ripple in once from today; shared's barrel never gets a runtime `export * from './x'` without an extension, since the smoke scripts load it with Node's strip-types), the `SearchBox` goo (behind the real field and panel, an aria-hidden layer of three `--card-strong` shapes merged by an inline SVG goo filter pours the panel out of the field on a GSAP expo.out and back on clearing; the real panel only fades; `data-goo` rest, opening, open, closing; none under reduced motion, reduced transparency or more contrast), `KanbanBoard`'s fluid drag (pointer events: a mouse lifts after 6px, a finger after holding 250ms so a swipe still scrolls; a dashed slot stays behind, an inert copy follows with quickTo and tilts with the speed, an allowed lane rings and opens a gap while its cards slide by FLIP, a drop glides in and the card waits at the gap until the new columns arrive; Escape, a refused lane or a drop outside glides it home; edge scroll only while dragging; the Move button and the keyboard are unchanged; no tilt, slide or glide under reduced motion) and the bento look (`BENTO_SIZE` large, medium, wide, tall and small in `lib/bento.ts`, Home's metrics as a large Life score beside two stacked cards from the laptop width up (its `Gauge` 240px with a 56px number there, 140px in the phone's row; Xp by source a 168px `Donut` with the fourteen-day total beside stacked `HBars`), the phone's sideways row unchanged; cells arrive once by `animation-timeline: view()` inside `@supports` and only without reduced motion, cells with their own `Reveal` left alone; a tinted outline on hover for a mouse, never a new shadow). Apple layer: semantic names over the existing tokens (`--label`, `--label-2`, `--label-3`, `--fill-1`, `--fill-2`, `--separator`, `--tint`), a rem type scale (`--text-*`, `.type-large`), `.toy` presses to 97% and springs back (`--ease-spring`), `.fx-bar` (the phone tab bar and the condensed title: a blurred, mostly opaque material with a specular top edge, solid under reduced transparency or more contrast), `.fx-inset` and `.fx-row` (an inset grouped list with hairlines and a press highlight, used on Apps; an `fx-row` lives only inside an `.fx-inset` list, a lone button row is a rounded `toy` tile), `Toggle` drawn as the iOS switch (52 by 32, a knob that springs across), and `Sheet` that scales in on a laptop and on a phone rises from the bottom and can be dragged down by its grab bar, always portalled to `document.body` (`createPortal`), so a sheet opened inside the sidebar's blur, a transformed card or another sheet is never framed or squeezed by it. Its Close and any small round icon actions sit in a rail outside the panel (2026-11-07, the founder: "move the close and settings and such icon menu items in popups on the right outside the card and they stay sticky", only for popups; `rail` on `Sheet`, `.dialog-frame`, `.dialog-rail` and `.dialog-panel` under "Popups" in app.css; `scripts/smoke/drive-popups.mjs` and `rail-probe.js`): the dialog node is a frame that animates and drags and holds the rail and the panel, and only the panel scrolls, fills and blurs, so the rail never moves with the words. One small gap off the panel's right edge, Close first, top-aligned, at every window from 37.5rem (a 600px sheet leaves 84px at 768, so nothing shifts or narrows); under that, a phone-width sheet, above the panel's top edge at the right, Close at the far right, in room the frame keeps for it (`padding-top`, the taps there reach the scrim), the panel held to `min(90dvh, 100dvh - 60px - the safe area)`. A sheet over a sheet covers the one under it with its own scrim (`~ .sheet-scrim`). Close keeps its look over the strong card; every rail button's name is a smart tip placed beside it, off the panel (over the scrim) when that side has room, never on the focus a popup gives its own Close as it opens. The assistant's rail is on the card's left on a laptop and above it on a phone (over where the apps menu sits), and the Reminders bubble's on its right; a word button (Start over, Sign out) or a full-width action stays in the popup, and a page's inline panels keep their own close. Rules: only transform and opacity move; mouse-only effects sit behind `(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)`; nothing endless runs off screen or in a hidden tab (`useRunning` sets `data-run`); reduced motion shows everything and moves nothing; glass turns solid with reduced transparency or more contrast; the header's fill stays above 88% opaque; a new third-party origin is not needed. Not done on purpose: hold-to-confirm (no natural place), an AI steps line, and the Profile and Features screens as inset lists (the classes are there for them).
