- Discover (`discover`, group Reflection, accent self, off on a new account, path `/discover`; `routes/discover.ts`, `screens/Discover.tsx`, migration 0041): a self-discovery quiz in five parts, built on the shape of a modern talent survey (a forced "most and least like you" pick from three statements, pattern puzzles, ten descriptor words with three most and three least, real situations with a most and least effective pick) with our own items and our own scoring, all in shared (`DISCOVER_*`, `scoreDiscover`). Eighteen groups of three statements (`DISCOVER_TRIADS`, each group from three different traits of the five: curiosity, follow-through, energy with people, warmth, calm), three word lists, a bank of thirty puzzles made by code from fixed seeds (`DISCOVER_BANK`: dice, turned arrows, 3 by 3 patterns, number series and odd one out, six at each of five levels, so there is no answer key to keep) of which a person sees twelve that adjust to them (`puzzleSequence`: start at level 3, up a level after a right answer and down after a wrong one, so the same answers always give the same twelve; changing an earlier pick clears the later ones), how long each took (kept as `puzzleMs`, a self-insight only), six situations keyed 1 to 4 by the team (`DISCOVER_SCENES`), and an optional emotional intelligence add-on after the core parts (`EQ_FEELINGS` four screens of five ratings, `EQ_READING` six scenes, `EQ_PEOPLE` eight situations; five parts: knowing your feelings, handling them, staying motivated, reading people, handling relationships; each part is half how you see yourself and half how you chose, `scoreEq`; it counts only when all of it is answered, and the quiz offers it at the end of the core parts and can end there). The score: a trait is the picks towards it shared by how often it was on offer, statements 60% and words 40%, centred on 50; puzzles are where the twelve settle, level 1 to 5 (the score is that level over the range), with the number right and the kinds as detail; situations are the key of your most-effective pick minus your least-effective pick over the best case; each of the seven strengths (one per relationship, `DISCOVER_STRENGTHS`: Getting it done, Connecting, Caring, Opening up, Knowing yourself, Learning, Finding meaning) is the weighted mix in `DISCOVER_WEIGHTS` (every row adds to 1). Nothing is compared with other people, and the screen says so. The answers are saved as the person goes (`PUT /discover/draft`, `parseDiscoverAnswers` drops anything unclean); `POST /discover/submit` scores on the server only, keeps the answers with the result (`discover_results`, so a changed algorithm never rewrites an old result) and clears the draft; limit `discover`. Owner only like the journal: never shown to brands, staff, the leaderboard or a coach, in the export, cleared by erase and delete. Two switches the owner controls (`discover_settings`, migration 0043, `PUT /discover/settings`): next steps (on by default) and the assistant (off until they say). Next steps: the daily brief's rules (`briefFor`, `BriefFacts.discover`) add the quiz when it is not taken and a step for the person's quietest strength, plus a step shaped by their traits (a low follow-through gets "finish only one task", low calm gets the breathing timer, high energy with people gets a people step, high curiosity gets a new thing), rules only and no model. The assistant: only a short `DiscoverProfile` (the top and quietest strength, the five trait bands and the reasoning level, never answers or scores) ever reaches it, through the `read_discover` tool and a paragraph in its system prompt (`profileForAssistant`, `styleHints`), only while the switch is on and only for a model that keeps nothing (`private`, like the journal), and the tool answers 403 when the switch is off. The result has up to five tabs: Strengths, Personality (a reading for each trait: low, middle or high, with at your best, watch for, with the people close to you, try this week), Reasoning (the level the puzzles settled at, the kinds, the two parts, the pace and a plain note that it is not an IQ score: a real IQ cannot come from twelve puzzles without norms), Emotional intelligence (only when taken: each part with how you see yourself against how you chose, the gap when it is 20 or more, and a reading) and Use my results (the switches). The setup chat's last step offers it beside the strategy session (`Take the Discover quiz`, which switches the app on first). The result shows the top three, all seven, the five traits, puzzles and situations, a use/watch/try line for each top strength and the quietest strength with a link to its pillar. The reference was the founder's own screen recordings of a commercial assessment (flow, section order, pick format and results layout); no item, answer key or scoring from it is used.
- Discover add-ons (migration 0050, `discover_extras`, `routes/discover.ts` under `/discover/more`, `components/DiscoverMore.tsx`; all in shared as `EXTRA_KINDS`, `TYPE_*`, `LOVE_*`, `MIND_*`): three self-checks under "More ways to know yourself" on Discover's front page, each taken alone in any order, saved as the person goes (`PUT /discover/more/:kind/draft`, `parseExtra` drops anything unclean), scored on the server only (`POST /discover/more/:kind/submit`, limit `discover`), one row per person and kind (a retake replaces it), owner only like the journal: never shown to brands, staff, the leaderboard or a coach, in the export (`discoverExtras`), cleared by erase and delete. **16 personality types** (named so people looking for Myers-Briggs or MBTI find it, and clear that it is a free check in that style and not the official test) is twenty-four lines on four screens rated from not like me to very like me, six to each pair (outward or inward, hands-on or big picture, head or heart, planned or open), half the lines leaning each way so agreeing with everything lands in the middle (`scoreType`: the share toward the first letter, a slight, fair or clear lean); sixteen own names (`TYPE_NAMES`, never the trademarked ones), a reading for each of eight letters, and the screen says it is a free self-check and not the official MBTI®. **Love languages** are twenty forced choices between two lines (`LOVE_PAIRS`, every pair of the five once, so each language shows four times and scores 0 to 4), ten for how you like to be shown love and ten for how you show it (`LOVE_LINES`, `lovePairLines`), with the gap between the two said plainly (`analyseLove`) and how to ask for each language and how to give it. **How your mind works** is twenty-four lines on how often something is true (never to very often) in three areas, attention, energy and impulse, and patterns and senses, banded few, some or many signs (`mindBand`: under 40, under 65, 65 and over); it is never a diagnosis and says so on the quiz and on the result (`MIND_NOTE`: not a test for ADHD or autism, only a trained professional can say), the assistant and the brief never name a condition, and when two areas read many or one scores 75 or more the result says a doctor or psychologist can talk it through. Learnings: only a short `ExtrasProfile` (the four letters, the top languages each way, and the three mind bands) ever leaves the table, never answers or scores. Type and love reach the assistant (`read_discover`, `learnedForAssistant`, only for a model that keeps nothing) while the assistant switch is on, and shape Home's next steps (`briefFor`: `type-*`, `love-give`, `love-ask`) while the steps switch is on. The mind part has its own switch, `discover_settings.mind`, off until the person says (`PUT /discover/settings`), and without it neither the assistant nor the brief sees the mind bands (`latestExtras(db, userId, allowMind)`); with it, the brief adds `mind-att`, `mind-ene` and `mind-pat` steps and the assistant gets pacing hints (`extraHints`: one step at a time, a short focus round, a movement break, say what is coming) in plain words. **Human design** (`design`, in the same table, one step: a form) takes the birth date (prefilled from the account), the birth time and the IANA zone of the birthplace (`DesignInput`, `parseDesignInput`, `designInputProblem`; births from 1900 on; `zonedToUtc` is the only zone conversion), and works out the chart on the server (`computeDesign`): the thirteen bodies (Sun, Earth, Moon, true nodes, Mercury to Pluto) at birth and at the design moment, the instant the Sun stood 88 degrees of longitude earlier (`designMoment`), in tropical longitudes with the Fagan-Bradley shift kept beside them (`faganBradleyAyanamsa`, 24.04 degrees at 1950 plus 50.26 arc seconds a year). The astronomy is our own and low-precision (Meeus for the Sun, the Moon and the node; the JPL approximate orbits for the planets, with nutation and precession), good to a few arc minutes for the Sun and Moon and about a tenth of a degree for the outer planets; a position nearer a line's edge than that is listed under the planet table (`edges`). The shared test pins it to published values (the two Meeus examples, the year 2000 planets, the 2020 Jupiter and Saturn meeting) and to the system's own claimed type shares over thousands of births (about 37 Generators, 33 Manifesting Generators, 9 Manifestors, 20 Projectors and 1 Reflector in 100). The sixty-four gates run round the wheel from 2 degrees of Aquarius (`GATE_WHEEL`, `gateAt`: a gate is 5.625 degrees and a line a sixth of that), each gate belongs to one of nine centers (`DESIGN_CENTERS`), thirty-six channels join them (`DESIGN_CHANNELS`), and `designChart(result, zodiac)` reads the chart in either zodiac: channels from both sides, defined and open centers, definition, type (motor to throat), authority (emotional, sacral, splenic, ego, self-projected, mental or lunar), profile and the cross's angle. The sidereal reading is labelled an experiment, since Human design is drawn in the tropical zodiac; `designCompare` says where the two agree. Each channel has our own plain meaning (`CHANNEL_MEANINGS` by `channelKey`, 36, one per `DESIGN_CHANNELS`: what it is, at your best, watch for, a small step; reflective, never predictive; the shared test checks it), and `gateSide` and `hangingGates` say which side holds a gate and which gates have no partner. The bodygraph (`BodyGraph` in DiscoverMore.tsx) draws every channel gate to gate as two thin parallel lines, each half in its own gate's side (conscious in ink, unconscious in `--color-r1`), hanging gates as short numbered stubs; under it on 3D, 5D and 7D (never Numerology) "Your channels" (`ChannelCards`) gives each a card (the gates, the two centers, a `StatusTag` for the side, its meaning in a Fold), and a card and its line pick each other (the rest dims); a chart with no channel lists "Gates without a partner" instead, and only then is a stub's badge pickable. `analyseDesign` reads it in three layers that are the app's own, grouped on its map of consciousness and said to be (`DESIGN_DEPTHS`): 3D body and day (type, authority, definition, defined and open centers), 5D mind and people (profile, channels, conscious and unconscious sides) and 7D purpose (the cross, nodes, Sun and Earth gates); and into goals (`DesignGoal`: a pillar, why, a first step and something to count) across the seven pillars, each with an "Add as a task" that lands under that pillar's project in Not scheduled yet. Human design is shown as a system for reflection, not science, and never predicts. The birth details stay on the person's own table; only the type, strategy, authority and profile (`DesignProfile`) can reach the assistant and Home's next steps (`design-strategy`), under the assistant and steps switches. **Numerology** is a fourth option beside 3D, 5D and 7D in the Human design reading (`DESIGN_DEPTHS` key `num`, `NumerologyPanel`), worked out from the birth date and an optional full birth name on the same form (`DesignInput.name`, letters only, up to 80); it needs no birth time and nothing is stored beyond that name. `computeNumerology` gives the life path (month, day and year each reduced alone, then added), birthday number, attitude (day plus month) and, with a name, expression (all letters), soul urge (vowels, with Y a vowel only when it is the word's only one), personality (consonants), maturity and the karmic lessons (digits no letter carries), on the Pythagorean table (A to I are 1 to 9, J to R 1 to 9, S to Z 1 to 8); `personalCycle` gives the personal year (turns over on 1 January), month and day. `numberValue` is the one reducer: 11, 22 and 33 are kept whole (with their base 2, 4 or 6), every sum on the way is kept (`chain`, so 19 reads 19, 10, 1), the turning point 10 has its own reading, and the karmic debts 13, 14, 16 and 19 are noticed whenever a sum passes through one (`KARMIC_DEBTS`). Every number from 1 to 9 and 10, 11, 22 and 33 has a reading (`NUMBER_READINGS`: name, line, gift, what to watch for, something to try, which of the five Discover traits it leans on and which pillar it calls on). `numerologyInsights` is what connects it to what the person already learned: for the life path, expression and soul urge it compares each lean with how the same trait reads in the main Discover result (agree, or a stretch, at most three of each, with a small step and an "Add as a task"), checks the life path's pillar against the top strengths and the quietest, combines the personal year with the Human design strategy, and with how they show love when the life path is a care number (2, 6 or 33); a person who has not taken the main quiz gets the Human design combination and an invitation. The assistant gets the life path and expression numbers only (`DesignProfile.lifePath`, `expression`), never the name or the date. Numerology is shown as a system for reflection, not science.
- The line of the day (`components/QuoteOfTheDay.tsx`, `lineForDay` in shared, shown at the very top of Home, above the night-slate header with the greeting, as a tip-coloured card with no point (2026-11-13, the founder; it sat under Water and reminders with a point aimed at it before), 64px down on a phone to clear the corner buttons, the header under it now a rounded card in the gutter on a phone too): one line a day, a philosopher and then a line written for the app, alternating, from about 130 philosopher lines (each with who said it; common English renderings) and about 240 lines written for the app, eye-openers and mind-benders in plain words, shown with no name under them. Every line is shown once before any comes back (`DAILY_LINE_COUNT` is more than a year of days), each round is shuffled anew with a seeded shuffle and never starts on the line the last round ended on, and the day is the person's own, so nothing is stored and the same day shows the same line to everyone. A round button on the card, "Another line" (2026-11-06, the founder), shows a new one on demand, taking turns between a tip (`TIP_LINES`, about forty small things to do for the seven relationships, the work and the day, in plain words, no claim) and a quote (a philosopher or one of the app's thoughts), never one this device has been shown until the whole pool has (`nextLine` and `lineByKey` in shared, a seeded order; a new round never opens on the last line); the card then says A tip or A line to think about, and a tip carries no quote marks. On a laptop the quote is always one row (the founder, 2026-11-06: "never make the quote more than one line on desktop"): from the `md` test up it does not wrap, the card measures its own width against a hidden copy of the text (`useWide`, a `ResizeObserver`, measured again once the font is in), and a line too long for the row gives way to the next that fits (`nextLine`'s optional `fits`), so the same day can show another line on a phone, where a long one may wrap; checked over 80 lines at 1024, 1280, 1600 and 1920px, none clipped. What was seen is kept on the device only (`kreators.lines`), and a new day opens on the day's own line. A new line is added to `PHILOSOPHER_LINES`, `APP_LINES` or `TIP_LINES` and nowhere else; the shared tests check that none repeats within a round, that the kinds alternate, that "Another line" never repeats until the pool is used and that the app's lines and tips avoid the words the app does not use.
A new add-on is added to `EXTRA_KINDS` with its own parse, score and analyse, and a line in Discover's How it works.
- Card games, the Games app (`cards`, group Connection, accent friendship, off on a new account, paths `/cards` and `/cards/:game`; `routes/cards.ts`, `screens/Cards.tsx`, `screens/CardTable.tsx`): the card text is in D1 (`card_games`, `card_decks` and `cards`, migration 0042, seeded once from the founder's print PDFs by `scripts/cards/extract.py`, `build.py` and `seed.py`; the applied migration is the source, like the School programs) and `GET /cards/:key` serves a game only to someone who unlocked it or to the team (`sub_admin` and up play every game to check the content); `GET /cards` lists every game with its decks and sizes and no text. The games are a three-column grid on a laptop (one column on a phone), on the Games page and on the Rewards shelf. A `PlayCard` has a front and, where the physical card had two sides (Truth and Dare, This and That, Roast and Flirt, a lesson and its idea), an `alt` side that the flip shows; a card with no second side flips to the game's cover. The table is freeform, in the spirit of a drag-and-drop flip-card kit: a pile per deck (shuffled in the browser), a tap on the pile draws (`placeCard` in CardTable.tsx: first a spot clear of every card and the pile with 8px between, highest up and nearest the middle across; when none is left a cascade a band and a title below a card already there and 12px across, never on any card's band or the pile; only a full table covers the least of anyone's band; a dealt card leaves the pile leaning left on one draw and right on the next, by its uid, 6 to 14 degrees by the distance, and swings just past level as it lands), a tap on a card flips it (GSAP `rotateY`, `gsap.set` under reduced motion), a drag moves it and the card you touch comes to the front, keys flip (Enter or Space) and move (arrows). A card's body is cut by its box with a fade, never a line clamp, and "Read all" shows whenever a ResizeObserver measures it cut (`useCut`) or a list runs past two, opening the whole card in a sheet. The games page (the 2026-11-04 redesign, read beside Mindful and Home) is titled Games: by the hero rule a plain header (`BackLink` to Apps, the title, one line), then an orange friendship `Hero` with the points to spend (`CountUp`) and "N of 9 games yours", a quiet link to Rewards, `Skeleton`s while loading, then the covers. `GameTile` in Cards.tsx (over `GameCover`) is the one cover, on the Games page and the Rewards shelf, with no emoji, laid out as the founder set it (2026-11-04; the sides put right 2026-11-07): the decks and cards as plain 12px text at the top left (never cut: it keeps its width) and Yours (a `StatusTag`) or Locked at the top right, then the name and its line, the game's character (`Avatar` 56px) at the bottom left and Play at the bottom right, or the price `FactTag` there on a game that is not yours; the tile is two rows of a subgrid (`row-span-2 grid-rows-subgrid`), so the covers in a row and the Unlock or Locked blocks under them line up; Play has a lucide Play icon that nudges forward once on hover (`.game-play-icon`, mouse only, still under reduced motion); a game that is yours is one `toy` link over the whole cover (`SpotlightCard`); one that is not wears a Locked `IconTag` (top right) and its price `FactTag` (bottom right), with an outline Unlock when it can be afforded (asked again in `ConfirmSpend`) or a `LockedButton`. Every number goes through `formatCount`. The table page opens with `PageHeader`, so its cover (back to Games, the name with `tie`, the tagline; hidden in focus mode) and carries `data-pillar` with the game's accent. The decks are a `TabRow` (every deck in its All list); the toolbar is one `fill-row`: Draw a card (the one solid button), then Shuffle, Flip all and Clear table as `ExpandButton`s, then Water and Focus mode at the right; the table's own cover shows the game's character, not its emoji; how many are left is a neutral `StatusTag` on the pile ("18 left") and a live line for screen readers. A deck's hint shows on the pile's cover only while it fits whole (the cover measures it, `onHint`), else as its own line beside the pile, never cut mid-sentence. The felt is glass tinted 14% with the pillar's fill (`color-mix`), not a flat colour. Moving a card follows the kanban's drag: a few pixels of slop and a short hold before it lifts on touch (the page still scrolls, no `touch-none`), a lift to 1.03 with a haptic tick, a lean from the pointer's speed (clamped to 8 degrees, `quickTo`) and a `power3.out` drop; a flip is `rotateY` with a small 1.04 swell; all `gsap.set` under reduced motion. A card's title is in the display face and its body in `ink-soft` medium. How to play is one Section with `Segmented` tabs labelled Rules (Basics, House rules, Special cards, a tab only when it has something); the rules paragraph is `prose-long` reading text. Card fills come from the tokens (`fillVar`, mixed toward `--fill-shade`; orange and yellow carry dark text). Migration 0072 adds cards to the games people have (FunFlow 12 a deck, HealFlow 8, 7RP Challenge 7 a pillar, WorkFlow 10 in two decks, DateFlow 8 with This and That), INSERTs only, after each deck's last card; ShadowFlow, DateGuru, Roast or Flirt and Ulta Seedha are unchanged. `node scripts/smoke/drive-cards.mjs` reports `covered`, `outside` (in focus mode, after leaving it and back), `clippedWithoutReadAll` and `decksHidden`, all 0 (`CARDS_ORIGIN`, `CARDS_API` point it at a Vite server).
- Apps: only school, work, army, journal, life, shop (plus profile and the core tabs) are on for a new account (reviews and store are off until switched on), and the setup chat then switches on the apps that fit and off the rest; Rewards is not an app; the Leaderboard is, in Essentials and on for everyone (see the Leaderboard line). The Apps page and Profile, Features both draw `components/AppGroups.tsx`: every group is one big accordion in its relationship colour (`MODULE_GROUP_ACCENT`) saying "N of M shown", and opening it lists the apps that are shown, the name opening the app and an outline Hide at its right; a hidden app leaves that list for a short, full-width "Hidden apps" `Fold` (its count beside it) at the end of the group, each inside with Show (the founder, 2026-11-06: hide and show, never an on and off switch; closed by default on Apps, open by default under Profile, Features), so hiding happens in both places and is the same per-person module switch underneath; on Apps the count line reads "N shown, M hidden" and, while an app is hidden, a round `EyeOff` button at its right (a `ToggleIcon`, the icon turning into a cross while open, its words as a tooltip) opens every hidden app in one inline panel under it (`HiddenAppsPanel` in AppGroups.tsx: each group's name in its colour over its hidden apps, each with Show), so an app comes back without opening its group. The eye on every row wears the round buttons' tooltip, Hide or Show (`.app-eye`, beside the eye and inside its row, since the list clips; its look at rest unchanged; 2026-11-07). **Select**, on Apps only (2026-11-07, the founder, who then moved its actions to the top and dropped a Select all per group; `useAppPicks` in `screens/Apps.tsx` is the one owner, `pick` on `AppGroups`): one row at the top, the words at the left in one `role=status` line (the count at rest, then "Tick the apps to hide or show.", "3 selected.", "2 apps hidden and 1 app is shown again." until the next tick, or "Could not save that. Nothing changed." with the ticks kept) and at the right, all outline `sm`, in this order: "Hide 2" and "Show 1" (each only while over 0), "Select all" (every app on the page, in every group, shown and hidden; reads Clear once all are ticked; `aria-pressed`) and Select, which reads Done (`aria-pressed`; Escape leaves too, unless a dialog is open). Every eye becomes `CheckMark` (the box `CheckRow` draws) in the same 44px spot, and a real checkbox named after the app covers the whole row, unseen, so a tap never opens the app and the keyboard's next stop scrolls the whole row clear of the sticky row; the row drops its link's arrow. At rest the round button sits left of Select, one `xs` gap apart, and Select at the far right (the founder's swap, 2026-11-07); it and its panel step aside while selecting and come back as they were, so an app is never listed twice, and Done is the same button in Select's exact spot, so on a laptop it never moves, entering, ticking or stuck, and neither does the list (measured at 1024 and 1280; under a 34rem column the row takes two lines and Done stays at the far right). The row and the search left the header for one wrapper with the list. While selecting the row is sticky 4px under the condensed title bar, under every sheet; its card (`.apps-row-stuck`, the strong card over the page's base, so opaque, with the matte finish) shows once the title's header has gone above that line, which is when the list reaches the row, since any sooner a card would cover the title's foot or push the row down. Under a 34rem column the words take a line and the buttons fill the next (`fill-row-all`). `html:has([data-apps-selecting])` raises `scroll-padding-top`, so the keyboard's next stop lands clear of the row. While selecting, each group and its Hidden apps fold are held in `Group`, never written to what they remember (`useKept`; `Fold` keeps its own state while a screen holds it): the folds open, Select all opens every group, a tap still opens or closes one, and all go back as they were after. One tap is one write (`PATCH /me/modules {changes}`, the whole list or a 400 and nothing, limit `prefs`; the old `{key, on}` still works): the cached account first (`showInCache`), put back on a refusal, read again after. The apps written lose their ticks and the others keep theirs, so Show 1 stays after Hide 2; focus moves to Done on purpose; selecting stays on. The buttons close up when one goes, so a tap on the row within `SAME_TAP_MS` (400) of Hide or Show counts as the same tap, or a double tap on Hide would land on Select all or on Show. Apps starts each group the way the person picked (by default Contribution and Essentials open, the rest closed, see below) and Profile, Features with them open; each remembers which were opened for the tab and the person (`useKept`, scope `appgroups:<user>:<apps|profile>`), forgotten on a reload, a sign-out or another device. How each group starts and the order of the apps inside it are the person's own, saved on the account (2026-11-04, migration 0074 `users.app_prefs_json`; `AppPrefs`, `parseAppPrefs` the one reader and `appPrefsProblem` the one check in shared, which refuses an unknown group, an app of another group or a repeat; `PATCH /me {appPrefs}`, the whole set, limit `prefs` only when the save carries it; on `GET /me` as `user.appPrefs`, in the export, cleared with the account, untouched by an erase): until they pick, Contribution and Essentials start open on Apps, in the laptop sidebar and in the phone menu (folds there too) and the rest closed (`GROUP_OPEN_DEFAULT`, `groupStartsOpen`), the group of the app you are in also opens, and Profile, Features opens every group. Profile, Features is where they pick: each group's header carries a "Starts open on Apps and in the menu" switch (its state in words) and a More menu with Reset order once the group has an order, and "Arrange apps" (outline) turns the rows into a drag list (`components/SortableList.tsx`: the KanbanBoard's pointer feel, a finger after a 250ms hold so a swipe still scrolls, Escape puts it back) with Move up and Move down; apps only move inside their group and groups never reorder. Every list then takes `orderInGroup`: the placed apps first in the person's order, the rest (and any app added later) in the default order. Enabled apps appear in the laptop sidebar under their category. Every app screen opens with `BackLink`.
- Sideways rows: a row of tabs or filter pills is `components/TabRow.tsx` (on a phone a line that scrolls sideways with All pinned at its right, see Icons and the HIG rules), never a bare scroll-row that cuts its last pill at a plain edge (Help's jumps and Word categories, the Agency tabs, Daily's Sort by, Inbox, Admin, the card table's decks, BadgeGallery, Channels, the Discover layers, 7rp, Explore and the rest; a row that only picks one value is a `FilterSelect` instead); a row of cards that scrolls sideways is `.scroll-row` (app.css): the scroll box bleeds 16px into the gutters and pads the same inside, so a `toy` that lifts and its shadow are never clipped. Any top margin goes on a wrapper, never on the row. On a laptop the sidebar (`components/TabBar.tsx`) folds to a 76px icon rail: `--nav-w` is 240px or 76px by `html[data-nav]`, kept on the device as `kreators.navMini`, and its width glides, still under reduced motion. A round bell link (`BellLink`, the theme button's look, to Inbox, with the unread count on it, so no Apps tab carries one; 2026-11-07, the founder; the same bell sits left of the theme button in a phone's top-right corner, so the phone's Apps tab dropped its count too) sits left of the theme button, and folded under it, above Home. Folded the sidebar shows only k with the dot, the theme, the bell, the five main tabs and Sign out as a door icon (`SignOut` `iconOnly`, which still asks first), with the names as tooltips and labels. The fold button sits outside the nav, a 36px round button (44px to tap) half over the sidebar's right edge, level with the logo while open and at the foot while folded (`.nav-fold`). Open, the role is a tag in its own pillar's soft colour (`RoleBadge` and the name) beside the theme button, and the logo has room above and beside it. `Screen`, the condensed title bar, the reminders bubble and the consent card follow `--nav-w`.
- Navigation: `Root` renders React Router's `ScrollRestoration`, so a new page starts at the top and Back returns to where you were, and `useLandOnHash` (`lib/useLandOnHash.ts`, wired once in `Root`; the landing is its own chunk, `lib/landOnHash.ts`, 2026-11-07) lands a hash on its element: after a tap, a redirect or the first load, never Back or Forward, it waits up to eight seconds for the `id` (or a word in a `data-land` list; a hidden element is never a target), opens it when it is folded (a closed `aria-expanded` toggle, one inside it marked `data-land-open`, or a `Fold` given an `id`), scrolls it under the title bar, moves focus to its toggle or heading (the ring still only for the keyboard: on a first load no key has been pressed, so `data-land-quiet` holds it back until a Tab moves on) and keeps going to that place while the page grows, until the person scrolls, taps or types (the scrollbar too: Chrome sends a pointerdown for it, and in any browser the window moving while the section stays where it is in the page, on a page no shorter, ends it). `BackLink` goes back one history entry when the tab has one (`window.history.state.idx > 0`) and only otherwise goes up to its `to`. A tab, a filter or a switch between two views keeps the page where it is: every URL change that only changes what is shown passes `preventScrollReset: true` (`useParamState` does it for all of them; the leaderboard, Run's tabs and the public Explore filters do it themselves), so the router never sends the person back to the top; only a new page starts at the top (`node scripts/smoke/drive-scroll.mjs shots.json` clicks every tab and toggle from the middle of every screen and must report 0). Screen state that should survive Back lives in the URL through `lib/useParamState.ts` (Community's category, sort and filter; the Store's category, stage and budget; Run's tab), replacing the entry rather than adding one. Writes made in one tick compose (`writeParams`): two setters in one handler start from the address as it is now and go out as one navigation, so a filter and its page reset never undo each other. **On this page** (2026-11-07, the founder, after Notion's page outline; `components/PageContents.tsx`, `.page-contents` in app.css, about 9 kB in its own chunk, mounted in `Root` only while `useWide`, so a phone never downloads it; no API, no module): one button named On this page, fixed at the right under the top bar (64px down on an app screen, 73px on the public site), a stack of 2px dashes, one per heading, longer for a page title and shorter for a deeper one, the one being read darker and the longest of its level (`--pc-dash`, 3:1 or better from pixels in both themes). A mouse opens a card to its left after 100ms and closes it 260ms after leaving (`--card-strong`, `rounded-tile`, `text-footnote`, at most 280px, one line a row with an ellipsis and the whole words in its title, the current one in `--pc-now` with no change of weight, 4.5:1 or better on every accent, scrolling inside itself and stopping above the assistant's button); pointing at a dash lights its row and a click on a dash or a row goes there, read from where the pointer is on the one button, so there is no row of tiny targets. Enter or a tap opens the card instead: it draws into the page's own `[data-contents-slot]` just before `main` (`Screen`, `SiteLayout`), so Tab meets it right after the skip link (the second stop on an app screen, the eighth on the site), Tab goes on to its rows, a keyboard row also moves focus to the heading (`tabindex` -1 only while it stands there, so a mouse never draws a ring), Escape hands focus back, a tap outside or a page change closes it. The row being read is the last heading up to the line, a line that slides to the window's foot over the page's last screen so each heading still gets its turn, and the first at the very top; what a row or a dash asked for stays lit until the person scrolls on, however near the end it sits. The card never scrolls under a mouse on its rows. The first row goes back to the top of the page. Going there is `scrollToTarget` and `topBarHeight` in lib/landOnHash.ts (the condensed bar, or the site's header where it is sticky, which a hash on the site now clears too), once more when the window stops, since a picture or a late section above can move the heading on the way (`scrollend`, within `LAND_MS`, unless the person took over or the window is elsewhere); the address and the history never change. Headings come from the DOM (`headingsIn`), h1 to h3 in `main`, really visible: never in a list item (unless it says `data-contents-section`, as the books on `/book` and `/features/books` do), a sideways row, a sheet, `nav`, `footer`, the search answers or anything hidden, nor a title on a row of cards (an h3 beside one card like it, an h2 beside two); past 20 rows the h3s go, so an agreement lists its numbered sections; a heading with more in it than its name says it in `data-contents` (How it works); the same name twice running is one row; fewer than two rows shows nothing, and neither does a page that cannot scroll (the founder, 2026-11-07, over his "on each page": `MIN_SCROLL`, 64px of room, read whenever the page or the window changes size, so a page that grows long gets them). It needs 76px free at the right of the page's column, measured (`ROOM`: the button's 44 and the 32 a band may grow into the gutter), and a guide standing in that margin counts by itself, so an app screen starts at 1160px wide, a public page at 1104px (from 1280px where a guide stands at the right, since those reach further from 1200px), a reading page at 808px and `/admin` at 1288px; nothing on a tablet's app screen, whose gutter is 32px, and no page was pushed to make room. Never on `/onboarding`, `/session`, `/vibe-check`, `/start` or a card table (`NEVER`), in focus mode (so not over Mindfulness's full-screen cover), while the assistant's panel is open, or on print; `z-index` 25, under a sheet's scrim. One pass at most every 200ms from one MutationObserver on `#root` (childList and a heading's own words, skipping `[data-rect-quiet]`, which it wears itself, so a ticking clock never starts one) and on a resize; the current one from one passive scroll listener at most once a frame. `node scripts/smoke/drive-contents.mjs` opens pages at laptop width, walks each down to see whether a word or a control ever sits under the button, hovers, clicks a row and a dash, uses the keyboard and a touch screen, reads where the heading landed, samples the dashes from pixels and checks the places it stays away from, and must print 0 problems (`CONTENTS_ROOM_ONLY=1`, `CONTENTS_WIDTHS` and `CONTENTS_PAGES` run only the walk over a long list). Finance has no invoice button or list any more (the API and public invoice pages stay); its two main actions, Money in and Money out, are solid and full width, and Owed, Monthly, Goal and Budgets sit under them as outline buttons.
- Layout: every screen is one full-width column on a laptop (`Screen` defaults to `columns={false}`); only Home flows into two, from `lg` (2026-11-07: a tablet's column beside the open sidebar is 464px, so there Home stays one column like a phone). From `lg` the column keeps the sidebar's width plus one `lg` gap on its left (`Screen`), so words never touch the sidebar. A new route starts at the top (`Root` in App.tsx; a hash still lands on its element). In a row of equal choices no button is solid until it is picked. `SearchBox` lights its border on focus, white (`onDark`) on a dark header. The `md` and `lg` variants are redefined in app.css to need height as well as width (`@custom-variant`), so a phone on its side keeps the phone layout; the raw media query for `.screen-columns` carries the same height. Beside the sidebar the column is `md:w-auto`, never full width shifted right. On phones the bottom-right corner holds the assistant's button, above the tab bar, the top-left corner holds the kreators.app logo (to Home), and the top-right corner holds three buttons, the Inbox bell with its unread count (`BellLink`), the theme and the apps menu, in that order (`TabBar`, a `Sheet` listing the sidebar's groups); these top buttons are `absolute`, not `fixed`, so they sit at the top of the page and scroll away with it, and the public site's header is sticky only from the tablet width up (`md:sticky`); a rule in app.css starts the first `header` of every `.screen` 64px down on phones to clear them. The whole-page chats (setup chat and strategy session) keep their own slim sticky header, which is the chat's controls, not site navigation, and Home's header puts My page on its own first row on a phone (Home carries no bell of its own since 2026-11-07, the founder: the top bar's and the sidebar's `BellLink` is the one bell, so there is never a second with the same count).
- Interests come from `INTEREST_GROUPS` (grouped the way a dating app does, for everyone, not only creators), at most `MAX_INTERESTS` (10) on at once, always picked with `InterestPicker`.
- School content: the two real programs (Editing fundamentals, Skills using ChatCut and AI) are seeded by migration `0016_school_programs.sql`, generated once by a scratchpad script that did not survive the move to a new machine, so the migration itself is now the source; their lessons are rewritten from the skill files in `apps/web/public/resources` and each lesson links the file it came from (`content_ref` under `/resources/`). Lesson bodies are the small markdown `components/Markdown.tsx` renders (`##`, `###`, `-`, `1.`, `>`, `**bold**`). The teaching voice: a hook, a promise ("In this lesson you'll learn"), short second-person sections, bold-lead bullets, a "Pro tip" and a "Result", then "The bottom line". Three more programs are the founder's books (migration 0078 `book_courses`, ids `prog-book-relationship-method`, `prog-book-dom-creators` and `prog-book-dom-20s`, generated by `scripts/books/build-courses.mjs` from `scripts/books/courses/*.md`, safe to run again): in the same voice from the books' own text, each lesson names the chapter it comes from, and anything it adds is labelled as the app's own. Never seed placeholder programs; the smoke test's program is called "Smoke test program". On screen (2026-11-06): a lesson's foot is its ways in, full width: a file is "Download resource" (our own `/resources/` files download, a file elsewhere opens), a YouTube or Vimeo link plays in the lesson with "Watch video" (its tag then reads Video), another page is "Open resource", and a lesson from one of the founder's books with none is "The book", opening its place on `/book`. **Several resources** (2026-11-07, the founder: "for link of the book in lessons, add ability to select multiple resources like youtube or book or others"; migration 0113 `lesson_resources`, the subpath `@kreators/shared/lessonResources`, chunk `lesson-resources`, `ResourcesEditor` in `screens/Cohort.tsx`): a lesson holds up to `MAX_LESSON_RESOURCES` (6) in an order, each a kind (`LESSON_RESOURCE_KINDS`: video, book, file, link, named by `LESSON_RESOURCE_KIND_META`) with what it needs (a link that `videoEmbed` plays; one of `BOOKS` by its key, through the no-prototype `BOOK_BY_SLUG`; https or `/resources/`), never the same kind and link twice, and an optional label (`LESSON_RESOURCE_LABEL_MAX` 60; else `lessonResourceLabel`: the kind's words, numbered when several of a kind have none, and a book its own title). `parseLessonResources` is the one reader of what is sent; `storedLessonResources` reads rows for the screen, so a link saved before the https rule keeps working, a row marked a video that does not play reads by `lessonResourceKindOf` (the rule moved from the screen), and anything else that is not a link is dropped. The migration (with 0115 for a link written as `www.…`, `//…` or `resources/…`) copied every lesson's `content_ref` across as its first resource, of the kind it read as (checked against `lessonResourceKindOf` on 58 links, 0 differ); `content_ref` stays only as a copy of the first link, rewritten in the same batch as every change (`keepFirstLink`), `contentRef` on the answer and on `PATCH /school/items/:id` and `POST /school/programs/:id/items` are kept for a tab of the older app. The courses' `--sync` never touches `lesson_resources` (checked on all three). One resource looks as a lesson always did; several list full width, the first with the pictures button at its right, each leading with its kind's icon, one caption under the last, and every video plays in the lesson behind its own `EmbedGate` (`quiet` on all but the first, so one Play is solid; any video makes the tag Video). Whoever may edit a program (`accessTo().edit` in routes/school.ts: its creator, a manager of one of its cohorts, an admin, or the team with the cohorts permission on a public program only) changes them in place, never in a sheet: "Add a resource" (a `Fold` with `adds`: Kind, then Link or Which book, Label, Save; on a book's lesson with none it says The book goes once it has a resource) and "Change resources" with a row for each (arrows, Remove on a second tap, Change opening the same form under the row), through `POST /school/items/:id/resources`, `PATCH` and `DELETE …/:resourceId` and `POST …/:resourceId/move` (limit `prefs`, each write audited `lesson.resource`, the most and the no-repeat rule checked again inside the insert); the same rule covers a lesson's title, kind, words, order, adding and removing; someone both learning in and running a cohort sees their own hand-in first and the others under "Hand-ins to review" (never their own; the server refuses reviewing your own); the program page's title is the program's name with the cohort's in the eyebrow, a learner who is not in the cohort gets one card and "See the programs" (never a blind enrol), and the page shows a skeleton while it loads; on School the team's tools are one More menu at a card's foot, enrolling in a program priced in points asks in `ConfirmSpend`, and `GET /school` lists by state (public, private, draft, archived), then by `PROGRAM_ORDER` (placed programs by `sort_position`, the rest newest first). **States** (migration 0086): a program is Draft, Private, Public or Archived (`programs.visibility`, read through `VIS`; `published` is kept in step). Private is invite only (`program_invites`): hidden from lists, search and Explore. Archived takes no new enrolments and is the only state a program can be deleted from (`DELETE /school/programs/:id`, by its creator or an admin). Only the creator or an admin changes the state, audited `program.visibility`; an admin sees every program with `?view=every`. Shop courses (migration 0087) keep `live`, shown as Public, and add `private`, invite only through `product_invites`; their page and checkout answer 404 to anyone else. **The Admin guide** is `prog-admin-guide` (migration 0088, built by `scripts/admin-guide/build.mjs`): 25 lessons, Private with no invitees and no creator, so admins only, opened from Admin's cover at `/admin/guide`; the server refuses to make it public (`ADMIN_GUIDE_STAYS`). **Onboarding into kreators.app** is `prog-onboarding` (migration 0096, built by `scripts/onboarding-guide/build.mjs`): public, free, no manager, 20 lessons, `sort_position` 0, with the cohort `coh-onboarding` "Open all year" (2026-01-01 to 2099-12-31). A lesson changes through its file and one new migration (`build.mjs --sync <file>`, `--order` for onboarding when one is added or moved; 0110 and 0111 are the 2026-11-07 ones, and 0122 and 0123 (`--only`) carry the lessons changed after those two were committed, since a committed migration that may have run is never edited again; 0126, 2026-11-10, carries invites and the waitlist, Connect people, the Invites tab, Dev Tools' counts, the two new badge sets and their pay; 0128 wave 26's staff reach; 0130 what was settled after it, the referral's three days of use, who a connection pays, staff by place and rank; 0141 and 0142 the second taps; 0145 (onboarding 07, 13, 15, 16, 19 and 20) and 0146 (the Admin guide's 06, 11, 21, 23, 24 and 25), 2026-11-13, what a coach and a super admin may now read as whole tables, the Tables action in People and All tables among the Essentials), never by regenerating 0088 or 0096; a new lesson keeps its number and sits by `AFTER`, and `docs/content-placement.md` says where each change is told. **Order** (migration 0095 `programs.sort_position`): an admin moves a program with `POST /school/programs/:id/move {to: first, up, down}` inside its own state, which numbers every program again from 1, audited `program.order`. **Editing in place**: `PATCH /school/programs/:id {name, description}` (audited `program.edit`) and `PATCH /school/items/:id {moduleTitle, contentType, body, contentRef}`, checked by `lessonBodyProblem`, audited `lesson.edit` and `lesson.resource`; `POST /school/items/:id/move` is audited `lesson.order`; `DELETE /school/items/:id` asks first in a sheet and is audited `lesson.delete`. All of these are in `ROUTE_LIMITS` (`prefs`; adding a lesson uses `create`). A cohort that `cohortAlwaysOpen` reads "Always open" on School's cards and the Cohort header. Whoever opens a cohort runs it; the team, and the program's own maker on their own program in any state, can name someone else (2026-11-10, `setCohortManager`, the cohort page's Who runs it and Connect people's Cohort manager; see Admin, People). A program's invites follow its state's rule, its maker or an admin (`mayManageProgram`), which Connect people asks too.
- Roles: `ROLE_META` gives each role a badge (`RoleBadge`) that sits on the profile picture and, in the open sidebar, beside the theme button as a tag in its pillar's soft colour with the name (the folded rail shows none); earned badges never go on the picture or the sidebar. The badge reads `badgeRoleOf(user)` over `BADGE_META`: Creator, Staff and Admin, plus Life coach (🎯) for a creator who holds the `coach` permission; staff and admins who coach keep their own badge, and the name beside the badge says the same everywhere (sidebar, Profile, Admin's Life coach column). Dev Tools (the app's name since 2026-11-07, the founder; `/internal/observability`, the key and the code keep the old word, and older notes here call it Observability; in the Operations group) exists only for staff and admins by `minRole`; the API behind `/api/internal` checks the role on every call, only an admin changes a role, never their own, and every change is audited. The journal, gratitude, wellbeing (mood, sleep, stress, mindful minutes), Fitness (movement, eating, protein), supplements, Discover's answers, the Companion and the assistant's words never appear there, not even as a count. The Build map is shown there as data, in the page, not as an HTML file in another tab: `apps/web/src/lib/buildMap.ts` holds every section, card and item with its state (built and checked, needs a key, test mode or checked by hand, not built), and `components/BuildMap.tsx` draws the totals, the tower, the cards with their rings and, in a closed "Files and checks" fold, each card's `files` and `checks`; edit a state in that file and the page follows. `ok` means a script checks it (2026-11-09): `scripts/check-buildmap.mjs` (run by `scan.sh`) fails when a named file, smoke section (`smoke: <name>`) or script is missing, a route a card names is not in App.tsx, or a card with a built item names no check. `docs/build-map.html` is gone (its content was moved there). Dev Tools also counts, aggregated and nameless (`ObservedAdditions`, one round trip with batchedReads): games unlocked, Discover's allowed counts, Agency with its team, coach links by state with invite links and those the team made, programs by state, lesson resources by kind, cohort talk lines, open bottlenecks by kind, own time blocks, people who came through an event and are not set up, friend codes made new, the connections the team made by kind and what super admins gave by hand (from the audit log), whether Invite only is on with codes by state, joins through a code this week and the waitlist by status (2026-11-10), and the last day of the log. The log lives on its own page, Logs (`/internal/logs`, `routes/internal.ts`, `logs.ts`, migration 0046 `app_logs`): a line for every change anyone makes (any request that is not a plain read), every refusal (4xx, rate limits, failed sign-ins), every server error with its message, every cron run that did something and every error a browser reports (`POST /public/client-error`, limit `clientError`), with the area, the cleaned path (ids replaced), the status, the time it took and who did it; never a body or a query string, and nothing from the journal, wellbeing, Fitness (and Kreators Run's `/run/move`), Companion, Discover or the assistant but a bare server-error line with no person and no path (`scripts/check-log-areas.mjs`, run by `scan.sh`, fails when a route file is mounted nowhere or one of those files is mounted outside a private area, 2026-11-09). Logs and Dev Tools keep what is on screen while a filter, a search or a page loads (`placeholderData: keepPreviousData`, 2026-11-09; before, the whole page left, so a search lost its field after one letter and a page turn went to the top). Only a super admin (role `admin`) reads it, since it names people; lines go after thirty days (the cron prunes) and a deleted account's name is wiped from them.
- Privacy on the public page: `PublicSettings` (one switch for the whole page, then social icons, links, portfolio, products, posts, activity, badges, friend requests, chats) is the owner's choice and the server enforces it in `pageFor`, `POST /bond/friends` and `POST /bond/chats`. Journal and wellbeing never appear on a page. A new part on the page gets its own switch.
- Links page: the public page is the door into the app, like a Linktree. Blocks live in `LinkBlock` (`users.links_json`, migration 0017): link (plain or featured), header, text, image, video (YouTube only, embedded from youtube-nocookie), carousel (up to `MAX_CAROUSEL_ITEMS` cards), at most `MAX_LINK_BLOCKS`; `parseLinkBlocks` in shared is the one validator (https only, bounded text) and runs on save and on read. Taps are page events with `ref = block:<id>`, counted for the owner only at `GET /me/link-clicks`. Edited with `components/LinksEditor.tsx` under Public page; rendered with `components/LinkBlocks.tsx`. Every public page ends with the "Made with kreators.app" footer that links to the site home. The page (wave 19, `screens/Public.tsx`) opens with the person's cover in the `AppCover` look (the way back at the top left, the trust ring and Pro at the top right, their picture, name and @handle at the bottom right with their line under it, the follow and friend actions on that line's left), its link blocks are calm glass cards (a featured one larger, with a star and a soft wash), its badges take `bento(i, n, 2, 3)`, and every switch in `PublicSettings` holds exactly as before. Sidebar groups start as the person picked (Contribution and Essentials open until then, see Apps), and the group of the app you are inside opens too.
- Fitness (`fitness`, group Reflection, accent self, off on a new account, path `/fitness`; 2026-11-07, `routes/fitness.ts`, `apps/api/src/fitness.ts`, `screens/Fitness.tsx`, `components/MoveForm.tsx`, `EatForm.tsx`, `RunMoveTool.tsx`; the model is the subpath export `@kreators/shared/fitness`, a leaf in its own chunk `shared-fitness`): movement and a daily eating check-in, both rows of `wellbeing_logs`, so no new table. **Movement** is the `move` kind: minutes in `value` (1 to `MOVE_MINUTES_MAX` 600, a ruler of 5 to 360), and in `extra` the kind (`MOVE_KINDS`: run, walk, cycle, swim, yoga, gym, sport, other, `MOVE_KIND_META` with an icon key), metres for a kind with a distance (typed in km and read with `typedMetres`, never a float, up to 300 km) and effort 1 to 5 (`EFFORT_LEVELS`); `parseMove` is the one reader (also on `POST /wellbeing/move`, Life's quick log and the assistant, which now keep the same day rule), a club entry's own log (`source: 'run'`) reads as a club run that can be removed and never changed. **Eating** is the new `eat` kind (`LogKind`), one a day (the route replaces the day in one batch, and the unique partial index of migration 0103 holds it), made holistic the same day (the founder: "track protein and fruits and the basic items like how many meals, were they healthy or tasty or both"; the design brief's sources are in docs/DECISIONS.md): one card of about thirty seconds, every part optional, that fills from the day's check-in like Sleep and Stress. Always shown: Meals today as chips (`EAT_MOMENTS`: Breakfast, Lunch, Evening snack or chai, Dinner, Something else, each once and in that order, looked up in a `byKey` object) and a Fasting today chip that clears them and is cleared by them; under each ticked meal two chips, Good fuel and Tasty (`mealLabel`: both lit reads Both, neither reads Just ate, never a grade); then Fruit (0 to `EAT_FRUIT_MAX` 5) and Vegetables and greens (0 to `EAT_VEG_MAX` 8) steppers with "A serving is about a fistful or a katori" and `fruitVegLine` ("Fruit and veg: 3 of 5", five a soft mark, never a red fail). In a closed `Fold` More: packaged, fried or sweet (`EAT_PACKAGED` None, Some, A lot), after meals I mostly felt (`EAT_FULLNESS`, never scored), meals without a screen (capped at the meals ticked) and the note. With nothing logged that day an outline "Same as yesterday" ("Same as last time" when the last one is older) copies the last check-in into the form, without its note, for the person to check; it never saves by itself. `parseEat` is the one reader (an empty check-in is refused: "Tick a meal, or Fasting today, first."; `fruit`, `veg` and `screenFree` are null when not said, apart from a real 0: a stepper reads Not said until it is touched, with its minus dimmed as it always was at rest, one brought back to 0 is a said 0, and one more minus takes that back; the form and the assistant's `log_eating` send only what was said); the answers live in `extra` (`eatExtra`) and `value` holds the day's 1 to 5 from the one map `eatHowWell` over `eatDayScore` (0 to 12 is 1 up to 88 and over is 5), so Reviews' `eatAvg`, Emerging's `eat-days` (4 or 5 is a day you ate well) and a coach's eating number read it as before; a fasting day stores `EAT_FASTING_VALUE` (0), has no score (`dayScore` and `howWell` null) and is left out of every average and pattern (`AND NOT (kind = 'eat' AND value < 1)` in reviews.ts and insights.ts, filtered in life.ts and coaching.ts), so it is never read as a poor day, and so does a check-in with no fruit or vegetables answered or with too little said (below), so a few taps are never 5 of 5 or 1 of 5; Reviews' tile, Life and the dashboard then read "no score yet", never nothing logged (the form's Saved line says "Say your fruit and vegetables, even 0, and this day gets a score." or "Tick a meal and this day gets a score."; 2026-11-07, the founder: the score ignores what was not answered, but not fruit and veg). `eatDayScore` is the one 0 to 100: fruit and vegetables together out of five (weight 40), packaged, fried or sweet (20; none 1, some 0.5, a lot 0) and the share of meals without a screen (15), about 53, 27 and 20 of 100 with all three said, a part not said left out and its weight spread over the rest; a score needs fruit or vegetables answered (a said 0 counts; the founder, 2026-11-07), and then a meal ticked or a second of the three (`EAT_SCORE_MIN_PARTS`), so the ordinary card gets one, a lone tick never is a grade, and lunch, None and a screen-free meal with no fruit or veg said have none; a stored row that holds a reading from before this keeps its score (`eatFromStored` reads `value`), is never rewritten, and takes the rule of the day only when saved again; Tasty, Good fuel, fullness and protein never move it. `eatFromStored` reads every row back kindly, an older one (how well 1 to 5, fruit and vegetables together, counts out of three) too, marked `legacy`, by the same rule; a row with a protein mark on its meals (saved before 2026-11-07's change) keeps its score by the old weights (protein at meals 25) and is never rewritten, its marks no longer shown. **Protein** (2026-11-07, the founder: "protien calculator should be also there not rating right?"; `components/EatProtein.tsx`, `PROTEIN_FOODS` and `PROTEIN_FOOD_BY_KEY` in the fitness leaf, the sources in the comment there and in docs/DECISIONS.md): its own number inside the eating card, added up by what was eaten and never weighed. One closed `Fold` holds it all (the founder, 2026-11-07, the card at 390 from 987 to 587 px): the row reads "Protein 41 g of 60 g today", a polite live region readable while closed, or a quiet Optional with nothing added, and folded on every day; the card holds its state, so Same as yesterday opens it when it brought protein. Taking a food's last serving off moves focus to its chip inside this card (a day's sheet on Fitness draws a second card), or to More foods when that fold was closed by hand. Open, six foods first (dal, egg, curd, milk, paneer, chicken) and nine in a `More foods` `Fold` with grams typed from a label, every amount whole grams and worded about; a tap adds one serving, a list under them has minus and plus, a scoop's grams can be changed (kept per day, offered again next time). `parseProtein` is the one reader, `proteinGrams` the one sum (up to `PROTEIN_DAY_MAX` 400, refused out loud past it), kept in the `eat` row's `extra` beside the rest; protein alone can be saved, a body with no `protein` key (the assistant, an older tab) keeps the day's, and the form's null clears it. The form also sends the row it read (`ifId`, null for none), so a tab left open never writes over what another tab or the phone saved since: guarded inside the batch, it answers 409 `changed`, and the form reads the day again and says "Changed somewhere else. Reloaded." A page that reads the day again by itself (a refocus past the thirty seconds) takes the other device's row too, and says the same line when the form held words not yet saved. No `ifId` (the assistant, an older tab) replaces the day as before. `PUT /fitness/protein-goal` needs `goal` in the body (null takes it off), so an empty body clears nothing. "42 g of 60 g today" with a plain bar shows only once something is added, never a fail, and `Protein, last 7 days` (`PillBars`) only once a day has any. The goal is `fitness_prefs.protein_goal_g` (migration 0119, nullable, 10 to 300; `PUT /fitness/protein-goal`, limit `prefs`; on `GET /fitness`, in the export, gone with the account, kept through an erase), set in a `Daily goal` `Fold`: what you weigh is typed once (`typedKilos`), held only in that fold's state and never sent, stored or logged, and `proteinGoalsFor` offers ICMR-NIN's two figures (0.83 g a kg, and 1 g a kg when most meals are rice or roti) to take or change; `PROTEIN_CAUTION` sits beside it. Grams and goal stay on Fitness: no coach (not even under Reflection; a day's grams reach one only in the Eating table, when its owner switches it on for that coach under All tables, and the goal never), Emerging, Reviews, the brief, the leaderboard, a badge, search, the assistant or the public page (a shared test reads those files, and smoke reads the answers); the assistant neither reads nor writes them (its one door, 2026-11-14: `read_table`, when the person switches the Eating table on with Show to AI chat, on a model that keeps nothing, reads a day's grams as a cell; never the goal). What How it works teaches instead of the form: the half plate, five a day, protein from food at each meal (dal with rice or roti counts), the label check; never "good or bad food", clean, cheat, junk, guilt or unhealthy, never calories, BMI, alcohol or photos (grams of protein are the one amount counted, and a weight is typed once for the goal and never saved, the founder, 2026-11-07), never a claim about what protein does, and water stays the water card's. **Supplements** (2026-11-07, the founder: "a toggle that tracks supplement intakes and or shows a list of supplements to take, like vitamins, minerals, nootropics, those can also be a toggle in daily"; migration 0104 `fitness_prefs`, `supplements`, `supplement_days`; `routes/supplements.ts` mounted at `/fitness/supplements`, so the Fitness gate refuses it too; `components/Supplements.tsx` on Fitness, `components/SupplementsToday.tsx` on Daily, its own lazy chunk): a section on Fitness (`#supplements`, after How you ate) hidden behind a `Toggle` Track supplements, off by default, whose line "Your list stays private. Only you see it, unless you share its table with a life coach or AI chat." makes it the consent; stored on the account (`PUT /fitness/supplements/settings`, `parseSupplementSettings`), never in localStorage. The person's own list, up to `MAX_SUPPLEMENTS` (12, checked inside the insert): a name as on the label (1 to 40), a kind (`SUPPLEMENT_KINDS`: Vitamin, Mineral, Protein, Omega-3 or fish oil, Herb or Ayurvedic, Probiotic, Nootropic, Other, each with an icon key), an amount as typed (up to 24, never read, added up or compared), when (`SUPPLEMENT_TIMES`: Morning, With a meal, Evening, Night), the days (the custom-reminder day pills, every day by default), a place and paused; `parseSupplement` is the one reader (kind and time in `byKey` objects). No starter list, no autocomplete, no picks from goals, Discover, setup or the brief, no Store or partner link. Each row is a `CheckRow` grouped by its time (ticked is Taken, untouched is not marked: no skipped, no catch-up, no "you missed"), with "Taken on 5 of 7 days" and `WeekDots` for a doctor's visit on Fitness only, a quiet "Mark all taken" per time (`POST /fitness/supplements/day`), a More menu (Change opens the form in place, Pause or Resume, Move up and down, Remove, a second tap in the menu ("Tap again to remove it and its ticks", with "Pause keeps it instead" beside it; 2026-11-13)); adding is a `Fold` with `adds`; the list's More menu holds Copy my list (`copyPlain`, `supplementListText`: name, amount as typed and when, for a doctor or pharmacist) and Remove the whole list (asked in a sheet). A tick is today or yesterday only (`logDay`, `dateNearOwnDay`). "Taken on N of M days" and the review's count read one rule, `supplementOnList` in `apps/api/src/fitness.ts`: from the day it was added in the person's zone, never while paused or in a stretch it was paused and came back from (`pauses_json`, migration 0115), `INSERT OR IGNORE` on `UNIQUE (supplement_id, day)` so two taps make one row, and unticking deletes it. The caution lines are word for word: `SUPPLEMENT_CAUTION` under the list and in the form ("The app does not check amounts or mixes. Ask a doctor or pharmacist before you start, stop or change one, more so if you take medicines or are pregnant."), a "Before you add one" `Fold` with `SUPPLEMENT_BEFORE` (natural does not mean safe; follow the label or your doctor, more is not better; show this list to your doctor or pharmacist), and `SUPPLEMENT_NOOTROPIC_LINE` once Nootropic is picked; the only words about any supplement are what the person typed. Show on Daily (a second `Toggle`, off, offered once the list has an item): one closed `Fold` Supplements with "N of M today" right after Habits (`GET /fitness/supplements/today?date=`, `show: false` unless both switches are on and something is due; Daily asks only while Fitness is on), opening in place to today's tick rows by time and "Edit list" at its bottom right to `/fitness#supplements`; never a task, never in the six or the Sort, never history. Reminders, off, one time per part of the day (a Reminders `Fold`): `sendSupplementReminders` in cron.ts writes one `reminder` note per person, time and day through `noteStmt` (`supplementNoteId` `supp-<user>-<time>-<day>`, once, within an hour after the time in the person's zone, only while Track supplements and Fitness are on and something of that part is due and not all ticked; it names a count, never a name), and `clearPastSupplementNotes` clears past days' lines (`pastSupplementNote`). It pays nothing, counts toward no streak or badge, and never reaches Emerging, insights, the brief, `earningGuide`, the leaderboard, Club, a coach (not even under Reflection; only the Supplements and Supplement days tables, when their owner switches them on for that coach under All tables), the assistant or Companion (no tool reads it but `read_table`, and that only when the person switches the Supplements or Supplement days table on with Show to AI chat, on a model that keeps nothing, 2026-11-14), search, the calendar link or the public page. The one exception (2026-11-07): Reviews' The numbers shows its owner a count of supplements ticked against those due in the period (`suppTaken` of `suppDue`, read by `numbersFor` only when `GET /reviews` asks with `supplements` while Track supplements and Fitness are on, and kept in that review's own snapshot, which a coach never gets), never a name, and never on the dashboards' `GET /reviews/summary`, in Emerging, to a coach, the assistant or the brief; Logs keep no line (the `fitness` area); owner only on every statement; in the export (`supplements`, `supplementDays`, `fitnessPrefs`), gone with the account, kept through an erase of progress like the trackers; /privacy names its ground (consent when switched on; explicit consent for health data under GDPR Art. 9(2)(a) for the EU and UK) and `RETENTION` its keeping. Shared tests check the icons, `parseSupplement`, the no-claim words (`CAUSE_WORDS`, boost, cure, treat, prevent, heal, detox, immunity, dose, "mg a day") and that insights.ts, home.ts, badges.ts, dayKinds.ts, `BADGE_STATS` and `earningGuide` never name it; `TERMS` has eating moment, serving, supplement and nootropic. Both are for today or yesterday only (`logDay`, `dateNearOwnDay`: the person's own today or yesterday in their zone, so Yesterday holds in New York's evening; a sent day that is not a real calendar day is refused, 400), kept out of Logs like wellbeing (`fitness` and Kreators Run's `/run/move` are private paths in logs.ts), owner only, never read by brands, staff, the leaderboard, Club or a badge, a coach sees movement's minutes, days and kinds and the eating number (never a note, a distance or an effort) only under Reflection, and the whole Movement or Eating table only when its owner switches that table on for them (All tables) (`CoachTrackerDay`, `moveKinds`), the assistant reads them through `get_fitness` only with a model that keeps nothing (`sensitive`), they are in the export, kept through an erase of progress like every tracker, and gone with the account. Routes: `GET /fitness?day=` (35 days, `weekMinutes` for the last seven), `GET /fitness/month?month=`, `GET /fitness/moves` (paged, prefix `m`), `POST`, `PATCH` (today's and yesterday's, self-logged only) and `DELETE /fitness/move/:id`, `PUT /fitness/eat`, `DELETE /fitness/eat/:id` (limit `prefs`); `fitness` in `MODULE_FOR_PREFIX`. Nothing pays: no xp, no points, and no badge (the Fitness badge family is public and counts club entries only, so self-logged movement never inflates it). Life's physical area adds an Eating part (the week's average `eatDayScore`, fasting days left out) beside sleep, movement and breath, only for someone who logged eating in the last two weeks, and `MOVE_WEEK_TARGET` (150) is its movement target; reviews carry `eatAvg` and `moveDays`, and `PeriodPanel` has a `fitness` part (Active minutes, Days moved, Eating). The assistant logs through the same routes (2026-11-07): `log_movement` (`POST /fitness/move`, Undo deletes it) and `log_eating` (`PUT /fitness/eat` with the meals as lists of moments, `goodFuel`, `tasty`, fasting, fruit, veg and More, keeping any protein the day has; Undo only for the day's first check-in, so an older note never sits in `assistant_actions`), both needing Fitness; and `write_gratitude` fills the day's empty gratitude lines under its own prompts (`PUT /journal/gratitude/:day`, Undo only for a day that had none, by emptying it). The screen follows the hero rule: a plain header, the self-blue `Hero` with the last seven days' active minutes and Dew, Log movement (the one solid button) and How you ate (outline, with today's water read from Water and reminders and a link to Home), Supplements, the last seven days' bars ("Last 7 days", ending today), thirty-day lines of minutes and the eating score, a donut of kinds, the month's `CalendarGrid` whose day sheet changes today's and yesterday's in place and removes any day's on a second tap, By period, and the paged movement list. Never BMI, body size or calories, and a weight is never stored.
- Kreators Run (`run`, group Connection, accent friendship, off by default): clubs, runs, entries and collabs live in migration 0025. A run's `code` is the host's own word (`normalizeCode`, `ATTENDEE_CODE_MIN` to `ATTENDEE_CODE_MAX` letters or digits) or `randomCode(RUN_CODE_LENGTH)`, set by `newRunCode` and `setRunCode` in attendee.ts (`POST /run/runs/:id/recode` takes `xp`, `code` or `fresh`, a fresh code when nothing is asked), and is only ever returned to the host and events staff (`toRun(r, mine, showCode)`); an entry needs membership, the exact code, the window from `runEntryState` (thirty minutes before the start to three hours after the end) and is unique per run and person. The pay is the run's own xp (`runs.xp`, set by the host before the start, `ATTENDEE_XP_DEFAULT` unless they say, up to `ATTENDEE_XP_MAX`) plus `RUN_REWARD.points`, through `xp_events` source `run` and `points_ledger` reason `run`, capped at `RUN_REWARD.perDay`; a void writes the reverse. Whoever is shown the code (the club's host, events staff, admins) never logs the run. A run marked done still takes entries inside its window and stays on the club's list until the window closes; a run added after its start keeps its code but pays `ATTENDEE_XP_DEFAULT`, and the create answer says so (`codeError`). The pay is capped inside the batch that pays (`payRun`), so two entries at once cannot both slip under `RUN_REWARD.perDay`, and it counts toward the friendship pillar like a claim; erase keeps run entries, so a run cannot pay twice. Entry minutes are a `move` wellbeing log so Life's physical score counts them. Collabs carry a `giveaway` and are posted by staff with `events` or a brand's own team; only a club's host applies. No self-reported run ever pays. Under the cover the sections (Clubs, My runs, Brand collabs, Discussions) sit on the left and Start a club on the right of one line (two by two and the button on its own line on a phone), with the pay line under them. **Discussions** (wave 21, migration 0080 `run_messages` and `run_discussion_reads`, `components/RunDiscussions.tsx`, `/run/talk` in `routes/run.ts`, `RUN_TALK_DAYS_AFTER` and `runTalkOpen` in shared `runTalk.ts`): one per run, for its people only (the club's host, the members in the club by the time it ended, and anyone who logged it with its code; a non-member gets 404), drawn and polled like a channel with an unread dot (`?tab=talk&run=<id>`), ruled like an event conversation: open for writing until thirty days after the run and never for a cancelled one, limit `message`, blocks hide lines both ways, a line can be anonymous through `anonAlias` scoped `run:<id>` (staff keep the id), mentions tell once, a report becomes a help ticket with the line's words, the writer, the host or events staff take a line down, and every line renders through `Inert`. A run's old lines in `event_messages` moved there. **Log a run or walk** (2026-11-07, `RunMoveTool` at the top of My runs, over the club tiles now headed Club runs): on your own, today or yesterday, with no club or code, through `GET`, `POST /run/move` and `DELETE /run/move/:id` (gated by Run, limit `prefs`), which share Fitness's one movement action (`logMoveFrom` and `removeMoveFrom` in `apps/api/src/fitness.ts`) and write the same `move` log; it takes a run or a walk only (`RUN_TOOL_KINDS`), removes only its own, counts as movement in Life and pays nothing: no xp, no points, never a run badge or the My runs numbers, which stay club entries with a start code. Lines are kept `RETENTION_DAYS.channelLines`, are in the export and go with the account.
- School cohort discussions (2026-11-06, migration 0084 `cohort_talk` and `cohort_talk_prefs`, `routes/cohortTalk.ts` mounted under `/school/cohorts` so the School switch gates it, `components/CohortDiscussion.tsx`, shared `cohortTalk.ts`): threads for everyone enrolled in a cohort (enrolled or completed), its manager, mentors and the team with the `cohorts` permission; anyone else gets 404. Replies nest three levels under a post (`COHORT_TALK_MAX_DEPTH`, `cohortTalkPlace`, `cohortTalkOrder`); a reply to a third-level line sits beside it and names it (`reply_to_id`, "Replying to"). Each member is Public (their @handle, opening their page) or Private, the default, per cohort (`PUT /school/cohorts/:id/talk/me`); a private member shows as `cohortTalkPrivateLabel` (handle and friend code, each with four stars in the middle, `maskHandle`, `maskCode`), masked on the server for everyone but themself and the cohort's moderators, the same name on the line, in "Replying to" and in the inbox note. Block-author is refused for a private writer (a block is listed by name under Bond) and offered on screen only where the writer's id was sent; report and take-down work for all. The writer or a moderator takes a line down (words wiped, the place kept while replies hang from it), a report becomes a help ticket with the line's words, blocks hide lines both ways, mutes apply, and every line renders through `Inert`. Limits `message`, `prefs`, `ticket`, `follow`; lines kept `RETENTION_DAYS.channelLines`, a whole thread at a time by its newest line (`tidyOld`); in the export (`cohortTalkLines`, `cohortTalkPrefs`); the account delete removes its leaf lines and wipes the words of the rest.
- Attendee codes, for the Connection apps that hold events with guests (Party events, runs, circles; Community, Bond and Club have none of their own, and Club's events are Party events): the host sets the xp each guest gets (`ATTENDEE_XP_MAX`, one cap for hosts and admins) when the event is made or later, and a code, their own word or a random one; the xp is fixed once the code opens, thirty minutes before the start (`attendeeXpLocked`), and the code can change any time. `apps/api/src/attendee.ts` is the one rule (`setEventCode`, `setRunCode`, `claimEventCode`): only the event's own guests (Party: registered or attended; circles: seated at one of its tables; runs: club members, through the start code), inside the run window (`runEntryState`; a circle counts as `CIRCLE_MINUTES` long), once each, never anyone the server shows the code to (the host, events staff, admins), at most `ATTENDEE_CLAIMS_PER_DAY` event codes in any 24 hours, checked inside the batch that pays (runs keep their own cap), the claim and its xp in one batch (`attendee_claims` keyed by app, event and person, migration 0033), paid as `xp_events` source `attendee` to the friendship pillar; a Party claim also checks the guest in. Codes are normalised the same way everywhere (`normalizeCode`, 4 to 12 letters or digits) and reach only the host, events staff and admins: a guest sees the xp, whether they claimed it and when it opens and closes (`AttendeeCodeView`, `codeWindow`; the card works the state out from its own clock), and a circle's host sees counts, never who. Circles are run by events staff as a team: `GET /circles` gives them `hosting`, every open or matched circle in any city until its code closes, paged with prefix `host`, and the code card lives there. A weekly circle's next week keeps the xp with a new random code (`rollWeeklyCircles`). Claims stay through an erase (so a code cannot pay twice) and go with the account. Hosts set codes on the event page (`HostCodeCard`), the run card and a circle's card; guests claim on the event page and the table page (`ClaimCard`); admins see every event with guests and set any of them under Admin, Attendee codes (`GET /admin/attendee-codes`, paged, filtered by app and title; `PUT /admin/attendee-codes/:app/:id`), by the same rules, audited as `attendee_code.set` with the hosts' own resource names, and the host is told; New random codes touches only events that already have a code. A create form that cannot set the code says why (`codeError`) on the event page or the Circles page. The limit is `claim`.
- Life coaches (`coaching`, group Reflection, accent guru, off on a new account, path `/coaching`): an admin makes someone a life coach with the `coach` permission, which a creator can hold (like `mentor` and `moderation`) and which, alone among permissions, an admin does not hold unless given it (`isStaffFor`); the badge then reads Life coach (`BADGE_META`, 🎯). People ask to become one in the setup chat ("I'm a life coach", the `coach` tag, answered by `POST /onboarding/setup` with `coach: 'asked'`), by picking the Life coach tag under Profile, Who you are (`PATCH /me`, the same `askToCoach`), or under Coaching (`POST /coach/apply`); `askToCoach` in apps/api/src/coaching.ts is the one rule, the admins are told once, and Admin, Life coaches lists the asks (yes is giving the permission through the one permissions route, which tells the person; taking it away ends every link of theirs and tells their students).
