A coach works only with students who said yes: a link (`coach_links`, migration 0034, one open link per pair) starts when a coach (`POST /coach/requests`, by email or @handle, at most `MAX_COACH_ASKS` waiting) or the team (Admin, Connect people, which runs the same `teamCoachLink` as the older `POST /admin/coaching/links`; Life coaches keeps its asks, coaches and links and points there) asks and the student answers, when a student asks a coach from the directory (`POST /coach/ask/:coachId`) and the coach answers, or when the team links two directly (mode `link`); `linkProblem` checks every start (not yourself, one open link, `COACH_ASK_AGAIN_DAYS` after a no, `MAX_COACHES` per student). The student's side lives under `/me/coach` (answers from either side, `PATCH /me/coach/links/:id/share`, ending a link, notes, the change log) and is core, so switching Coaching off never stops anyone saying no or ending it; the coach's side is under `/coach`, gated by the module. What a coach sees is the student's choice per link, six switches (`COACH_SHARE_PARTS`: Daily and streaks, pillars and pyramids, routine and contract, life map answers, reflection, patterns; `COACH_SHARE_META` says exactly what each holds), read fresh on every request by `studentView`, `rosterRow` and `coachReport.ts` with the link active and the permission held at that moment: never the journal, gratitude, a tracker's note, money, water, supplements, focus, time blocks, Discover, the Companion, practices, the contract's signed name or a review's snapshot (since 2026-11-13 any of those that is a table reaches a coach only as that whole table, read only, when the student switches it on for that one coach under My coach's "Tables your coach can see"; Discover, the Companion, the signed name and a snapshot are no table and never do; see All tables), and the roster's red week is the contract's `roster` penalty made real. Reflection is mood, sleep, stress, mindful minutes, movement (minutes a day, days moved, kinds; `trackerDays`, the extra read only for its kind) and the eating number, with the saved reviews, named by `reviewNameFor` unless the person's own template names them, the daily ones in a list of their own (prefix `revd`), a review's walked layer only while Pillars is shared too (`toCoachReview`). **The report** (2026-11-07, the founder: "a detailed report of each student both visually and in other ways"; `apps/api/src/coachReport.ts`, the leaf `packages/shared/src/coachReport.ts`, subpath `@kreators/shared/coachReport`, chunk `shared-coach`; `components/CoachReportParts.tsx`, `CoachPatterns.tsx`, `CoachTaskLine.tsx`, `CoachTaskSheet.tsx`): `GET /coach/students/:id/report?kind=&date=` (Day, Week, Month, Quarter, `COACH_REPORT_KINDS`, in the page's address as `period` and `date`) builds on `summaryFor` and lets through only `COACH_PERIOD_FIELDS` (Daily: tasksDone, checkins, habitTicks, overdueAtEnd, notToDoSlipped, xp; Routine: routineDays; Reflection: moodAvg, sleepAvg, stressAvg, mindfulMin, moveMin, moveDays, eatAvg), plus the streak and each day's tasks and xp under Daily, xp by pillar and each pillar's layers and project pace under Pillars, the last seven routine days and the contract's days under Routine: never anything else in `PeriodNumbers`. **Patterns and suggestions** (`GET /coach/students/:id/patterns`, rules only, no model, stored nowhere): `factsFor` reads only what the link shares and leaves the journal, gratitude, money, water, focus, time blocks, Discover, Agency and reviews' numbers empty, `insightsFor` runs the Emerging rules, and `coachPatternsFrom` keeps only ready patterns on `COACH_PATTERN_KEYS` whose every read is shared, strips actions and reworded for the coach by `coachSays` (they for you; a quoted title stays). Patterns is the one part read off when a stored share does not name it (`DEFAULT_COACH_SHARE`): off on every link made before it and on a link the team makes at once, on for a link someone says yes to (`NEW_LINK_SHARE`, and they see the switch first); `POST …/patterns/ask` is one inbox note with a Turn it on in MyCoach, once per link per week, guarded on `coach_links.patterns_asked_at`, logged. `coachSuggestions` is the one rule for the coach's lines (at most `MAX_COACH_SUGGESTIONS`, worst first; sleep under 6 hours, stress at 4 or more, mood down, no movement, moved often, no check-in, tasks past due, tasks down, not to do slips, a streak, projects behind pace, routine under or over the contract, no review in three weeks, and from Patterns a good or bad habit or a task that keeps coming back): a line from a shared number follows that number's part and stays with Patterns off, which the Patterns switch says; a line about eating is never made; Leave a note fills the note box and Add a task the task form, and nothing is sent before the coach sends it. **Sections** (`COACH_SECTIONS`, `coachSectionState`, `parseCoachSections` the one reader, `components/CoachSections.tsx`; migration 0108 `coach_prefs`, `GET` and `PUT /me/coach/prefs`, limit `prefs`, core so it works with Coaching off, owner only, in the export, gone with the account): each section of the coach's student page and of Coaching is a `CoachFold` (a `Fold` whose open state is saved) with a sensible start (on the student page Report numbers, Charts, Patterns and suggestions and Notes open, the rest folded), and a "Show on this page" fold of `Toggle`s hides any of them, the roster's lines too, the same for every student; a link's hash still finds a hidden section. MyCoach's switches are a fold, open while an ask waits. **Becoming a coach**: every way in ends in one line ("Asked. The team will look at it."), Admin, Life coaches opens with the asks and a one-tap Yes (the one permissions route, which tells them and switches Coaching on) and No in each row, and a new coach gets First steps on Coaching (`CoachHub.steps`: fill your directory card, invite your first student, read how sharing works; ticked by doing, paying nothing, gone when done). **Adding a student**: one box first on the page (`AddStudent`), @username, friend code or email; `GET /coach/lookup` (limit `coachLookup`, 300 ms after the last key; nobody and someone blocked answer the very same words, a real code included) says which it read and on an exact @username or friend code shows a picture, a name and a handle, never an email or a phone, and the same words for nobody and for someone blocked either way; an email is never looked up until it is sent, and an email with no account gets the coach's invite link by private mail (never marketing, from `PUBLIC_URL`, at most 20 in 24 hours, the address masked in the log), in the same words as an address that blocked the coach, which gets nothing. **The invite link** (migration 0108 `coach_invites`, `retired_coach_invites`; `POST /coach/invite` on, fresh, off; Copy link and a QR code fold in `InvitePanel`, renewing and closing asked in `ConfirmDelete`): `/coach/join/<token>` (`screens/CoachJoin.tsx`) reads `GET /public/coach-join/:token` signed out and `GET /me/coach/join/:token` signed in, a renewed or closed token, kept only as a hash, or a coach who lost the permission answers 410 and an unknown one 404 (a blocked pair reads closed); signed out it shows the card and keeps the token for the tab in sessionStorage (`lib/coachJoin.ts`, read by `Shell`, which brings them back to the card once they are in); signed in it shows every switch, all on with Patterns, and Yes (`POST /me/coach/join/:token`, core) starts the link through `linkProblem` (an ask already waiting between them becomes the yes), logs `link.join` and tells the coach; Not now writes nothing. Limits `coachJoin` per address and `coachJoinLink` per coach, the token named `:token` in the log, retired rows gone after a year. A coach leaves notes (`coach_notes`, the student reads every one, in Coaching and the inbox) and adds or changes tasks under the student's own projects through the same `createNode` and `editNode` the student's taps use (`nodes.added_by`, shown as From your coach; `coachTaskFields` lets through title, note, do date, due date, time, length, priority, repeat and the habit flag, never a time block, a status, Remember or For review; a coach never ticks, archives or removes); writing needs the Pillars switch. A block by name from either side ends the link at once (`endLinksOnBlock`, in the words of any ending, never naming a block), `linkProblem` refuses a blocked pair, the directory leaves them out, and a streak that lapsed reads 0 (`streakNow` in db.ts, the one reading of `users.streak` for Daily, Home, the Companion, a public page and a coach; only a check-in moves the stored number). Every ask, answer, end, share change, note and task change is a line in `coach_log` with its time, shown at the end of the coach's student page (`/coaching/students/:id`) and the Coaching page for both sides (`GET /me/coach/log`). `MyCoachSection` (components/MyCoach.tsx) is the one student block, on the Profile tab (`#coach`) and in Coaching. In the setup chat "Work with a life coach" is the `find_coach` goal. Links, notes and the log are in the export, stay through an erase (a coach is a person, not progress; the tasks they added go with the pyramids) and end or go with the account. Limits `coachAsk` and `coachWrite`.
- Engagement, learned from Circle and Wylo (migration 0035): Community has six reactions (`REACTIONS`, `REACTION_META`; `community_likes.kind`, `community_reply_reactions`): the first reaction on a post is the like that pays through `rewardSocial`, changing it pays nothing, the same one again takes it back, and reactions on replies pay nothing. A post's author and everyone who replies follow it (`community_post_follows`, `POST /community/:id/follow-post` toggles); a reply tells the author (`reply`), the people it @mentions (`mention`, `mentionsIn`, at most `MAX_MENTIONS`, never across a block) and every other follower (`thread`), each once. Guides (staff) pin up to `MAX_PINNED` posts per category (`/manage/community/posts/:id/pin`; pinned come first inside a category) and can switch on holding a new member's first post (`community_settings` `hold_first_posts`, off by default): a held post is seen only by its author, pays and tells its mentions when a guide says okay (`/manage/community/held`, `/posts/:id/approve`). A note's date and time are its card's foot, never wrapping (`stamp`, "7 Nov 26, 4:17am", the founder, 2026-11-07): from `md` at the bottom right, level with the helper's line while they fit side by side, else on a line of their own under it, so the title and the helper keep the whole width; on a phone (under `md`) on a line of their own at the left, under the note's icon, which sits above the words there instead of at their left; the unread dot stays at the top right and the archive button at the row's right, 44px (its name is its `aria-label` only, with no tip drawn on hover or focus, since one would cover the next note; 2026-11-13). The inbox has tabs by kind (`INBOX_TABS`, `INBOX_TAB_META`, 2026-11-07 after the founder's "inbox items are not going in right place"): every kind the server writes is one of `INBOX_KINDS` (`InboxKind`, each named once, under its tab, in `TAB_KINDS` in shared `community.ts`; `notify` and `notifyMany` take it, and every note is written by the one statement `noteStmt` in `apps/api/src/notes.ts` (modes add, once on a fixed id, renew in place), whose kind is an `InboxKind`; `scripts/scan.sh` refuses `INTO notifications` anywhere else; a note to the team goes to every admin, `adminIds` in db.ts through `notifyMany`, never a capped read) and sits in exactly one tab besides Everything (`inboxTabOf`, a shared test insists): Replies and mentions (reply, mention, dm, support, and moderation, which goes to the writer), Following (thread, follow, broadcast, sequence, season), Reminders (reminder: your own reminders, the check-in, Remember tasks, focus and reviews), Events (party, circle, run and their reminders), Friends (bond), Work (work, campaign, org, collab: a run club's brand collab, win, club, tv, fund), Shop and money (order, booking, refund, lead, affiliate, review, certificate, store, kyc, invoice), School and coaching (school, invite, coach), Agency (while Agency is on, or while the person holds an Agency note: a member asked onto someone's team has Agency off) and Team (`STAFF_INBOX_KINDS`, only `team`, only for staff); a tab not offered is read as Everything by the list and by Mark all read, Archive read items and Delete all archived alike, and the screen marks the tab the server answered (`q.data.tab`); the row's pills say Replies, Shop and School, the All sheet the whole names; the first three stay Everything, Replies and mentions, Following, the rest behind the row's All, each with its own empty line, and the unread count per tab sums its kinds; the daily check-in notes are found by their ids (`checkin-<user>-<day>` from the cron, `daily-<user>-<day>` from Daily), never by kind, since the rest are reminders too; Unread and Archived views and an archive (`notifications.archived_at`; archiving marks read; in Archived each note has Restore and Delete, and "Delete all archived" empties the tab, all behind a confirm sheet: `DELETE /inbox/:id` and `DELETE /inbox/archived` remove only archived notes, so a note must be put away first). A note opens the page where its thing is and lands on its section (the founder, 2026-11-07: "ensure the notifications open the right page and sections too"): your own reminders on `/home#reminders` (Home's Water and reminders, opened on that panel at every width, since a laptop's Daily shows only the bubble), supplements on `/today#supplements` with Show on Daily and `/fitness#supplements` without, a Remember task on `/today?task=<id>` ("N more waiting" opens when it is not among the six), a reply on `/community?post=<id>#reply-<id>` (the post's replies, read through `GET /community/posts/:id` by the feed's own rules), an offer or a listing's message on `/store?listing=<id>`, a run's folder on `/run/clubs/<club>#run-photos-<run>`, a follow on the follower's page (by id, `/people/<id>`, since a handle can pass to someone else; the link comes off when they leave), and the rest on a section's heading id; a note into an app that is off opens that app's own "is off" page, and a copyright notice has no link, since it has no page in the app. A note stored with an old link opens in the new place through `MOVED` in `screens/Inbox.tsx`. `scripts/check-note-links.mjs` (run by `scripts/scan.sh`) reads every `noteStmt`, `notify` and `notifyMany` with the TypeScript parser and fails a link with no route in App.tsx (a redirect does not count) or a hash no id in the web app has; a link it cannot work out (a column from SQL, a row's property) needs a `note-link: /party/:id` comment on the call or just above it. The cron sends a reminder a day before and an hour before every Party event, circle table and run someone signed up for (`sendEventReminders` in engage.ts, once each through `event_reminders`, in the person's zone), and a host runs a Party event again on a new date (`POST /party/events/:id/duplicate`, no guests, xp with a fresh code). The leaderboard has a monthly season (`by=season`, xp earned since the first of the month, UTC); `closeSeason` closes last month once, keeps its top three (`season_results`) and gives them the `season_top` honor. Home carries the daily brief (`GET /home/brief`; a toggle since 2026-11-13, the founder: its heading is the button with a `ToggleChevron`, open by default, folded per person on the device under `kreators.briefFolded:<user>`, read and written in try/catch; Getting started under it unchanged): three signals and three next steps from `briefFor` in shared, rules only so it costs no credits, never pointing at an app that is off (`briefPath` in shared is the one check: a path inside an app that is off loses its link and keeps its words, for the coach's and the team's lines too); from Fitness, Kreators Run and Journal (2026-11-07) it says a good week of movement (`move-week`, `BRIEF_MOVE_WEEK` 150 active minutes in seven days) and offers a short walk from noon on a day with no movement (`move`, to Fitness or Kreators Run's tool), the eating check-in from 18:00 (`eat`, Fitness only) and the day's gratitude from noon (`gratitude`), each only while its app is on and it is not done (`BriefFacts.move`, `eatToday`, `gratitudeToday`, read in `routes/home.ts` as counts in one statement holding only the parts whose app is on), plus lines about a coach's students (from what each shares) and about waiting work for the team; under it, for the first `GETTING_STARTED_DAYS`, the Getting started list (`gettingStarted`), ticked only by doing the thing, paying nothing, folded for good with `POST /home/getting-started/hide`.
- Chat channels (migration 0062, `routes/channels.ts`, `components/Channels.tsx`; `CHANNEL_*`, `MAX_CHANNEL_*`, `channelSlug`, `channelNameProblem`, `cleanChannelMessage`, `mergeChannelLines`, `isNearBottom` in shared): topic rooms in Community (a Channels tab beside the feed, `?tab=channels&channel=<id>`) and Bond (a Channels section, `?channel=<id>#bond-channels`), mounted at `/community/channels` and `/bond/channels` so the module gate and `ROUTE_LIMITS` (limit `channelMessage`) treat each as its own app. Community channels are public; only a guide (`can(user, 'moderation')`) opens, renames or closes them; anyone joins, leaves and writes, and a line can be anonymous through `anonAlias` scoped `channel:<id>` (no id, handle or rank for others; staff keep the id; the writer's `mine` flag finds their line); `canMute` and `community_mutes` apply. Bond channels are private (`MAX_CHANNELS_OWNED`, `MAX_CHANNEL_MEMBERS` counting the asked), the owner asks accepted friends only (they answer yes or no), removes people and takes down any line, a non-member gets 404, and nobody is anonymous. Polled, never pushed: `GET …/messages?after=<seq>&from=<seq>&read=1` returns only newer lines plus `gone` (held lines taken down); `before=<seq>` is Load earlier; the screen polls every `CHANNEL_POLL_MS` only while the tab is visible and scrolls to a new line only when the reader was near the bottom. Unread comes from `channel_members.last_read_at`. Blocks hide lines both ways, mentions tell once (`mentionsIn`, never across a block), a report becomes a help ticket with the line's words, a takedown by someone else is audited and wipes the words. Every line renders through `Inert`. Lines are kept `RETENTION_DAYS.channelLines`, are in the export and go with the account.
- Anonymity: circle members and Bond chats are aliases only, with no reveal; member ids never reach the browser (chats from a table are opened by alias).
- No demo or mock data, anywhere: there is no seed route, no demo flag on rows, no placeholder content. Sign-up creates the user and seven zero scores and nothing else. Test data lives only in the smoke server's own database on port 8788. Sign out is `components/SignOut.tsx`, in the laptop sidebar footer, the phone's apps menu and the Profile tab (at the top right of its header, `compact`): a button with a door icon and a pale red outline that always asks first in a sheet ("Sign out?", the red yes "Yes, sign out" and "Stay signed in"); a device-only account is also told what it would lose and offered an email first.
- Contrast rule, from the kit: on a solid colour fill (`bg-r1`…`bg-r7`, a `Hero`) every foreground element is white (`text-on-fill`, rings and lines in `var(--color-on-fill)` with an `rgba(255,255,255,0.3)` track, buttons `variant="light"`). Yellow is the one exception and takes `text-on-yellow`. `bg-ink` is different: it turns cream in dark mode, so everything on it uses the `on-ink` token (`text-on-ink`, `text-on-ink/70`, `bg-on-ink/15`), never `text-white` or `bg-white`. Dark text and dark buttons sit only on cream, card and tint surfaces. Never put a brown ring, brown text or an ink button on a coloured card.
- Dashboards use the kit's chart vocabulary in `components/Charts.tsx`: `AreaLine` for trends, `PillBars` for days, `Gauge` and `Ring` for scores, `Donut` with `Legend` for shares, `Bubbles` for what comes up most, `CalendarGrid` for a month, `EmojiRow` for moods, `Sparkline`, `Segments` and `WeekDots` on rows. Pick the chart that fits the data; a screen with numbers should never be a wall of text. Moods are `MoodFace`, not emoji. A hero or a coloured card carries one of the seven characters (`Avatar`) with a mood that follows its number. Every tracker screen shows a line over thirty days. Line charts (`AreaLine`, `MultiLine`) keep their frame on: y labels, baseline and gridlines show even with no data; only the small line inside a metric card turns `axes` off.
- Store (`/store`, group Creation, off by default): three shelves and a rule for each. New gear is added only by staff (`isStaff`: admin or sub_admin) and bought through a partner link; the app sends people through `/out/:kind/:id` (store items and verified tools) so the tap is counted in `outbound_clicks` with a salted hash and never a raw address, and the card says "Partner link. Kreators may earn a share." Pre-owned gear is listed by anyone (`preowned_listings`, up to `MAX_OPEN_LISTINGS`, condition from `PREOWNED_CONDITIONS`, https picture links only), takes offers (`preowned_offers`: one open offer per buyer, the seller accepts or declines, accepting marks the listing agreed and declines the rest) and a chat per listing and buyer (`preowned_messages`, polled, the seller picks the buyer); the direct store takes neither. Money and the handover are settled between the two people; Kreators takes nothing and never moves the money. The filters are one `FilterBar` of three `FilterSelect`s (Category, Stage, Budget), and "Sell something" sits on the cover's helper line. Kits (`store_kits`, staff only) group store items and pre-owned pieces by stage and budget and add up live. A featured spot is a week at the top (`FEATURE_DAYS`): staff give it free to anything, a seller pays `FEATURE_FEE_POINTS` points for their own live listing (ledger reason `feature`); featured cards carry the yellow tag and the yellow edge (`FeaturedTag`, `cardClass`) and sort first everywhere, including Tools. Creator bundles of a creator's own products stay in the Shop (`kind: 'bundle'`).
- Content's strategy path (`strategyPath`, `StrategyPath` on the brand folder, `counts` on `GET /studio/brands/:id`): eight steps from the brand's niche and voice to a calendar, done by doing them — see who is winning (`channels` research: YouTube channels with subscribers when there is a key, else videos grouped by who made them), find outliers, take one apart, write the playbook (`playbook` piece: counted hook types, structures, lengths and topics), ideas, a script, the month. It is the guided order the separate tools lacked, in the spirit of Sandcastles' research-to-script flow.
- Content is a strategist (`content`, group Creation, path `/shop/content`): one folder per brand (`brands`, up to `MAX_BRANDS`; migration 0028), each with the links it is fed (`brand_sources`, https only, kind guessed by `sourceKindOf` except competitor, which is the owner's choice), the research runs on them (`research_runs`, kinds in `RESEARCH_KINDS`, every run kept with its items and the backend that answered) and the pieces written from that (`brand_pieces`, kinds in `PIECE_KINDS`, each labelled with its `writer`). Research goes through `apps/api/src/reach.ts`: Agent Reach (a read-only Python CLI, github.com/Panniantong/agent-reach) over the bridge in `scripts/reach/server.mjs` at `REACH_URL` with `REACH_KEY`; without it the Worker reads plain pages itself and uses the YouTube Data API, and everything else answers with a plain "needs Agent Reach" instead of pretending. Writing goes through `apps/api/src/strategist.ts`: Claude through the official SDK (`claude-opus-5-5`, streaming, server-side fallbacks) when `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` is set, Workers AI when only the binding exists, otherwise `assemble` lays the research out in the shape of the piece and says so. Pieces are the app's small markdown. Only the brand's own folder goes to the model: never journal, wellbeing or customers. Folders are in the export and go with the account. The attribution screen (posts and tracked links) stays at `/shop/content/results`. Limits `research` and `generate` guard the runs and the writes. Writing a piece researches first by default (`autoResearch` in routes/strategist.ts, off with `research: false` or the "Research first" box on the Create tab): it reads every unread link in the folder and, when Agent Reach is connected, runs outliers on the niche and a search on the topic, skipping anything already run in the last day, and leans the piece on those runs.
- Onboarding is the setup chat (`screens/SetupChat.tsx` at `/onboarding`, from Welcome): a few taps that find out what someone wants and who they are, then show the apps that fit and hide the rest; the chat says show and hide like the Apps page ("Show 3 apps", "All set! Your 3 apps are ready."; the founder, 2026-11-07), and his "each one a switch" headline on the site and the real switches stay. **Event first, setup later** (2026-11-07, the founder's attendee flow; migration 0121 `users.attendee_event`, shared `eventJoin.ts` in `shared-base`): the site lists upcoming public events at `/events` (`site/Events.tsx`, `GET /api/public/events`, paged, `ListingCard` with four facts: price, the time in the visitor's view by lib/date.ts, the place or Online, seats left; Explore's event cards open the same page) and each has a landing page at `/events/:id` (`GET /api/public/events/:id`; `routes/eventsPublic.ts`, limit `eventsPublic` per address; only public events, a private or Club-only one reads exactly like a missing one, no guest, host id or room link is ever sent). Its one main action, Join this event (`SITE_PRIMARY`), carries the sign-up for a visitor right on the page (the device session first, as Welcome makes it, then `POST /auth/signup` on it, same fields and rules and `TermsBox`), then `POST /party/events/:id/arrive` (limit `eventJoin`; a public, published event still to come; for an account not set up it sets `attendee_event` and switches Party, Community and Bond on in `users.modules`, for one that is only Party; no seat, no points), then the ordinary RSVP for a free event; a ticket in points is bought on the event's page in the app on the person's own tap. They land on `/party/:id`, which for any guest has Event chat (`#talk-<id>`), Community and Bond one tap each. `Shell` lets an account with `attendeeEvent` and no `onboardedAt` into `ATTENDEE_PATHS` only (`/party`, `/community`, `/bond`, `/inbox`, `/you`, `/help` and what is under them; `attendeeMayOpen`), everything else goes to the setup chat, whose Close then returns to the event; sign-in from `/events/:id` returns there. While setup is unfinished `components/AttendeeBar.tsx` (lazy) is a bar fixed along the bottom left, level with the assistant's button on a phone and 16px up after the sidebar from the tablet, stopping short of the assistant, z 35 under sheets and the privacy card, with a spacer in the flow: one line (read out only on a phone) and Finish setting up, the ✕ hiding it until tomorrow on a second tap (`kreators.attendeeBar:<user>`, the device's day). The setup keeps Party, Community and Bond on for someone who came through an event; an erase and the delete clear the mark. It is light on purpose: one guide (Bumling, the app's own guide) the whole way, in clear, full sentences that say what to do and why, a few questions (the wording says "a few", never a number, and never how long it takes; where a time must be named it is "a few minutes"), the last one only for people who make things. The parts: the account when there is none, in two small cards (`SignInCard`: name, email, password twice; then `PrivateCard`: phone and birthday, with one line on why: 18 or over, never shown), then what brings you here (up to three goals), which of these sound like you (roles and How you move, or "None of these"), what you make (only for a creator or freelancer: their own content and/or what they do for clients, "Skip this"), what usually gets in your way ("Nothing, skip"), and the apps. Every list is drawn in small labelled groups (`SETUP_GOAL_GROUPS`, `SETUP_ROLE_GROUPS`, `SETUP_BLOCKER_GROUPS`; the chips say what a person would say: "I'm a student", "Earn from my content", "Hard to focus (ADHD counts)"), and every answer is said back in plain words ("So you're a student and you freelance for clients. Nice!"). The typing box works at any point: a question ("what is Army?") gets a plain answer from `lib/explain.ts` (the app's line in `SETUP_APP_LINES`, one of the seven, a level on the map, a word in `TERMS` or a short line of its own for a word that is not one, such as the daily review or the mid-year check-in; the longest phrase wins and plurals count; a chunk of its own, fetched the first time someone asks) and the choices come back under it; anything else is read by `readAbout` in shared, the tags it picked up are shown as pills, and every question it already answered is skipped. The lists themselves are `IDENTITIES`, `SETUP_GOALS` and `SETUP_BLOCKERS`; the roles include Agency or studio owner, Agency team member, Brand or client, Life coach and Run club host, and the services Video editor and Videographer read the same words as Agency's `TEAM_ROLE_META` (wave 21; stored keys never change, so `editor` and `shooter` stay); a tag may imply a kind of day (`dayType`, read with `dayTypeFor`: an editor's deep work day), Reader leans to Knowledge and School, those who move are led to Fitness (2026-11-07: `SETUP_MOVE_GROUP`, How you move, on the who card: I run, I train, I do yoga or meditate, I swim, the Swimmer tag new; Move more and the new Eat well goal put Fitness first; low energy weighs it; never a supplement, a test checks), and `SETUP_COMBOS` lean an editor or videographer to Agency, Army, Work and School, a brand or client to Agency and Army, and an agency owner or team member to Agency with its Team tab named in the reason; tags in someone's own words go through `cleanIdentity` and `parseIdentities` (three at most, one stage, a label that names a known tag becomes that tag). `matchSetup` is the one matching rule: every tag, goal (counted double) and blocker weighs the apps, each pair in `SETUP_COMBOS` that is all there adds its own weight and its own reason, every answer brings its own strongest app first so the mix always shows both who someone is and what they came for, and the rest fill in by score up to the cap (`SETUP_APPS_MAX` 5; four when focus is hard, three when time is short); each app says why in words built from the answers (a tag, goal or blocker's `why` for one app says more than its `because`), `identityLine` says who they are in one sentence, and the shared test checks that every role and goal pair gets its own result and nine in ten get their own set of apps. The match is a short list of tick rows, each with the app's one plain line (`SETUP_APP_LINES`, about one line on a phone) and its reason one tap away ("Why these apps?"), apps that could fit too behind "See 1 more", and one button. `POST /onboarding/setup` works the match out again from the answers, switches the kept apps on and every other app the setup can switch (`setupSwitchable`: Contribution, Creation, Connection and Reflection, never the essentials or the team's tools) off, writes `users.identities` and `users.intake_json` (migration 0030), fills focus, skills, interests, experience, availability and profile goals from what the tags carry, and sets `onboarded_at`; limit `setup`. The last card tours the tabs in two lines and offers the strategy session by the match (`session`, which picks its words: now, soon or some day); the choice itself is always the last one, on a line of its own at full width (`ChatChoice.row`), and the pills before it fill their lines (`fill-row-all`), so no button is left alone (the founder, 2026-11-06). Profile opens with its cover (left-aligned: the picture, the whole name, never cut, with the Pro and role marks, which drop under it on a phone, and the line), then "Who you are" (the founder, 2026-11-06: "make the set my tags bento style options to select"): the title, the identity line (or "No tags yet. Pick the ones that fit you."), then every tag in `IDENTITIES` as a tile to pick (`components/TagPicker.tsx`): the chat's own groups, `SETUP_ROLE_GROUPS` open with their eyebrow and icon, then Your own content, For clients, Where you are with it (one stage at a time, so a second replaces the first and the status line says so) and The rest of life, each a `Fold`, closed on load with "N picked" beside its title so the section stays short on a phone; each tile a `<button>` with `aria-pressed`, the tag's icon, label (`tie()`, so a wrapped label grows going down) and `blurb`, a round tick in the corner, `toy toy-soft` on glass at rest and picked in `--soft-self` with an `r5` edge and a filled tick (no weight change), laid out by `evenGrid` and `evenTile` on a container grid of two columns, three from a 32rem column and four from 48rem, so no row ends in a lone tile; a tap saves at once through `PATCH /me {identities}` (`parseIdentities`, at most `MAX_IDENTITIES`, past which the other tiles wait), written to the cached account first and put back on a refusal, the account read again after the last of a quick run of taps, with a quiet `role=status` line (Saved., or the error); tags in the person's own words sit above the tiles on one wrapping line after the "In your own words" label, as neutral `IconTag`s (marks, not controls) kept on every save, each with a small ✕ beside it (28px, a 44px hit area) that removes it on a second tap ("Tap ✕ again to remove", four seconds), and an "Add your own" field with a + button at the end of that line while there is room, and "Match my apps again" (outline, to `/onboarding?fresh=1`, which offers a second go filled in with the last answers and is the one way to match the apps again) ending that same line at its right on a laptop (`action` and `actionClass` on `TagPicker`), and under `lg` at the right of the "Who you are" title's line instead, since a phone's and a tablet's line has no room for it (one drawn at a time; the founder, 2026-11-13) (`MAX_CUSTOM_IDENTITIES` 3, `IDENTITY_LABEL_MAX` 30, cleaned by `cleanIdentity` as the server does: words that name a known tag pick that tile and the status line says so; the founder, 2026-11-06); picking Life coach there asks to become one exactly as the chat does (`PATCH /me` calls `askToCoach` when the `coach` tag is newly added by someone without the permission, answers `coach: 'asked'` or `'waiting'`, and the status line says the team was asked); picking tags never switches apps (only the chat does), and a two-line note says tags mark the apps that fit you and never show on the public page (only the chat fills skills and interests from tags); Features marks every app that fits with "Fits you" and its reason; `PATCH /me` takes `identities` through the same validator. Tags never reach the public page; they are in the export and go with erase and delete. The reader is rules, not a model.
- Every chat draws with `components/Chat.tsx`: the setup chat and the strategy session with `ChatScreen`, the assistant and the Companion with its parts (`ChatTurns`, `useChatMotion`, `ChoiceChips`, `ChoiceGroups`, `ChatActions`, `ChatLinks`, `ChatComposer`; a speaker that is not one of the seven is a `ChatVoice`). Under every answer the buttons are one row (`ChatActions`): a skip on the left when there is one, the main button on the right with a few words after its label (how many are picked, "one more step"). In the strategy session every button fills the width instead (`full` on `ChatActions`, `ChoiceGroups` and `ChoiceChips`; a row of numbers shares one full-width row). A label never breaks inside its button: when the skip and the main button do not fit side by side (measured from their labels), both take the full width, one above the other. The two whole-page chats are drawn the way ChatGPT and Claude draw theirs: a plain page (no tint behind it), a slim sticky header (Start over on the left, Close and the theme switch at the far right; the title and the step, left-aligned, sit between them on a laptop and on a second line under them on a phone, so they are never cut short; then a thin progress line), the guide's lines as plain text under its avatar and name, the person's lines as bubbles on the right, the choices or the card right under the question (`data-answer`), "Tell me more" and "Change my last answer" as small links under that, and a typing box at the bottom (`data-composer`) with a soft fade above it. The page is the only thing that scrolls. Each new turn is lifted to just under the header (two frames after the answer, so a typing box shrinking back cannot cut the lift short), and the last turn is at least a screen tall, so the question and its choices are read near the top, never behind the header or the typing box and never at the bottom edge. The column is at most 760px wide. Ticks on a list are kept by the chat (`picked`), so a question typed in between never loses them. `/onboarding` and `/session` sit outside `Standalone`, because the header carries the theme switch.
- The strategy session is the pillars' onboarding, one conversation (`screens/SessionChat.tsx` at `/session`, from the ink card on Pillars and the setup chat's last card; the spec is `Strategy Session App Product Spec.md`). Never a left-to-right card flow, and never one tap per question: thirteen parts in a chat (`PARTS`: the account, the frame, the map, what gets in the way, the scorecard, the real day, the focus, routine and practices, the contract, the call, the pillars, first steps, what comes next), each answered in one go on a single card that sits in the conversation itself, right under the question (`data-answer`, full width), drawn with the elements the card flow used (band and level names with their lines, option cards with a title and a blurb, slider rows, time fields, pills). The map is placed for all seven at once (`MapForm`: two selects per relationship, levels grouped by band with that relationship's band statement; evidence asked once, in one card, for every pillar placed at 400 or over) and the focus is derived from the whole. Sign-up is inside the onboarding: the site's "Start free" goes to Welcome, Welcome opens a device session (`POST /auth/anon`) and the setup chat, and when the account has no email Dew's first card is the account (`components/AccountForm.tsx`: name, email, password twice, phone, date of birth, the same rules as the sign-up page; the session keeps the same card for a device account that opens it from Pillars), sent to `POST /auth/signup` with the device token so the device user is upgraded in place and nothing said is lost; an account that already has an email (from `/signup` or sign-in) skips that card, and the card comes first whenever the account has none, even mid-way or after onboarding, after which the chat resumes where it was. Every "Create an account" and "Start free" link (sign-in, the site's feature pages, Profile, the sign-out sheet) goes to the setup chat, never to the form at `/signup`, which stays only as a route. A part is hosted by the one whose subject it is: Bumling, the app's own guide, hosts every part that belongs to no one of the seven (the account, the frame, the map, what gets in the way, the scorecard, the real day, the focus, the routine, the contract, the call, the pillars, the first steps and what comes next), Lumen, guide to faith, hosts the practices, and each focus pillar's own questions are asked by its character. The host's avatar and name sit above its lines, and its typing box answers what any word means. Every answer, tapped or sent from a card, is written into the chat as the person's line. The host explains before each ask, every ask carries a "Tell me more", and "Change my last answer" undoes one step. The vibe check for the focus pillars runs inside the chat (three taps per pillar from `QUESTION_BY_ID`; every pick question also takes your own words with Write my own: a foundation in your words replaces the template's, sent as `pillar` in `LadderPicks`, and an answer of your own counts as the middle choice), then one card picks the value goal and project for every focus pillar, `build` runs, and the chat ends with the pyramids drawn, then two choices: Open my pillars, or Add my interests (`components/ProfileCard.tsx`: interests with `InterestPicker`, the city and a line about you, saved with `PATCH /me`; the setup chat's last card offers the same) (`Pyramid` with the derived words) and the order to work through all seven (`priorityOrder`: floors under Courage first, then the lowest cores weighted by insecurity, each with its reason). The chat resumes from the server, the next step first and "Place me again" beside it: a saved assessment without a contract offers the focus, a signed plan without its pyramids offers "Build my pillars", and a finished session offers "Keep my map". The rules and the content are unchanged: the frame (health, wealth, seven relationships); a placement on the map for each relationship (`MAP_LEVELS`, sixteen named levels from 20 to 540 in three bands, `BAND_STATEMENTS` per relationship, shown as names and never numbers): the *core* where you usually live and the *floor* where you drop under stress, with a line of evidence asked when the core is 400 or over; fears (`FEAR_OPTIONS`, a pillar and whether it drives decisions) and insecurities (a weight picked in words, `INSECURITY_WEIGHTS` from A little to Very heavy, kept out of ten and read back with `weightWord`, and a pillar), asked early; a scorecard (`SCORECARD`, five stars each, `STAR_WORDS` from Very poor to Great, kept out of ten at two points a star with `scoreOfStars` and shown back with `starsOf`) with the behaviour question (days in seven) whenever a relationship scores 8 or more over a floor under Courage; the real day; the focus; the routine; practices; the contract; the optional live call. `placementFlags` in shared is the one rule: a core of 400 or more with no evidence is capped at 350, the floor never sits above the core, a floor under `COURAGE` (200) marks the pillar as the suggested place to start (the key stays `required`; nothing is forced: the person picks the relationships to work on, up to two, nothing is locked, and `POST /session/plan` keeps exactly what they picked), and a gap over `VOLATILE_GAP` (150) marks it volatile. `pickFocus` suggests at most two, ticked to start with: floors under Courage first, then the lowest cores less ten times the insecurity weight sitting there. `settleScore` caps a score of 8 or more at 6 when the floor is under 200 and fewer than four of seven days were courageous. `routineFor` builds the six blocks (`BLOCK_KEYS`) from the real day for a general and an exceptional day, ramping wake-up and lights-out by `RAMP_MINUTES` (30) a week of compliance towards `TARGET_WAKE` and `TARGET_SLEEP`; only the morning block has steps, and the day is done only when every step is ticked. Each morning step carries its minutes (`minutes`). The life map shows every block's times and how long it runs (`blockMinutes`, `minutesLabel`) and what the steps add up to (`stepsMinutes`), and edits the routine there (`components/RoutinePanel.tsx`, for a general and an exceptional day: each block's times, the morning steps and their minutes, up to `ROUTINE_STEPS_MAX`, and a note per block), saved by `PATCH /session/routine` through `parseRoutineBlocks`, the one check (the routine's own order, and a morning that always keeps a step). Daily shows the minutes beside each step. Practices are a mantra from `MANTRAS` (the person's own words are the `own` choice, on the left beside No mantra yet, kept in `ownMantra` up to `OWN_MANTRA_MAX`; `mantraLine` says any mantra in words), breathing from `BREATHING` (box, four seven eight, alternate nostril and, since 2026-11-06, a general Pranayam: slow belly breaths with a longer breath out), a line (`affirmationFor` by default) and a page to read. The contract (`contracts`: days a week, `CONTRACT_MIN_DAYS_DEFAULT` 5, a check-in time, a penalty from `PENALTIES`, a typed name, the next weekly and monthly dates) is what makes it a plan; its check-in time is written to `users.reminder_time`. Tables in migration 0029 (`assessments`, `placements`, `routines`, `contracts`, `routine_days`), in the export and cleared by erase and delete; routes in `apps/api/src/routes/session.ts` (`GET /session`, `POST /session/assessment`, `POST /session/plan`, `POST /session/day`), core and always open, owner only. The session lives inside Pillars: the *life map* (`components/LifeMapPanel.tsx`, the section `#life-map` under the pillar list on `/tower`; `/tower/map` redirects there) is everything the session found (all seven on the map ladder, the placement bars, the focus, the scorecard, fears and insecurities, the routine, the practices and the contract), with "Run the session again" and "Build the pillars for your focus"; On Pillars the map is not a separate card: each pillar card carries its own line (floor to core, the Courage mark, a Focus tag, a note when the floor is under Courage or the drop is big) under one summary line that jumps to the life map below; before the session Pillars shows the ink card that starts it. Every pillar page carries its one-line placement. `components/LifeMap.tsx` holds `MapLadder` (the sixteen levels in three bands with the Courage line, a full character on each core and a faded one on each floor), `PlacementChart` and `placementLine`; the session's map step draws `ConsciousnessMap`, the picture of the whole scale the map comes from, laid out like the chart it was drawn from: the title, Self Identification and Location on the left with their spans, a funnel of the dimensions in the middle (12th Dimension down to 3rd Dimension, silver and black at the top through gold, violet, blue and green to red at the point), every level beside it with its number from 1000 Full Consciousness down to 20 Shame (`ABOVE_THE_MAP` plus `MAP_LEVELS`), the three categories on the right with the app's band names under them, and the Courage line; coloured with the relationship colours and the `--color-scale-*` tokens. It fits its column and never scrolls sideways: the whole chart in one SVG (`WIDE`) when the column is 560 or wider, and on a phone `NARROW`: the funnel, the levels and the categories only, without Self Identification and Location, with a light band's chip pulled in from the funnel's edge so "3rd Dimension" never touches a level. The white dimension labels carry a soft `--color-ink-fixed` halo under the letters (`paint-order: stroke`). Placements are still picked by name; since 2026-11-06 (the founder: "give additional details as tags ... explaining numbers from inverted pyramid") every level on the map card (`MapForm`) carries two tags at the right of its row, centred on it (`LevelTags` in LifeMap.tsx, the tag look: a pale wash, a dot in the colour, ink words): its number on the scale in the funnel's colour for that row (`scaleColor`, the nearest stop of the funnel's gradient) and its dimension (`dimensionOf`: 5th Dimension, New Humanity, 4th or 3rd Dimension) in its category's colour; a ticked level's wash deepens, and on a narrow phone the tags drop under the words at the right so the title keeps its width. The same chart sits on Pillars, under How a pillar works and above How it works. Every level picker lists the levels top down, like the map: At ease first, Shame last. `Shell` sends anyone not onboarded to `/onboarding`, the setup chat; Welcome's button, sign-up, sign-in and the erase under Profile all go there too (never straight to `/vibe-check` or `/session`), and `/vibe-check?floors=` still works on its own for the life map's build button. The session loads the latest assessment on open; its Close goes back to Pillars (the life map section once there is one), and the vibe check's Close with a preset focus goes back to the session. The session ends with the vibe check for the focus pillars inside the chat, which builds only those pyramids; Daily shows the routine under Show up (`components/RoutineSection.tsx`: one closed `Fold`, "Your routine" with today's morning count beside it ("2 of 5 today", Done today), opened only by a tap or a link to `/today#routine` (Emerging's card, Life's and Reviews' Routine tiles, the brief's routine line; 2026-11-14, the founder: "put routine tasks in a toggle on daily and keep it in non open state"; without a session the same closed row reads "Not set up yet" and holds the Run the strategy session card, so every state and the loading row are one 44px height), holding the week's line and dots, the day type, the morning steps as tick rows, the other blocks as tick rows too, each with its times and note (2026-11-13, the founder; migration 0143 `routine_days.blocks_json`, `RoutineDay.blocks`, `blocks` on `POST /session/day`, only keys the day type has and never the morning, a body without `blocks` keeps the day's; a block's tick pays nothing and never moves `done`, which stays every morning step), the week's dots against the contract). The live call (₹10,000, charged in rupees) is a switch on the contract card and a paid service (2026-11-06, migration 0100 `call_requests`, `liveCall.ts`, `components/LiveCall.tsx`, `LIVE_CALL_STATUSES` in shared): a yes calls `POST /session/call` (core, owner only, limit `checkout`, one open ask per person, `GET /session/call` reads it) after the plan is saved, which keeps the ask and, while payments are not live (`PAYMENTS_LIVE` false, or no provider keys), tells every admin's inbox once and answers plainly that nothing was charged and the team will write to set a time; once payments are live it makes one provider order through payments.ts, the browser opens Razorpay Checkout (`lib/razorpay.ts`, shared with the product page) and the call becomes `paid` only in the webhook (`handleCallPayment`, guarded on `paying`), which then tells the admins and the person. The chat says the outcome as a line and goes straight on to the pillars (no sheet, no link out); the contract on the life map shows where the ask stands or an outline "Ask for a live call". Never read by a coach; in the export; an unpaid ask goes with the account and a paid one stays for the books without who it was for; an erase leaves it. Skipping it changes nothing. The site names its price from `LIVE_CALL_CENTS` as the third thing that costs money (the catch question, the home's offer, `/pricing`'s fine print), not on sale while `PAYMENTS_LIVE` is off.
- Chats are kept while you move around the app: the setup chat, the strategy session and the assistant keep their lines, step, answers and open panel in `lib/kept.ts` (`useKept`, `useKeptRef`, scoped per person), never in component state, so leaving and coming back lands on the same line and a line still being typed arrives while you are away. Its setters are the same on every mount, so a card made before you left still answers. It lives for the tab; a reload resumes from the server. Signing out (any `setToken(null)`) forgets every kept chat. Each chat has a `components/StartOver.tsx` button in its header that starts it fresh on a second tap ("Tap again to confirm"), and saved answers stay saved; the assistant's Close keeps it and folds it into its button, and "Run the session again" links to `/session?fresh=1`. Neither chat has a dock any more: see `components/Chat.tsx` above for how the newest question is kept high on the screen. `node scripts/smoke/drive-keep.mjs` checks the keeping with in-app navigation.
- Bumling (`BUMLING`; a chat speaker is a `Host`, one of the seven or Bumling) is the app's own guide, drawn by `Avatar` as a neutral grey friendly robot with a gold antenna, so it never reads as one of the seven. The characters are final (the 2026-11-03 look): friendly flat monsters with a gradient body, one clear mark each, big eyes and an open mouth, the seven in the rainbow of their pillars. It speaks wherever no one of the seven owns the subject: the setup chat, most of the strategy session, and the assistant (its flows on Home, Daily, Pillars and Reviews, and everything typed to it). A character speaks only where its relationship is the subject (Ember for money, Dew for the journal and moving, Lumen for practice and mindful minutes, each pillar for its own questions). A character's name is never alone where it speaks or is named: each of the seven has a `role` in `RELATIONSHIPS` (Ember, guide to servitude; Pip, guide to friends; Sunny, guide to family; Clover, guide to love; Dew, guide to yourself; Sage, guide to teachers; Lumen, guide to faith). `characterTitle` gives "Dew, guide to yourself" for labels and headers, `characterHello` gives "Dew here, your guide to yourself." for a chat introduction; a host that already spoke may say just its name ("Sunny again").
- The assistant (the button is `components/AssistantButton.tsx`, mounted once by `Shell` and in the first download; its panel, `AssistantPanel` in `components/Assistant.tsx`, downloads the first time a pointer or the keyboard reaches the button or a kept chat reopens it; module `assistant`: core, locked on, its page `/plans`) is a round button with Bumling in it at the bottom right of every screen inside the app (above the tab bar on a phone, `md:bottom-md` on a laptop; hidden on the Companion, and when the team switches the module off). Bumling bobs and blinks, gives a small hop and wiggle every few seconds, and jumps with excited eyes when a mouse points at the button or the keyboard reaches it, while a "Chat with AI assistant" bubble rises above it, only the first three times for a person on a device (2026-11-13, the founder: `kreators.assistantTip:<user>` in localStorage, read in try/catch so a private window always shows it; opening the panel ends a showing and the focus handed back as it folds neither shows nor counts one; after that he still jumps, the button wears `data-tip-spent`, and its name and description stay); on a touch screen the helper text never shows by itself (space is tight on a phone): the jump and the bubble belong to a mouse pointing at the button or the keyboard reaching it (GSAP; still under reduced motion, and the bubble just appears). It opens a panel headed "Bumling AI assistant" over the page (the name and its line wrap rather than being cut short, and the model picker takes a line of its own when a phone is too narrow for it beside its label): the width less the gutters and at least half the screen tall on a phone, 400px wide and up to 640px tall on a laptop, so the page under it stays in view and refreshes as things land. It replaced the quick chat. Its free half is the quick chat's flows, unchanged (`FLOWS`, each saying where its answer shows up, hidden when its app is off, writing through the same `api` calls the screens use; the screen under the panel picks the first few, `FIRST`). Its paid half is the typing box: with a plan a line goes to `POST /assistant`, where a model does it in the app as the person and answers with what it changed. One action, two callers: every tool in `apps/api/src/assistant/tools.ts` is a description over an existing route, run inside the Worker by `dispatch.ts` with the person's own Bearer token (`app.fetch`), so the session, the module switches, the rate limits, the waits, the xp caps and the owner filters apply exactly as to a tap. Never write a second version of an action for the assistant; give the route a tool. The user comes from the token, never from the model; every id a model gives passes `seg` (no slash, no dot) and a path the URL parser had to tidy is refused. The person's date and zone come from the device, never the model. A tool is `read`, `write` (logged in `assistant_actions` with the reverse route for Undo) or `confirm` (removing from a pyramid or the not to do list, settling a due: a card waits for Confirm, an hour at most), and is offered only when its app is on. Each change comes back as a row with Undo (`Receipts`), and the client clears the query keys the tool names (`refresh`). `assistant_actions` keeps tool names, ids and plain labels, never journal text or a log's note (a review that replaced an older one has no Undo, so the older words never sit there); it is in the export and goes with erase and delete. The model is provider neutral (`assistant/model.ts`): any OpenAI-compatible endpoint from `ASSISTANT_BASE_URL` (OpenRouter by default) and `ASSISTANT_API_KEY`. For now the panel has a model picker under its header whenever a model is connected, plan or not (kept in the browser, `useAssistantModel`), over `ASSISTANT_MODELS` in shared, OpenRouter's free models that take tools; the server takes a pick only from that list and otherwise uses `ASSISTANT_MODEL` (the first of the list unless set). A free model costs nothing (`priceOf`), so it spends no credits; the plan is still needed. Privacy follows the model: one with a zero-retention host (`private`, Xing4.0 among the free ones) is held to hosts that keep nothing and collect nothing, and only then are the journal and tracker reads (`sensitive` tools) offered; a model that keeps data is sent without that ask (asking would find no host) and never offered those reads, and the panel says so under the picker. A model off the list is asked for zero retention unless `ASSISTANT_ZDR=off`. Every model is asked not to reason (`reasoning: { effort: 'none' }`, since reasoning shares `max_tokens` with the answer and can leave it empty) and is sent no temperature; a model whose reasoning cannot be switched off (`thinks`, Flint and LFM 2.5) gets `MAX_OUT_THINKING` instead. A reply cut off before it said anything, or stopped on an error, is a `ModelError`; a model failure before any tool ran answers 502 and the line is not kept for the model. Each model reply is drawn as small markdown with "Answered by" and the model's name, so it is never mistaken for a built-in line, and a $0 message still writes a usage row with its tokens and model. Arguments are read kindly (`checkArgs`: case and spacing in a choice, a pillar by its name, "true" as text, one string for a list, a code fence around the JSON, ₹ and grouping commas in an amount); a tool result too long to read is replaced by a note, never cut mid-JSON, and every tool call past eight in one turn is answered with a note to call it again. The model is told to finish a job in one go (look it up, then do it, without asking leave), and when it still ends a turn asking leave before changing anything (`asksLeave`: "Do you want me to…?", never a question that offers a choice) the server answers "Yes, go ahead" for the person once and goes on; removing and settling still wait for Confirm. Stop: while a message runs the send arrow is a Stop square (`ChatComposer` `onStop`); the browser names each message (`runId`), `POST /assistant/stop` marks it in `assistant_runs` (migration 0032, rows live only while a message runs), and the loop looks before each model call and before running any tool, so nothing changes after the mark is seen. The request is never cut off (a dropped request could end the Worker before its Undo rows and its charge), so the reply still lists what was done before the stop, each with Undo. The model reads the journal only when the person asks about it. The chat is kept for the tab (scope `assistant:<user>`, its open state kept apart so Start over leaves it open); Close folds it into the button and keeps the chat. Without a plan a typed question about a word still gets `explainTyped`, and anything else gets the plan card. `node scripts/smoke/drive-talk.mjs` (`TALK_FROM`, `TALK_TAPS` as button text, `fill:<label>=<value>`, `submit`, `type:<text>`, `TALK_WIDE=1`) opens it on a screen and reports where the panel sits against the tab bar. `scripts/assistant/eval.mjs` sends thirty-odd real messages to a real model and checks the tool calls each one led to (`SMOKE_MODEL=real SMOKE_RUN=eval ASSISTANT_API_KEY=... bash scripts/smoke/blueprint.sh`, `ASSISTANT_MODEL` to compare), so a model is swapped on numbers, not by feel. The same tool list is what an MCP server would serve later. `read_table` (2026-11-14) reads a whole table only when the person switched on Show to AI chat for it (see All tables).
- Credits and plans: typing to the assistant (and later every agent that moves onto a model) is paid in credits, one credit a tenth of a US cent of model use, worked out from the tokens with whole numbers only (`creditsFor`, prices in micro-dollars per million tokens in `MODEL_PRICES` or `ASSISTANT_PRICE_IN` and `ASSISTANT_PRICE_OUT`; a model with no price is charged `FALLBACK_PRICE`, high on purpose). The only three plans are `PLANS`, priced and charged in Indian rupees (`PLAN_CURRENCY` INR: Pro ₹1,499 for 10,000 credits, Pro Max ₹12,499 for 100,000, Ultra ₹49,999 for 400,000, each for `PLAN_DAYS`; plan credits do not carry over; a credit is still counted from the model's cost in micro-dollars, which is cost, not price), top-ups are `TOPUP_PACKS` (₹899, ₹2,299 and ₹4,499; extra credits that stay until used and are spent after the plan's), and each plan's limits per agent are `AGENT_LIMITS` (tokens read per message, credits per message, credits per 24 hours, steps per message), all in shared. `apps/api/src/credits.ts` is the one ledger: `credit_accounts` (the plan, its period, the plan and extra balances) and `credit_ledger` (every grant, every use with its tokens, every expiry), migration 0031; a plan whose time is up closes the next time the account is read. `POST /assistant` refuses with a `paywall` (402 `plan` or `credits`, 429 `day`), stops a message before a model call that could pass what it may spend, and takes the cost once at the end, plan credits first, never below zero. A plan wears the Pro mark (`is_pro` and the `pro` honor follow it). Until payments are live (`PAYMENTS_LIVE`), plans and top-ups are shown and picked on `/plans` (`screens/Plans.tsx`, linked from Profile) but `POST /credits/checkout` answers that nothing was charged; only an admin starts a plan (`POST /admin/users/:id/plan`) or adds credits (`POST /admin/users/:id/credits`, positive, up to `MAX_CREDIT_GRANT`), from Admin, People, and every change is audited; the old Pro toggle starts or ends a plan. The free flows never spend credits. The feelings reader (the strategy session's map card): "Read my words" is `readFeelings` in shared, rules only, free, on the page; "Read with AI" shows only with a plan and goes to `POST /assistant/feelings` (`routes/feelings.ts`, gated by the assistant's switch, limit `feelings`), which takes only a `private` model from `ASSISTANT_MODELS`, spends through the same ledger under `AGENT_LIMITS[plan].feelings`, sends the words once and never keeps or logs them (the ledger keeps tokens and the model), and answers level numbers (`feelingsFromModel`) that the person still ticks. The Companion is the third agent (2026-11-04; `AGENTS` `companion` and `AGENT_LIMITS[plan].companion` in shared, `routes/companion.ts`, the loop shared with the assistant in `assistant/run.ts`, and the panel's `ModelPicker` and `ModelReply` on its screen): with a plan, credits and room in the day's cap it answers through the same model layer and ledger with the model picked, Stop through `assistant_runs`, reading only through `COMPANION_TOOLS` (the day, the routine, reminders and the week plan, and the trackers, focus and the journal only for a model that keeps nothing, and since 2026-11-14 `read_table` for the tables switched on with Show to AI chat, the journal, gratitude and body and mind ones only for such a model; any other name, a write included, is answered and never run, so it has no Undo rows); without a plan, with no credits or the cap used, or when the model fails before it says anything, the built-in companion answers free (Workers AI on our own host, else the rules), never a paywall. Crisis words are checked before any model call or ledger row and again on a reply; mood, sleep and the wellbeing score reach only a model that keeps nothing; each line says who answered (`chat_messages.model`, migration 0079: empty for the person, else a model id, `workers-ai`, `local` or `crisis`), drawn as "Answered by"; limit `companion` (60 an hour) on `POST /companion` and `/companion/stop`.
- The daily reminder is sent by the cron: `sendReminders` in cron.ts reads every `reminder_time`, finds the person's local day in their zone, skips anyone who checked in that day, and within sixty minutes after the due instant writes one `reminder` notification linking to Daily, never twice for the same due time. Inbox sets and clears the time (`PATCH /me` with `reminderTime`); the contract check-in time sets the same field.
- Social: the Community feed sorts by `FEED_SORTS` (recent, popular by reactions, most replies) and filters by `FEED_FILTERS` (everyone, following, mine), as the Show and Sort `FilterSelect`s beside Topic; a person can be followed from a post (`POST /community/follow/:userId`, the same `follows` table as the public page). Every social action pays through `rewardSocial` in apps/api/src/rewards.ts with the amounts and daily caps in `SOCIAL_REWARDS` (a post 5 xp up to three a day, a reply 2 xp up to five, a like you give 1 xp up to ten; a like on your post pays the author a point, a new follower two, up to twenty a day), each once per thing (`source_id`/`ref`), so unliking and liking again never pays twice. Trust: `trustScore` in shared is the one formula (xp, points earned, best streak, time here, sales with rating, honors; weighted, 0 to 100, four labels), computed server-side by `trustForMany` in one query and shown with `components/TrustBadge.tsx` on the public page header, the product page and every pre-owned listing; nothing in it can be typed in.
- Do not build the white-label app, instalments or native chat transport (see docs/DECISIONS.md). The Store's chat and offers are polled tables, not a transport.
- Discount codes (migration 0061, `discounts.ts`, `routes/discounts.ts`, `routes/adminDiscounts.ts`, `DiscountField`, `AdminDiscounts`; the decision is in docs/DECISIONS.md): the team makes them under Admin, Discount codes (`/admin/discounts`, needs the finance permission, audited as `discount.create`, `discount.update`, `discount.off`; a code's word, kind and worth never change, a new deal is a new code). A code takes a percent (`percent_bps`, `discountCents` and `percentOff` in shared round the amount off down) or a fixed amount (money only, `typedCents`) off the `DISCOUNT_AREAS`: product, program, event, plan, topup, optionally one item, with a smallest price, `max_uses`, a per-person limit and dates (starts inclusive, ends exclusive). `discountCodeKey` makes the stored word; `discountProblem` is the one reason in plain words. The price is always worked out on the server from the item's own price (`listPriceFor`, `quoteCode`), never from the request; `POST /discounts/check` only quotes and uses nothing, limited per person and per address (`discount`, `discountAddress`, `by: 'address'` in `ROUTE_LIMITS`). Store checkout, School enrolment and Party RSVP take `discountCode` and call `redeem` (guarded `used_count` and a unique redemption per code and order) before any points move, and `release` on failure; `payment.failed` and the cron's expiry give the use back, a refund or a cancel does not. The platform pays: `orders` keep `list_cents`, `list_points`, `discount_cents`, `discount_points`; the seller is credited and refunded on the list price, the buyer gets back what they paid, a Party ticket keeps `paid_points`; a 100% money order is paid at once with no provider call; a creator coupon and a team code are never used together. Plans and top-ups are priced and checked in `POST /credits/checkout` but nothing is used up while `PAYMENTS_LIVE` is off. Redemptions are in the export and go with the account.
- Agency email notes and what an agreement prints (2026-11-02, 2026-11-07; `packages/shared/src/agencyEmail.ts`, its own export `@kreators/shared/agencyEmail` and chunk, `EmailNote` in `components/DocActions.tsx`, `coverFrom` and `logOtherPapers` in `agencyPaper.ts`, `DocPassword` in `AgencyLifecycle.tsx`): "Email a copy" on an invoice, agreement or document opens with a note written for the person to change (`EMAIL_TEMPLATES`: agreement and invoice to start, the same with a pricing P.S., this month's invoice, renewal, revised paper after a change, a reply about clauses and exclusivity, signed copy, content plan, before the next shoot, a friendly and a firm payment reminder, payment received; `fillEmail`, the order a client reads: what they can open, what must happen, the sign-off from Agency settings) and one textarea. A Worker makes no PDF, so the mail carries no file and no note says "attached" (a shared test insists): it names each paper with its own link, "You can open and download it here:". `GET /agency/clients/:id/papers` (owner only, no team rule, since a member is never given a client's link; a draft has no `path`, a void one is not listed) is what the notes read, through `emailVarsFor`: a first send and a renewal name the agreement and the invoice together, from whichever one the sheet was opened (`pair`, the one out with the client first); one with no link yet is left out, the note says it will follow in a separate email (`follows`) and the sheet says why with a way to it (`PaperGap`, `paperGapLine`); a client with a password gets one line saying the links ask for it, and a note never holds it. The revised note lists every agreement and invoice with a link and names the billing legal name; the clause reply fills its longer deal from a retainer on offer or running, else the client's monthly fee, and asks for the length when neither is on record; the P.S. is written from the profile's packages and the client's lower first invoice, with the saving per unit in whole numbers, and is left out with a line on the sheet when no plans are set. `suggestEmailTemplate` also offers the revised note for a paper changed after it was emailed and the P.S. when this send costs more than the first invoice. Nothing in the file holds a real name, number or price; a shared test reads every note with nothing on record and finds no digit, currency or proper name, and scores the new wording at Flesch 60 or above (his own first-send sentences stay as he wrote them). A kept note loses the client's links wherever they sit, the invoice's number and amount and the sums it was filled with (`withoutPaperLinks`). One month is no longer deal, so the note names the fee and asks for the length. The three email routes send that note and the client's link when `message` and `subject` come with the request (`coverFrom`: plain text, held to `EMAIL_NOTE_MAX`, a one-line subject), and the old document text otherwise; the server adds the paper's link and "Questions? Write to" only when the note does not hold them, and every other paper of the client whose link is in the words gets the same line in its own log (`logOtherPapers`, by real token, never the words). A team member's note names only the paper it was opened from, and the server adds that link. Each document's own page carries the client's password state and the same controls as the client's page (`DocPassword`; the password is the client's, one lock for their page and every document). An agreement section can be left off the printed page (`ContractSection.print`, `sectionPrints`: a brief is never printed unless turned on; in the Trial template "What happens after the trial" and "How we work" are information and start off); it stays on the screen and at the client's link, keeps its number so "section 6" in the text still points at the right heading, and one line under the printed text says what was left out. An invoice prints with its own colours and sizes on white (`print-color-adjust: exact`); agreements and other documents print plain black on white.

## Commands
- pnpm dev (web on 5173, api on 8787)
- pnpm typecheck
- pnpm --filter @kreators/shared test
- pnpm build
- pnpm db:migrate:local
- bash scripts/scan.sh for the spacing and colour scans (numeric Tailwind spacing, arbitrary px, hex colours outside the drawn art, white on the ink fill, the old product name)
- Cloudflare from the root: pnpm cf:login, pnpm cf:whoami, pnpm db:create, pnpm db:migrate, pnpm cf:secret <NAME>, pnpm deploy, pnpm cf:logs (the cf: prefix dodges pnpm's own login, whoami and logout commands)
- bash scripts/smoke/blueprint.sh for the end-to-end smoke test (2607 checks on 2026-11-08, passing on a fresh database and on a kept one, and again on the kept one twice on 2026-11-09; 2686 after wave 24's Connect people and Dev Tools sections, 2026-11-09, on the kept one; 2765 after wave 25's Invites and Invite and connection rewards sections, 2026-11-10, on the kept one; 2836 after wave 26's "Connect people for staff, brand teams and cohort managers", 2026-11-10, on the kept one; 2921 after wave 27's "Connect people by rank, place and pay" and its break-it checks, 2026-11-10, on the kept one, where a run now puts every account it made staff or an admin back to a user at its end, so a kept database never gathers staff from run to run, in `scripts/smoke/smoke.mjs`, which imports the shared model straight from TypeScript). smoke.mjs serves a stand-in model on 8799 and blueprint.sh points the server at it (`ASSISTANT_BASE_URL`), so the assistant checks never pay for a model; a server that was already running without it skips those checks and says so. It runs against a second API on port 8788 whose database lives in `scripts/smoke/state` (`wrangler dev --port 8788 --persist-to scripts/smoke/state --test-scheduled`, migrated with `wrangler d1 migrations apply kreators-life --local --persist-to <that dir>`), so the dev database on 8787 never fills with test accounts; `--keep` leaves that server up, `--fresh` wipes its database first. The checks send their own `CF-Connecting-IP` per run so the sign-up and sign-in limits never trip a rerun; the founder account is `founder@smoke.test` with a fixed password, checked for the admin promotion and then left alone: the rest of a run acts through its own admin (`admin-<stamp>@smoke.test`, made a user again at the end), so a rerun within the hour never spends the hourly limits (create 30, post 10), and checks find this run's rows by id, since the database keeps earlier runs' (2026-11-07). Screenshots (`node scripts/smoke/shoot.mjs [routes]`, puppeteer-core driving the installed Chrome) use the same server unless SHOOT_ORIGIN says otherwise; it clears the service worker first so every shot sees the latest build, SHOOT_MOTION=1 keeps animation on, SHOOT_THEME picks dark, light or both, and a shot can set `width`/`height` (a phone on its side is `desktop: true, width: 844, height: 390`). `scripts/smoke/contrast-probe.js` is its `SHOOT_PROBE` step: it walks every text node, composites its colour onto the real background, and lists anything under 2.2:1. Run it over every route in dark mode after touching colours; the only accepted hits are white captions on the green fill and greyed unearned badges. `scripts/smoke/overflow-probe.js` is the other probe: it reports a page that scrolls sideways and any element that pokes past the screen (unless an ancestor scrolls or clips it). `node --experimental-strip-types scripts/smoke/shots-all.mjs > shots.json` prepares an account with real data behind every detail page and writes the shot list for about 120 routes, the newer pages among them on real data: a logged Fitness day, gratitude, a daily review, a coach with a student, a lesson with three resources (`SHOOT_TOKEN` reuses that account, `viewport: true` on a shot takes the first screen only, `SHOOT_NO_PNG=1` takes each shot and writes no file, for a probe run); run the overflow probe over it at phone width after touching layout, and it must come back with 0 hits. The other probes for the same list: `lone-probe.js` (a pill alone on its line, 0), `target-probe.js` (a tap target under 44 by 44; an absolute `::before` or `::after` counts as the hit area, so `A hit` in legalBits.tsx grows a lone policy link without changing it at rest, and the paper's own inline edit text inside `.legal-doc`, sr-only links until focused and inline glossary words are left out like running text; 0 on all 123 routes at 360, 390, 768 and 1280 in both themes, 2026-11-07), `density-probe.js` (copy too heavy for a phone, per docs/copy-brief.md), `box-probe.js` (a link, button, `role=button`, `.toy` or summary that draws a shadow, fill or edge with square corners, outside paper and tables; `toy` belongs on rounded controls only, never on a text link; 0 with no sheet open, since an open sheet's full-screen scrim counts), `cut-probe.js` (a one-line row of tabs or pills that scrolls sideways or cuts a pill at its edge, which should be a `TabRow`; a TabRow's sideways line passes only while it and its pinned All end inside the edge; rows of cards are left alone; run at 360 and 390, 0), `height-probe.js`, `orphan-probe.js` and `rag-probe.js` (all above), and `spacing-probe.js` (2026-11-08, the founder: "check redundant spacing like this and improve all over the app", after Plan my week's hours line sat on its ruler's numbers): it measures what is drawn, not boxes (words by their ink from the font's own metrics, an svg by what it draws, a box by its edge) and reports, with the route, a short selector and the nearest heading, `touching` (words over a control, a box or a picture, or a slider's own numbers, under 6px of ink; two plain lines of words, or words over a tag or a dot, under 2px, since a title over its note is set tight on purpose), `doubled` (two of a flex stack's gap and two margins each 16 or more; a padded card holding a wrapper that pads again, or whose first or last child adds a 12px margin; an empty element holding a second gap; one gap in a list of one kind over 1.6 times the common one), `uneven` (a gap in a list of one kind more than 4px off the one most of them share, unless a heading starts a group; or a list whose boxes agree while one draws 10px and 30% more empty room) and `empty` (an in-flow element over 24 by 24 with nothing drawn, outside decoration, a height set inline, a grid's blank cell and a busy region; or a childless first or last element whose margin still adds room). It leaves out inline runs, a small control's insides, tables, chart SVG and paper; `data-spacing-root` limits it to one element, and `drive-popups.mjs` runs it inside each open popup with `POPUP_PROBE` (and `POPUP_HITS` for JSON). An empty `ul` or `ol` takes no room (`display: none` in app.css unless it is `aria-live`). The rule is 0 hits at 390 and 1280 and in the popups (the gates of 2026-11-08 and 2026-11-09: one a run at 360, 390, 768 and 1280 in both themes, /features' closing band 120px under Books, since moved to the page's 64px (the founder, 2026-11-09), and 0 in the popups, 89 and then 87 drawn; the last gate, 2026-11-09: 0 in all eight runs and 0 in 89 popups; after wave 24, 0 again and 0 in 109 popups, the five admin sheets among them; after wave 25, 2026-11-10, 0 again in all eight runs and 0 in 109 popups; after wave 26, 0 again in all eight runs and 0 in 108 popups, the card table's Read all drawn only when a dealt card is cut; after wave 27, 0 again in all eight runs and 0 in 109 popups); the first full run and what is left are in docs/DECISIONS.md. `contrast-probe.js` still misreads the sidebar's active tab (it does not see the sliding pill) and the white labels inside the life map's funnel (SVG fills); both were checked on screen. It leaves out words only a screen reader gets, since a 1px clipped box draws nothing. `node scripts/smoke/drive-signup.mjs` fills and submits the real sign-up form in Chrome against that server and prints the URLs it passed through; a new account must land on `/onboarding`. `DRIVE_START=welcome` takes the front door instead (Welcome, the device session, the account card inside the setup chat), `DRIVE_TAPS` taps chips, fills cards (`fill:<option>`) and types (`type:<text>`) in turn, `DRIVE_MOTION=1` keeps the typing pace, and the report gives the header, the newest turn, the answer and the typing box positions and says whether anything inside the page scrolls on its own; it sends its own `CF-Connecting-IP` so repeated drives never trip the sign-up limit. Tailwind 4 orders `inline-flex` and `inline-block` after `hidden`, so never put one of those on the same element as `hidden sm:…`; give the element the display class only in the variant.

## Done means
Typecheck and build pass, shared tests pass, the smoke test passes, the spacing and colour scans are clean, and docs/DECISIONS.md has a line if the plan changed.
